# GuestFlow 4.0.0 — full changelog detail

This file is the complete, unabridged detail of release 4.0.0: every `changelog.d/` fragment merged for it, grouped by category exactly as `node scripts/build-changelog.mjs` folds them. The `CHANGELOG.md` section of 4.0.0 is a condensed digest of this content, because GitHub refuses release bodies above 125,000 characters. The fixes made after the shadow trial of 4.0.0 appear in that section only.

## Added
- Test suite: a census of the mounted Express routers fails on a write route with neither an input-shape baseline nor a listed exemption.
- **Duplicates set aside** (spec `calendar-property-parity.md` rules 22-24). A stay that the old « platform disabled » filter hid and that sits on a visible stay of the same logement, with no money in, is set aside as a duplicate (`kind = 'superseded'`): out of every calendar, list, availability check and the books, still found by the search (« Doublon écarté »). Its fiche is read-only and an admin can « Rétablir » it. The iCal sync never resurrects it.
- **Plans and subscriptions, inside each instance** (specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md, C1): an instance hosted on the GuestFlow platform reads a signed licence from its data directory. A plugin outside the customer's plan shows its plan (« Forfait Pro ») and cannot be installed; one installed before a downgrade reads « Désactivé — hors forfait » and keeps its data. Units and accounts beyond the plan's quota are refused. An unpaid subscription shows an admin banner, then turns the instance read-only: reads, exports, the calendar sync and collecting payments keep working, and the website stops taking new bookings. The login page fills in the email passed by the shared login page (`?login_hint=`).
- An install that is not hosted on the platform and has no licence file (Solio today, dev machines) is unaffected: every plugin, no quota, no banner.
- **Control plane — the operator console (C2a)** (`specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md`). A new
  app in `control-plane/`, deployed separately from GuestFlow and inert for self-hosted installs:
  the plan catalogue (Essentiel / Pro / Premium, nested, versioned, with the impact of a change
  shown before saving), the customers with their creation steps, the fleet and its alerts, the
  signed licence written to each instance, the subscription lifecycle recomputed daily (a payment
  recorded by hand, extensions and « Remettre en actif » with a reason), and deprovisioning with a
  full export. Operators log in with a password and a second factor of their choice: an
  authenticator app or a code by email, with backup codes. The Qonto invoices and reminders come
  with C2b.
- **Instance: `server/scripts/create-first-admin.js`**, which the console runs to create a hosted
  customer's first administrator (and close the unused bootstrap account).
- **Console (control plane), billing (C2b):** the renewal invoice is created in Qonto (ADN Dev) 7 days before the end of a monthly subscription, 30 days for a yearly one, with its payment link, which the instance's « Renouveler » opens. The invoice, J-7, J and J+7 emails are templates the operator edits; each is Manuel (approved from the home page) or Automatique, all shipped Manuel. « Relancer maintenant », « Vérifier le paiement », and payment detection every 15 minutes and through the webhook renew the subscription once. The operator gets a daily email when something needs a look. Customers gain a billing address.
- **Console:** Qonto is set up from GuestFlow's own « Paiements en ligne » page, reused whole; GuestFlow's Qonto settings handlers now come from a factory both apps mount.
- **Console (control plane), shared login (C3):** `app.<domain>` asks for an email only and sends the user to their space's own login page with the email filled in; several spaces give a list, none a neutral answer; the last space is remembered for 90 days; 10 lookups per minute per IP. The page is rendered by the server, with no script and no password field.
- **Console:** the directory is read every minute from each instance's active accounts, keeping only an HMAC of each email. « Changer l'adresse » renames a customer after a five-item checklist; the old address stays reserved and redirected for 12 months.
- **Pay a direct reservation in several times** (spec `direct-installments.md`, 2026-10-27). The fiche of a direct reservation (own channels, `isDirectChannel`) gets an « Échéancier »: 2 to 12 installments, the first on the day it is created, the last at the usual solde date, the others evenly spaced; every date is editable while unpaid and every amount but the last unpaid one can be typed. The plan replaces the acompte and the solde (the arrival complement and the caution are untouched). Euros are whole, the last installment carries the cents. Before the first payment every change of the total recomputes the amounts; after it they are frozen — a rise is spread over the unpaid installments, a drop changes nothing and shows « Échéancier au-dessus du séjour ». Each installment is requested with its own Qonto link at its exact amount, through the new manual email « Demande de règlement d'une échéance » (FR + EN): one « à valider » entry per installment from its due date, or « Envoyer la demande » from the fiche. A paid link marks its installment (webhook and polling); a payment received another way is marked from the fiche with its date and mode. An installment unpaid three days after its due date reads « En retard » and joins the dashboard's payment-deadline card. A link replaced because its amount changed is now deactivated at the provider before the new one is minted. Each paid installment is its own accounting encaissement, its tourist tax shared in proportion. Unpaid installments are due at arrival (the SAS settles them), a plan fully collected attaches the stay to its last installment's day, the bank reconciliation expects and records each installment, and the stay's open acompte / solde links are retired when a plan is created. A request email that asks for money can no longer be set to « auto ». No automatic reminder and no cancellation for non-payment.
- **Guest email texts on one page, in send order** (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md): Paramètres › « Textes des emails » groups, email by email (confirmation, deposit/balance, J-7, J-2, day after, November/January, cancellation), the subject, the sentences, the mentions and the property hooks. Variables are inserted with chips and shown by their French label. The full email is rendered by the server beside the text while typing, in French or English, on the next stay or a sample one, with « M'envoyer un test ». An « À compléter » list says what would render badly, and the dashboard shows « Emails incomplets » before an arrival.
- **Arrival facts per property**: address, map link, how to get in, key-box code and Wi-Fi (code and password encrypted) compose the J-2 of a new instance.
- **One file for every text**: CSV export, and an import that shows its changes before saving and refuses a bad row with its line number; « Traduire les textes manquants » when the platform has a translation key (`GUESTFLOW_TRANSLATE_API_KEY`).
- **Start assistant**: a step « Vos emails » before « C'est prêt ».
- **Google Vacation Rentals feed** (spec `google-vacation-rentals-feed.md`, 2026-10-26). New plugin `google-vacation-rentals`: for every enabled property it computes, with the website's own quote, the tax-included total of every stay of the horizon (arrival × length up to 14 nights × party size), each night's availability, minimum/maximum stay, changeover days and closures, and pushes the difference to Google's ARI endpoints. Simulation by default (the XML is written on the server, nothing is sent) until Google opens a partner account. Triggers: a reservation or iCal import (within a minute), a saved tariff (debounced 30 s), a nightly full re-send; a « Vérifier les prix » check compares what was sent with the website's quote. Settings tab under Intégrations. Core: the generic `pricing.changed` plugin event emitted by every pricing, option, option-default, closure and property write that can move a price; plugin SDK 2.4.0 (`ctx.events.emit`, `coreModule('directQuote')`, the website quote moved to `utils/directQuote.js` unchanged). WordPress plugin 1.17.0: the booking block opens with the `checkin`, `checkout` and `guests` of its address. +66 server tests, +6 client tests.
- plugin:google-vacation-rentals First version: stay prices, availability and restrictions sent to Google, in dry-run by default.
- plugin:tariff-recipes Announces every recipe application and every calendar extension.
- plugin:website-booking The website quote is computed by GuestFlow's core.
- **Application clients in GuestFlow** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 9bis, 22, 37bis-quater, 43, 45). The option and resource editors gain an « Application clients » section (sold in the app, photo, descriptions, preparation, maximum per order, and the stock: follow, count, « Prévenir sous », « En rupture »); « Stock : épuisé » under a fiche's line; the page « Commandes clients » (admin) and its cards on the planning; the fiche's « Lien Mon séjour » card; « Ouvrir le Studio » in Paramètres › Intégrations.
- **Client extension points for plugins** (spec `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md` rule 49, SDK 2.26.0): `options.fields`, a `section` of its own for a `*.fields` contribution, `nav.items` in the side menu, `api.pluginRequest`, and `/login?next=` (same-origin paths only).
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », slice E (server)** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 1, 11ter, 15bis, 16-21, 47-49). One booklet per lodging: pages placed in each lodging's booklet in its own order (published or not there), shared between lodgings and edited once, blocks restricted to some lodgings, « Dupliquer vers… » for an independent copy; « Publier » freezes one lodging's booklet and republishes a shared page in every lodging it is placed in. Guests read the last published version (`GET /public/v1/guest/booklet`, `/booklet/pages/:slug`, `/booklet/search`, by session or by the printed code), in their language with the French as fallback per field; the `wifi` and `contact` blocks read the lodging's own facts (« Pas de wifi » when it has none), and a button to an item the session cannot buy is not sent. The studio API (`/api/guest-app/studio/**`) edits pages and the closed list of eleven block types, rich text sanitised on the server, previews and searches the draft (accents ignored), counts the untranslated texts, and uploads photos (JPG, PNG, WEBP up to 10 Mo, stored at 1600 / 800 / 400 px in WebP under `/uploads/guest-app/`). `POST /api/guest-app/lodgings/:propertyId/regenerate-qr` kills a lodging's old code at once, `GET …/qr-poster.pdf` prints the A4 poster. A lodging entering the app for the first time gets five neutral placeholder pages. `/me` gains `suggestion`: the one dated Soliomap event of the days left (read from Soliomap's feed, cached an hour, nothing when Soliomap does not answer within 5 s). SDK 2.26.0 also exposes `safeFeedFetch` (the public-only outbound fetch of the iCal import) and `instancePaths.uploadsDir`.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », slice D (server)** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 24-38, 41-45). The guest orders from the stay's shop: `POST /public/v1/guest/orders/quote` prices the cart with the fiche's own engine (the same save played without writing, so free units, free minutes, the stay's price lock and per-lodging prices apply as on the fiche), each hourly session re-checked against the resource planning and the other sessions of the order, the breakfast's mornings and hour, the delivery time of the staff-prepared items and the stay's 0 € allowance. `POST /public/v1/guest/orders` takes it « sur la note » or at 0 € (confirmed and written on the fiche at once as mid-stay extras, in one transaction with the order, history « Commande client #N »), or by card: a 30-minute `complement` payment link for exactly the order's basket (VAT lines at the stay's rate, back to `/commande/<id>` on the guest host), the bath slots held meanwhile; paid, the order is committed and its money recorded as a settled mid-stay note carrying the provider's reference, so the end-of-stay complement owes nothing for it. Unpaid after 30 minutes it expires; the guest can abandon it; a payment that lands late still commits it when its slots are free, else the order is « Payée · à rembourser » and the fiche's ledger shows « Commande client à rembourser ». `GET /public/v1/guest/orders` and `/orders/:id` follow it (chips of rule 38, the provider read on demand). Staff side: the « Commandes clients » push on every confirmed or paid order, `GET /api/guest-app/orders` (« À préparer » / « Toutes »), `POST /api/guest-app/orders/:id/delivered`, and `GET /api/guest-app/planning/order-cards` for the planning's « Commande client » cards (admin and reception). An option of the planning other than the breakfast can no longer be flagged « Vendu dans l'app ». SDK 2.26.0 adds the generic `ctx.paymentBasket` (its owner's refund words, else « Paiement en ligne à rembourser »), `ctx.core.payments` and `coreModule('midStayOrderCommit')` (a plugin's order written as mid-stay extras, named and historised in the caller's words); the plugin limits its own orders (`GUEST_ORDER_RATELIMIT_*`, 10 orders / hour / session). +56 server tests.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », slice C (server)** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 28-31, 39-42). `GET /public/v1/guest/shop/resources/:id/slots` lists the bookable slots of an hourly resource (free or evening rate only, by day, priced per lodging), read from the resource planning by the scheduler `hourly-resources` declares: other stays' sessions, outside bookings, held orders, turnover, capacity, heat-up and retention, closed days; a slot that needs the resource lit from cold is offered only when its ignition falls in the service hours. The service hours are the plugin's (`GET/PUT /api/guest-app/service-hours`, shared or per lodging: up to three ranges a weekday, exceptions by date, the all-properties establishment closures applied); `POST /public/v1/guest/shop/delivery-slots` gives a cart's delivery times (30-min steps, the longest preparation lead inside one service range, check-in to check-out, « Dès que possible » while open), and the shop payload gains `service` (open now, next opening, « Le service reprend demain à 8 h — … ») and « Plus disponible pour votre séjour » on a staff-prepared item with no delivery time left. SDK 2.26.0 adds `ctx.resourceScheduler(provider)` with `ctx.core.resourceScheduler()`, and `ctx.resourceHolds(fn)` with `ctx.core.resourceHolds(range)`.
- plugin:hourly-resources Declares the resource scheduler of the guest app, and reads every holds source as occupancy: a guest order awaiting payment blocks its slot on Calendrier › Ressources, in the arrival SAS picker and for outside bookings. Asks for SDK 2.22.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », slice A** (spec `guest-app.md`). New plugin `guest-app`, off until installed: per-lodging configuration (modules, wifi, contacts), a QR code per lodging, stay sessions by QR or by name + reservation number, `GET /public/v1/guest/me` and `/lodging`, and the « Application clients » card of Paramètres › Intégrations. No guest screen yet. SDK 2.26.0 exposes `stayWindow` (moved out of `gate-access`, same computation) and `encryption`.
- **Application clients — « Mon séjour »** (spec `guest-app.md` §6, slice F). The guest app itself, in the plugin's own folder (`server/src/plugins/guest-app/apps/guest`): the
  home with the lodging's tiles, the highlight and one Soliomap idea; the welcome booklet with its search, readable
  offline; the shop with the bath's slots, the cart priced by GuestFlow, delivery or pickup time, « Ajouter à ma note »
  or « Payer maintenant »; « Mes commandes »; the « Retrouver mon séjour » fallback. Phone, tablet and computer, day and
  night, French and English, installable. Served by the plugin on the guest host.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », slice B (server)** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 22-27, 27bis). The sell switch of an option or a resource lives in the plugin (`GET /api/guest-app/items`, `GET/PUT /api/guest-app/items/:kind/:id`, a non-sellable price type refused with `NOT_SELLABLE`), and `GET /public/v1/guest/shop` serves the stay's lodging catalogue: per-lodging prices and labels, « Inclus », free units (own-channel stays) and free minutes left, « Déjà inclus dans votre séjour », the breakfast's mornings, categories, open exactly while the fiche takes mid-stay extras. The bath tile now follows the shop, and the gate tile follows the `gate-access` plugin. SDK 2.26.0 also exposes `publicCatalogProjection` (the option / resource projections, moved out of `website-booking`, which re-exports them unchanged), `midStayExtrasWindow`, and the pricing engine's `applyFreeUnitsToLine`, `getApplicableOptions`, `getApplicableResources`.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », one link per stay** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 1-9bis, 33; decision Q10 of 2026-11-06). Each reservation gets its own link `https://<guest host>/s/<token>` (128 random bits, stored encrypted with its hash), created the first time the arrival SAS, a reminder e-mail or the fiche needs it: pre-arrival from J-7, the whole stay inside the gate window, the lodging's visitor mode before and after. « Régénérer le lien » kills the old link and every session opened from it. The lodging poster now opens the visitor mode only, and no name is ever asked (the name confirmation is removed). Routes `GET /api/guest-app/reservations/:id/stay-link` and `POST …/regenerate` (admin and reception). The plugin declares the e-mail variable `{{stayAppUrl}}` « Lien Mon séjour » and the condition `hasStayApp` (J-7 and J-2 chips): the stay link, else the gate link; both are empty while the plugin is inactive, so a template keeps its gate paragraph under `hasGateAccess`. A plugin e-mail variable may now name the e-mails it belongs to (`in`). +11 server tests (`guest-app-stay-links`), sessions / current-stay / input-shape suites rewritten, +7 client tests.
- plugin:sas The arrival « Portail » step can be taken over by a `sas.portal` contribution marked `leadsStep` (the guest app's « Mon séjour »), the gate key's own step handed to it as its fallback.
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », stock, delivery or pickup, studio and gate (server)** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 12-14, 37bis-37quater, 41bis-41ter, 47, 48, 52, 53). Each option and each non-hourly resource may follow a stock for the whole estate (`GET/PUT /api/guest-app/items/:kind/:id`: « Suivre le stock », « En stock » with « Stock réglé à 24 — Sophie, 6 nov. 14:02 », « Prévenir sous », « En rupture »): every sale counts when the article leaves the cellar — an app order in its commit, a line of the website or of the staff on the stay's arrival day or when written later, a card order holding its units while it awaits its payment — and comes back when lowered or cancelled; staff and website sales are never refused (the stock may read « −2 »), the app shows « Plus que 2 » then « Épuisé » and answers `OUT_OF_STOCK`, the staff is pushed « Stock bas » then « … épuisé » once each until the next restock, and `GET /api/guest-app/stock` gives the reservation editor its « Stock : épuisé » warning. The orders are « Livrées au logement » or « À retirer » at a place typed once (`fulfilment` / `pickupPlace`): the picker's question, the guest's « Livrée à L'Estiva aujourd'hui vers 17 h 30 » or « Prête … Lieu de retrait : … », « il sera chaud » for the bath, and the staff's « à retirer » / « Retirée » follow. The studio lists every GuestFlow property live (`GET /api/guest-app/studio/lodgings`; the plugin no longer names a lodging), reads and writes the app settings in one place (`/api/guest-app/studio/settings`) and « Synchroniser » re-reads Soliomap. `GET /public/v1/guest/gate` and the gate tile give the stay's key link while Sowel has made it, « Accès en préparation » before. `/me` says `isNight` from sunset to sunrise; `GET /public/v1/guest/manifest.webmanifest` makes the app installable. The plugin serves the guest app on its own host and the studio at `/studio` (admin only) through the new SDK entry point `ctx.webApp` (SDK 2.26.0); the plugin package carries both builds. The tile « Soliomap » reads « Activités ». +39 server tests.
- **Application clients — Studio du livret** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 47-50, slice G). At `/studio` on the admin host, for the admin: a lodging switcher (« Hors application » greyed), per lodging Modules, Infos du logement, Livret (pages and a block editor, shared pages with blocks kept to one lodging) and Boutique (the lodging's own articles at its price, `GET /api/guest-app/items?propertyId=`); shared Service, Application and Photos. A phone preview draws the guest app's own components. Kept in the plugin's folder (`server/src/plugins/guest-app/apps/studio`), shipped in its package.
- plugin:accounting-export **Accounting synthesis — server and API** (spec `accounting-synthesis.md`, 2026-11-05). `GET /api/accounting/synthesis` answers, for a month, a calendar quarter, the fiscal year or a custom window (≤ 750 days, the Suivi financier's refusals), the tiles of the future « Synthèse » tab of « Comptabilité » with their last-year comparison (the dashboard's rule): revenue HT/TTC of the stays, collected and number of payments, tourist tax we remit (the « Taxe de séjour » extraction, month by month), VAT to declare by rate (or the franchise/exemption mention). Plus the VAT return by rate and by revenue account read from the very rows the CSV writes (CSV byte-identical, pinned by a golden file), the checks strip (balanced entries, VAT coherence entry by entry to the cent, finished stays not settled, last CSV export), collections by means (platform payout, payment link, marked perçu) and by settlement type with shares adding up to 100, and the collected series with last year's running total. Each panel names its source (stays vs collections). The CSV download now stamps the last export. SDK 2.22.0 exposes `financeModel`, `financeDashboardModel`, `financeWindow`, `fiscalYear`, `yearOverYear`; the dashboard's N-1 range is now one function (`lastYearRange`), its payload unchanged. Accountant role: read access. The « Comptabilité » page now opens on the « Synthèse » tab (tiles with last-year pills, the dashboard's own comparison notice, checks strip with the named entries and stays, VAT by rate or by account, cumulative chart) followed by « Encaissements » (share tables by means and type, aligned daily/weekly/monthly series) and « Écritures » (the journal, unchanged, with its CSV); the period bar offers month, quarter, exercise or custom. The client SDK exposes the report grammar components. Review of 2026-11-06: a cancellation that keeps collected money is neither a collection nor a refund; VAT coherence shows one cent per entry as a grey rounding gap; the VAT mention follows the regimes in force during the window; the tourist tax of a window ahead of the server's month says « Rien à déclarer avant le mois en cours. » and a straddling one says up to when, reading the shared extraction rows once per span; period options are only those the server accepts (an exercise far ahead answers at once — December 9999 no longer loops); breakdowns fold beyond five rows into « Autres »; the « Écritures » badge counts its own month; a refused first period offers « Revenir au mois en cours »; the CSV stamps « Dernier export CSV » only for a same-origin download; the client SDK loads the two report charts on first use, so Recharts leaves the initial bundle (412,8 → 298,1 kB gzip). +59 server tests, +35 client tests. Verification of 2026-11-06: euro axes read « 1,5 k€ » (never two equal labels), a window with no collection shows « Aucun encaissement sur la période. » instead of empty charts, the app's own download header stamps the last export on plain HTTP too (a cross-site request never does), a stray tap on the shown period kind keeps the period, « Mois à venir : rien à déclarer. », « Autres » lists what it holds, the « Écritures » badge shows no stale count. +63 server tests, +43 client tests in all. A query key of the synthesis sent twice is a 400 whatever the period kind.
- **Suivi financier — « À vérifier »**: a panel at the bottom of the page counts, over the exercise and the chosen logement, the finished stays whose tourist tax is missing while it is ours to remit (a tax already frozen is never judged), the past months whose tax is not fully ticked « Déclarée » (the remaining amount is the tax page's own figure, with the part not declarable yet named), the finished platform stays without « Paiement plateforme », and the finished stays still on an iCal placeholder client. Each row opens its stays (to their fiche) or months (to « Extraction taxe de séjour » on that month), 20 at most with the rest counted (specs/finance-data-checks.md).
- Report building blocks for the upcoming finance reports (specs/report-grammar.md): KPI tiles with a definition button, a secondary line and a year-over-year pill giving last year's value (« — vs N-1 » plus one notice when there is nothing to compare), titled report panels, tab counts, a line chart with series named at their end, a legend that switches series on and off and a « ⋯ » menu to read the chart as a table or copy its data, aligned daily and cumulative charts sharing one cursor, and a segmented share bar above its table.
- Report charts load with the first chart shown rather than with every page, keep their axis on round steps (0–100 % for a rate), say when a period is empty, and keep long series names and definition bubbles readable on a phone.
- Report charts keep their top graduation close to the highest value and their month labels evenly spaced; a definition bubble closes when the page scrolls; a failing accounting check is titled by what fails (« Écart de TVA », « Écritures déséquilibrées », « Séjours non soldés »).
- Chart axes hug a negative day as closely as the highest value, long series names stay whole on wide screens, and the Suivi financier curve spaces its months evenly at every width.
- Suivi financier (server): RevPAR of the window with its open nights (closures excluded, as occupancy already does), a year-over-year comparison of every headline figure — revenue, collected, stays, nights, occupancy (in points), revenue per night, RevPAR — with one reason and one notice when last year does not compare, the source each figure reads, and the monthly occupancy of all logements with last year beside it (`specs/finance-revpar-year-over-year.md`). Existing dashboard fields unchanged.
- Suivi financier: each tile and hero figure has its definition (« i », naming its source), « Encaissé », « Réservations », nights sold, occupancy (in points), revenue per night and the new RevPAR carry a year-over-year pill, one notice explains a missing or partial comparison, and « Taux d'occupation » draws all logements with last year dashed above the per-logement charts.
- Suivi financier → « Profil des séjours » (`/finance/profil`, after « Vue générale »): for the dashboard's window and logement, the non-cancelled stays split by channel (« Direct » and each platform), length, booking lead time (judged per logement's tracking start) and price band, in count and amount with shares adding up to 100 %, and the average basket per month of the exercise against last year on comparable months, without the tourist tax actually inside each stay's total; past five channels the tail is one « Autres » row; every panel names its source (specs/stay-profile.md). API: `GET /api/finance/stay-profile`.
- Tourist tax report over any period (specs/tourist-tax-report-v2.md): `GET /api/finance/tourist-tax/report` reads a month, a quarter, a year or a custom du / au (≤ 750 days), by logement and declaration status, with taxed and exempt persons and person-nights, the stays a platform remits itself counted apart, what is declared and what remains, and a « Par nuit » register adding up to the list; `GET /api/finance/tourist-tax/report.csv` exports the same selection. The monthly extraction now reads its rows through the same function; its payload is unchanged.
- « Taxe de séjour » page rebuilt on that report: Mois · Trimestre · Année · Personnalisée, logement and declaration filters, a « Liste » / « Par nuit » switch, the stays a platform remits itself greyed with their tag, CSV export, and the totals pinned at the bottom of the screen (« À reverser », « reste à déclarer »; two figures and « Détail des totaux » on a phone) in a new generic `StickyTotalsBar`. `PeriodSelector` gains optional quarter and year kinds.
- **Hosting H1 — the console runs the fleet** (`specs/hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md`): creating a customer provisions its instance by itself (directory, secrets, port, database, first admin, systemd unit, Caddy site, health check, Litestream replica), each step shown live with « Reprendre » on a failure; stopping, reactivating, renaming (301 for 90 days) and erasing need no shell. New console page « Versions »: releases installed once per host (SHA-256 verified) and updates one instance at a time with automatic rollback on a failed health check. The fleet shows real version, process, backup, verified restore, disk and certificate; a probe raises alerts (down, backup lag, disk, certificate, memory, email quota) emailed once then every 24 h; every Sunday each backup is restored and checked; « Restaurer à une date » on the customer page. Host files and runbook in `deploy/hosting/`.
- A hosted instance (`GUESTFLOW_MANAGED=1`) shows « Mises à jour gérées par l’hébergeur » and refuses its own update (403); without an SMTP of its own it sends through the platform (« Envoi par la plateforme », Reply-To the company, daily quota). Self-hosted installs are unchanged.
- Hosting isolation: instances and Litestream run as `guestflow-app` (each instance sees only its own directory), the console as `guestflow`; Litestream's configuration is root-owned and written by a checking wrapper; Caddy's admin API is a unix socket.
- **Accounts: forgotten password, two-step login, support access with consent** (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md). « Mot de passe oublié ? » sends a one-hour, single-use link by email (same answer whatever the address, 3 requests per address and 20 per IP per hour). « Mon compte » turns on a second code — authenticator app (QR code) or email code — with 10 backup codes, « Faire confiance à cet appareil 30 jours » and a 15-minute lock after 5 wrong codes; an admin can turn another user's off, recorded in the account's new security journal; a dashboard card invites admins without one. On a managed instance, the console asks for support access, an admin authorises it (1 h, 24 h or 7 days) or refuses, the console opens the space through a signed 2-minute link as « Support GuestFlow » under a red bar, and Paramètres › Accès du support lists every access and what it did. The TOTP helpers are now shared by the instance and the console. `reset-admin.js --disable-2fa`.
- **Laundry days — several trips a week and a laundry turnaround** (spec `linen-laundry-days.md`, 2026-10-28). The `linen` plugin (1.2.0) takes a set of laundry weekdays and a turnaround (1 to 14 days) instead of one weekday: the Linge page edits both, the Planning shows a card on every laundry day with where each pick-up comes from (« déposé mar. 21 ») and what still waits at the laundry, and the stock projection keeps linen unavailable until it is ready. A change applies from tomorrow and the past keeps its rhythm. New `GET/PUT /api/laundry/schedule`, `POST /api/laundry/schedule/preview`, `GET /api/planning/laundry/days`; removed `GET /api/planning/laundry` and `GET /api/planning/linen-inventory`. The shortage alert says when the shortage ends (`lastShortageDate`, `laterShortage`).
- **One payment, several methods** (spec `mixed-method-payments.md`, 2026-11-09). A payment recorded by hand (acompte, solde, complément, fin de séjour, paiement unique, note en séjour, échéance) can be split over 1-4 lines — CB, espèces, chèque, virement, chèques-vacances, autre — behind a discreet « Plusieurs moyens » in the payment form; cash goes to the caisse interne by default. Lines under the amount due ask « Reste à percevoir » or « Geste commercial »; a split payment is corrected with « Modifier », its reste collected later with « Marquer payé ». An amount raised after the payment becomes a reste à percevoir; an amount lowered below it is shown as a trop-perçu. The books, « Déjà payé », the turnover and the bank reconciliation count only the part on the books. SDK 2.24.0.
- plugin:accounting-export A split payment is booked settlement by settlement, at each date, for its share on the books; its geste commercial is a rabais.
- plugin:online-payment The bank reconciliation expects only the card, cheque and transfer lines of a split payment.
- plugin:sas « Plusieurs moyens » records a split payment at the check-out and on the single arrival payment.
- **Restore an archived option** (spec `options-resources-ux.md` rules 26-27). A folded « Archivées » section at the bottom of the options list shows what was archived, with « Restaurer » (`POST /api/options/:id/restore`); the per-property defaults the archive dropped are not brought back.
- **A closure is refused over an open payment request, and « Retirer le lien » withdraws a link by hand** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 40-42, 49, 2026-10-30). In the closure dialog, as soon as the logement and both dates are set, the server says what the closure would meet: a red line « Réservation sur ces dates : fermeture impossible. » listing every stay, an amber line « Demande de paiement en cours sur ces dates. » listing each devis whose payment link is still open (amount, end of validity), and an amber line « Devis en cours sur ces dates. » for a devis without one; each row has « Ouvrir » (new tab on desktop, in place on a phone), and the lines are asked again when the operator comes back to the tab. Save stays enabled: the server refuses a reservation (409 `RESERVATION_OVERLAP`, now with its list) and an open request (new 409 `OPEN_PAYMENT_REQUEST`, « … la retirer avant d'enregistrer la fermeture. »), never a devis without a link. On the fiche and the devis, the ledger row of an open link offers « Retirer le lien » behind the confirmation « Retirer ce lien ? Il ne pourra plus être payé. »: the link is withdrawn (`cancelReason = 'operator'`), deactivated at the provider (the poll retries when that fails), and the row reads « Lien retiré le {jour} » with « Renvoyer la demande »; a link the guest has just paid is refused and its payment booked at once (also later, by the poll or before a new request, when it was paid before a withdrawal made while the provider was disconnected; on nights closed or taken since, without the guest confirmation and with the alert « Action requise : déplacer le séjour ou le rembourser. »), a second open link of the same row gets its own button, and cancelled or set-aside stays never offer it. A stay with at least one night under a closure gets no payment link: the mint and the request or reminder e-mail are refused 409 `CLOSED_DATES` « Logement fermé sur ces dates : aucun lien créé. » (spec rule 55). A payment received on a link withdrawn by hand after its withdrawal blocks every new payment request of the stay (409 `UNSETTLED_WITHDRAWN_PAYMENT` « Paiement reçu sur un lien retiré : à traiter avant toute nouvelle demande. ») until the ledger row's « Marquer remboursé » or « Enregistrer sur le séjour » settles it — both name the amount, « Enregistrer » says whether a devis converts and whether the guest is confirmed, a cancelled stay offers the refund only — and the website's payment page tells the visitor « Un paiement est en cours de vérification : contacter l’établissement. » (EN included) (spec rule 56). New endpoints `GET /api/establishment-closures/conflicts` and `POST /api/payments/links/:id/withdraw`; new generic component `ConflictList`. Closures written by a tariff recipe are not concerned. +68 server tests, +22 client tests.
- **Payment e-mails state until when their link is valid, and the acompte reminder carries a fresh link** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 26-30, 35, 36, 2026-10-30). New template variable `{{paymentLinkValidUntil}}` (« Fin de validité du lien »), written in the guest's language and Paris time: « Ce lien est valable jusqu'au lundi 2 novembre à 18 h 00. » / « This link is valid until Monday 2 November at 6:00 pm. », on the line after the link of the acompte, solde, full-payment and installment requests and of the acompte reminder. The acompte reminder no longer re-offers whatever link is open: when it is sent, it reuses the open link only while it has 24 hours left and otherwise mints a fresh one; the request templates sent from the e-mail dialog do the same. Their preview prints « (lien de paiement créé à l'envoi) » when only the send will create the link, and the send fills the previewed text with the link it resolved (an outdated link in the preview is replaced, a placeholder never leaves); a request without any link to carry is refused, and a send with no address or no SMTP is refused before a link is created. Every template stays Manual: nothing is sent without the operator. The public payment status answers `expired` when the request's link lapsed unpaid (while a payment made just before the deadline may still land, it keeps answering « en attente »), and the WordPress plugin 1.19.0 shows « Ce lien de paiement a expiré. Refaire la réservation depuis la page du logement. » (English included); an older plugin ends on « Paiement en attente » as before. +74 server tests, +3 client tests.
- **Paid dates are held, and a request whose dates were taken loses its link** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 10-12, 31-33, 37-39, 46, 48, 2026-10-31). A reservation holds its nights once it carries money (an acompte paid is enough, the solde open or not; a cash payment, a paid installment or a paid link count) or when it comes from a platform; a devis never holds them, an unpaid direct reservation neither, and only a cancellation frees them. As soon as such a stay covers a night of an unpaid devis or reservation — a platform booking imported by iCal or moved by its feed, the first of two devis paid, an acompte recorded, a stay created or re-dated by hand, « Rétablir », an approved iCal date change — that request's payment links are withdrawn (« Lien retiré · dates prises »), read at the provider then deactivated, and no new link is created on it (409 « Dates déjà prises par une autre réservation : aucun lien créé. »). Two unpaid requests on the same nights both keep their links: the first paid wins. A link paid in flight is still booked, in date conflict, without the guest confirmation, and the admin is told « Paiement reçu sur des dates déjà prises — … Action requise : déplacer le séjour ou le rembourser. ». A platform booking that lands on held nights is recorded and called an over-booking (« Surbooking : Booking sur des dates réservées », naming the stay that holds them), and approving an iCal date change onto them asks first, with the stays to open. A devis cannot be created or moved onto held nights (409 « Dates déjà réservées : devis non enregistré. », with the stays listed in the dialog; the devis fiche no longer refuses a save on the nights of an unpaid reservation by itself); a devis or an unpaid reservation whose nights were taken since stays as it is, marked « Dates prises » in its action bar and in the devis list. A duplicate set aside withdraws its links too. No e-mail is sent to the guest by this change. New generic component `DatesTakenBadge`. +44 server tests, +12 client tests.
- **A guest whose dates were taken is told, once** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 35, 43-45, 47, 64-66, 2026-10-31). When the payment link of a direct devis or unpaid reservation is withdrawn because another stay took its nights, and once the provider has read that link unpaid one hour after its deactivation (no payment paid or still in flight), GuestFlow mails the guest « Dates plus disponibles » (FR / EN, warm and short, no link, no amount), once per stay (the devis and the reservation it became count as one): the single exception, granted by Adrien on 2026-10-30, to « no guest e-mail without validation » (one named constant `AUTO_SEND_EXCEPTIONS`, read by its sender alone; the template stays Manual in the database and `autoSendPolicy` still refuses it). The notice answers that withdrawal only, judged once: nothing is sent when the stay that took the nights no longer holds them (even if another stay books them later), for a platform stay, when the nights went to a stay of the same client, for a lapsed link, a started stay, « Retirer le lien », a link paid meanwhile, or the stays withdrawn at the upgrade. A send interrupted after SMTP accepted it reads « envoi incertain » and is never repeated. A payment still in flight 24 h after the withdrawal stops the checks: the guest is not told and the operators get one alert « Paiement en cours depuis 24 h sur un lien retiré : à vérifier chez le prestataire. ». Template off, no address, no English version or no SMTP: nothing leaves. The operators get a push « Dates prises — {client} » (client prévenu / non prévenu, with the reason) and the home a « Dates prises » card (the stay that took the dates as a link, the reason when not told, « Ouvrir », « Vu »). In Emails › Modèles its mode reads « Automatique (exception) » and cannot be changed (409 `SEND_MODE_LOCKED`); the history says « Envoyé automatiquement (dates prises) ». The acompte reminder on taken dates is no longer proposed in « Emails à envoyer », and its send and preview are refused « Dates déjà prises par une autre réservation : relance non envoyée. » (traced once in the history, and proposed again once the nights are free). Sent by a core per-minute pass, never from a GET. New component `DatesTakenAlert`. +52 server tests, +8 client tests.
- **Payment links now expire** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 6-9, 13-24, 2026-10-30). Every link minted from this version is valid 36 hours from its creation (`expiresAt`, whatever Qonto answers), and a request reuses an open link only while it has 24 hours left — otherwise the old link lapses and a fresh one is minted. From 00:00 Paris on the arrival day, the acompte / solde / séjour / échéance links of the stay lapse too (a link minted that day keeps its 36 hours; complement links are untouched); links of a cancelled or missing stay are withdrawn. A lifecycle pass moves them out of `open` without a provider call, then deactivates each at the provider first and reads its payment after: a payment made before the deactivation is booked and confirmed like any other, and one last look an hour later catches a payment completed after that read. It runs at the start of every poll pass and from the new online-payment job `payment-link-lifecycle` every 15 minutes (online-payment 1.5.0, SDK 2.18.0: `paymentPollRunner.runLinkLifecycle`). A refused deactivation still reads the payment (a provider refuses to deactivate a link already paid), so a payment whose webhook was lost is booked — at once when it surfaces on a new request, which is then refused; a lapsed link whose deactivation keeps failing alerts the admin once after 24 hours. The ledger row says « valable jusqu'au … à … », « Lien expiré le … à … », « Lien expiré · séjour commencé », and « · désactivation en attente chez Qonto ». A paid link no longer keeps an owed-deactivation marker, which could block the plugin's uninstall. +74 server tests.
- **Performance guards in CI** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). The client job fails when the JavaScript loaded at startup exceeds its budget (`scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs`, chunks named), and a server test fails when the email texts, preview, attention, platforms, timeline and pending endpoints run more SQL statements on 2 000 reservations than on 20 or take over 250 ms. +17 server tests.
- **Plugin dependencies, seen on both sides** (spec `plugin-dependencies.md`, 2026-10-29). One server-computed graph (`utils/pluginGraph.js`) over the manifests' `requires`, read by the Plugins page, the reconciler, the loader and the console. Every plugin view carries `dependencies` (« Nécessite … » with the status of an unmet requirement, « Utilisé par … ») and `actions` (activate / deactivate / uninstall, each with the plugins it takes along or the reason it is refused, said before the click). Install and activate take the requirements that are off along under one confirmation (`withRequirements`), deactivate takes the active dependants along (`withDependents`), all or nothing; uninstall never cascades. A development tree now installs from the same plan (a requirement was silently skipped there). A dependency cycle is refused `REQUIREMENT_CYCLE` and its members are left unloaded. `check-plugin-versions.mjs` fails a `requires` naming a plugin that is not shipped, a range the shipped version breaks, a self-requirement or a cycle. Console: « Nécessite / Utilisé par » under each module, switches that take the linked modules along with a confirmation, and a plan or option that allows a plugin without its requirement is refused.
- **Plugins are set up by the operator, from the console** (spec `plugin-operator-provisioning.md`, 2026-10-28). The customer page has a « Modules » section: one switch per module the plan allows, saved at once, with the state the space actually reports (active, waiting, failed, blocked, to configure). The choice travels in the signed licence (`activePlugins`) and the space applies it within a minute, with no restart and no new listening port. « Nouveau client » ticks the plan's modules. A space not yet read is seeded from what it runs before any licence carries the choice, so no existing space changes. +46 server tests, +13 console client tests.
- **Erasing one module's data, from the console** (spec `plugin-operator-provisioning.md`, rules 22–23). On an inactive module that still holds data, « Effacer les données… » lists what will be erased and asks for the customer's slug typed. The order travels once in the licence; the space runs it through the existing purge, and the console closes it when the space reports it done. A hosted space keeps its continuous backup. +7 server tests, +3 console client tests.
- **Plugins — phase 0: the Plugins page, and what is not installed disappears** (spec `plugins-phase-0-foundation.md`, 2026-09-28). Twelve optional features become built-in plugins, driven from **Paramètres › Plugins** (tabs « Installés » / « Disponibles », search, Activer / Désactiver, Désinstaller in two clicks): hourly resources, linen, website booking, Sowel gate access, Neat, online payment (Qonto), Google Agenda, accounting export, guided arrival/departure (SAS), tariff recipes, school holidays, Météo-France. An inactive plugin leaves no entry point — menu, settings, dashboard alerts, reservation page, SAS steps, planning cards — its routes answer 404 `PLUGIN_INACTIVE` and its background jobs stop. Deactivating keeps every piece of data; money already recorded never changes. Deactivation is refused while a Qonto link is open or a reception-only / accountant-only account is active. +27 server tests, +22 client tests, +5 E2E.
- **Plugins — phase 3c: hourly resources move out of the core** (spec `plugins-phase-3c-hourly-resources.md`). The core gains two plugin contracts: a **price-line contributor** (a plugin prices a resource line inside the engine's loop; a sold line still goes through its locked snapshot) and a **SAS commit contract** (a plugin step validates before the commit, bills lines tagged `sasLineKey` recomputed on every commit, writes inside the transaction). The bath's pricing, external bookings, scheduling, planning cards, SAS step, resource fields, session picker and Calendrier › Ressources become the `hourly-resources` plugin; new client slots `calendar.menu`, `resources.fields`, `reservation.resourceLine`, timed `planning.days` entries and collecting SAS steps. Without the plugin nothing is sold by the hour: the resources leave the catalogue, the property and the site, an addition answers 422, and a sold line stays frozen and read-only with its sessions. Hours, slots and the free hour now apply to `per_hour` resources only. Erasing the plugin warns about paid external bookings, then drops them and the slot settings. +27 server tests, +9 client tests, +1 E2E.
- **Plugins as packages, a signed registry, install and update from the Plugins page** (spec
  `plugins-phase-4-registry.md`, phase 4 of `plugins-inventory.md`). Each of the twelve plugins has a
  manifest and its own version, and is built into a package (`<id>-<version>.tgz`, server code + its
  own ES module) shipped in the release archive under `plugins-dist/`. A release loads every plugin
  from its package — the client bundle no longer contains any plugin code, and a plugin that is not
  installed costs nothing. The control plane gains a « Registre » page and serves a signed index;
  an instance with `GUESTFLOW_REGISTRY_URL` offers the newer versions it publishes. The Plugins page
  shows each version, a « Mise à jour x.y.z » chip with the changelog lines, « Tout mettre à jour »,
  refusals in plain French, an « Incompatible avec cette version » state and a 7-day rollback. An
  update restarts the instance (PM2). Release tooling: `scripts/build-plugin-packages.mjs`,
  `scripts/check-plugin-versions.mjs` (the `verify` job), `plugin:<id>` changelog bullets folded into
  the manifests. A production-build E2E suite (`npm run test:e2e:prod`) runs in CI.
- **Plugin packages, after review** (spec `plugins-phase-4-registry.md` rules 26–32): a plugin bundle
  uses the app's own MUI (its icons keep the GuestFlow theme) and the app loads only the MUI parts
  plugins use; plugin files are cached by content hash; a pull request that changes a plugin bumps its
  version and writes its French changelog lines in the manifest (CI checks it), so the release PR never
  edits a manifest; installs take the update lock and are all-or-nothing; a replayed registry index is
  refused; writes under `/api` from another site are refused (403); the rollback asks first and the
  restart wait gives up after 90 s with « Réessayer ».
- **A new instance starts neutral: the start assistant** (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` §3.D, 2026-10-07). An admin of a new database lands on `/demarrage`: company, first property (beds, capacity, price), plugins, then the dashboard. « Plus tard » at every step; it reopens from Réglages › Système.
- **« Textes des mails »** (Réglages › Emails, §3.A–3.B). Every sentence of the guest sequence is an editable text in French and English, with neutral defaults, token chips and the refusal of an unknown token. Options reach the J-7 and J-2 through « phrases » that may cover several options; the J-2 confirmations have their own order. A property may override four texts; a resource carries its own sentence once booked.
- **Plan comptable as settings** (§3.F). The ten accounts and the journal code of the sales export are editable on the Plan comptable page, with today's numbers as defaults; the CSV is byte-identical with the defaults.
- **Public API, additive** (specs/public-api.md; website-booking plugin 1.2.0, SDK 1.3.0): `GET /public/v1/properties/:id/availability` returns `nextAvailableDate`, the first bookable arrival on or after the window (free nights that pass the minimum/maximum stay and the arrival/departure days), looked up to a year ahead (`null` when none). A refused contact field of `POST /public/v1/booking-requests` carries `reason` and a `message` in the request's language under `details[]`, and the summary message reads « Coordonnées incomplètes ou invalides. » The SDK exposes the core module `changeover` and `pricing.calculateBaseStayPrice`.
- plugin:website-booking Website booking requests carry an internal proof of work (signed challenge, solved by the browser in a Web Worker, checked with one hash, spent once, never sooner than 3 s): its difficulty grows per visitor address and slightly during a flood, capped, and never refuses a guest. Enforced once the site reports WordPress plugin 1.18.0; until then Paramètres › Intégrations › Site says « Protection anti-robot inactive ».
- plugin:website-booking At most 3 new quotes per e-mail address per 24 h; the same e-mail, property and dates update an untouched website draft in place (same number and CGV acceptances, price recomputed by the engine, message appended, « Demande mise à jour depuis le site » history line); a quote the operator opened, edited, sent or linked to a payment never changes — only the guest's message is appended (« Message du client après envoi » when it had been sent). A quote's id, number and payment token are only answered to a request carrying that token. Messages are capped at 2,000 characters and 10 appends per quote.
- plugin:website-booking Website requests nobody opened, sent, edited, paid or converted are deleted after 30 days with their CGV acceptance and the client created for them; their numbers are never reused. The home card flags more than 20 requests in an hour.
- Operator e-mails for website requests are grouped: the first of a 15-minute window is sent at once, the rest in one summary (state kept in the database across restarts).
- WordPress plugin 1.18.0: proof-of-work challenge relayed and solved in the drawer, « Vérification… » and « Votre demande du … a été mise à jour. ».
- **Bank reconciliation (online-payment plugin 1.2.0, SDK 1.6.0).** The credits of the Qonto account are read (every 8 hours, and « Actualiser ») and matched to the payments the fiches expect — platform payouts, payment-link payouts, guests' transfers and the on-site card batches — to surface the gaps (`specs/qonto-bank-reconciliation.md`). A « Rapprochement bancaire » card under the payments ledger of the fiche, a page under Suivi financier, a subject on the home and a settings card (account, start date, recognition patterns, fees). A proposal is never a decision: « Confirmer » marks the échéance received on the fiche at the date of the transfer through the core's own « Marquer perçu » (same guards and history), « Défaire » puts the fiche back, and nothing is ever changed on an échéance already marked received. New core modules exposed to plugins: `reservationLedger`, `reservationPaymentMark`, `platformDisplay`; the Qonto client gains `listTransactions`; a recette tool loads a Qonto CSV export into a shadow database (`server/scripts/bank-transactions-from-qonto-csv.js`).
- **Migration.** New plugin tables `bank_transactions`, `bank_reconciliation_lines`, `bank_reconciliation_fiche_writes`, `bank_reconciliation_discards`, `bank_transactions_outside` (migration `plugin:online-payment:reconciliation_v1`); no core table changes. « Effacer aussi ses données » drops them; the fiches already marked stay marked.
- **Bank reconciliation — split payments, grouped payouts, automatic confirmation, bank line on « Échéances » (online-payment plugin 1.4.0, SDK 2.9.0; lot 2 of `specs/reconciliation-assisted-matching.md`).** A guest who pays an échéance (or the whole stay) in two or three transfers carrying their name is proposed as one proposal over several credits. Among equal installments only the earliest due one is proposed. A grouped platform payout now also combines stays whose payout the fiche does not carry yet (gross minus the platform's commission, 1 % tolerance per estimate, one payout run of 14 days): one estimate → « probable », two or more → « à vérifier » with « Répartir et confirmer » — shares are typed, previewed, and refused until they make the credit to the cent (`SHARES_DO_NOT_SUM`). After each read of the account (8-hour job, « Actualiser ») the « sûr » single exact matches confirm themselves — paid flag and date only, never a payout or an amount — with an « auto » badge, an history entry « Rapprochement bancaire automatique » and « Défaire » (which also discards the pair); the setting « Confirmer automatiquement les correspondances sûres » turns it off. The home's « Échéances » card shows « Virement trouvé : … » with « Confirmer », or « Rien sur le compte », through the new generic SDK hook `ctx.paymentDeadlineDecorator` (inert without a plugin). `late` receipts no longer count in « Écarts de règlement » (the « Échéances » card already shows them).
- **Platform penalties (online-payment plugin 1.4.0, accounting-export plugin 1.0.15, SDK 2.9.0; lot 3 of `specs/reconciliation-assisted-matching.md`).** An amount a platform keeps on a payout besides its commission and the tourist tax is declared on the stay (« Déclarer une pénalité » in the « Paiement plateforme » block, or from a reconciliation gap, prefilled with the gap). The server asks whether the payout typed on the fiche is the statement figure or the bank amount: in the second case the penalty is added back to the payout so the commission drops by the same amount instead of swallowing the penalty. Guards in words (direct or cancelled stay, amount, motif, date, penalties above the payout, negative commission); a warning when a month already transmitted changes; « Supprimer » restores the payout only if it was not edited since. The payout row of the ledger shows a line per penalty; « total de séjour », « encaissé », « à encaisser », a late payout and the cash-flow line are net of the penalties, commissions stay the commission alone. The reconciliation expects `payout − acompte net − penalties` and writes `credit + acompte net + penalties`, so the gap clears once declared. Accounting: one entry per penalty, dated on the penalty, debit the « Pénalités plateformes » account (**`658000` by default, editable in the Plan comptable — to be confirmed with the accountant**), credit the client's account, no VAT. New routes `…/reservations/:id/platform-penalties[/preview|/:penaltyId]`; new core module `platformPenaltiesModel` exposed to plugins.
- **Payments as a status ledger on the reservation fiche** (specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md): one row per money movement — acompte, solde (or « Versement <plateforme> »), complements, notes en séjour, refunds and trop-perçu, caution received and returned — each with its amount, its state in words (« À percevoir avant le 23/08 », « En retard depuis… », « Perçu le 12/08 · lien de paiement »), one action and « Reste à percevoir » / « Tout est perçu » at the bottom (the same figure as the Finance « Suivi opérationnel »). The server builds every row.
- **Acompte and solde are settable from their row**: the acompte between 0 and the accommodation, the solde between the tourist tax and the pre-arrival total (the split moves, not the price); once the acompte is perceived, a different solde is a different accommodation price, found by re-running the whole pricing engine, to the nearest reachable cent, never below the acompte received. The consequence shows before « Valider »; an invalid amount is refused in French under the field.
- « Marquer perçu » opens a small form under the row (date of the day, « Caisse interne » on the complements only); « Marquer reçu » on a platform payout asks for the transfer, shows the gap with the expected net and proposes the commission. Undoing a payment asks for confirmation, naming the date and the accounting entry.
- Hourly resources (plugin): a booking outside a stay shows its price line by line (day, evening, offered by the property, offered time, VAT), offers time by steps of 15 minutes, and is paid at booking or at a one-screen arrival SAS with the fiches' payment lines (cash to the caisse interne by default). Paid on the books, it gets a Qonto invoice joined to the day's on-site card batch, and enters the accounting export with the invoice number as « Pièce ». Setting « TVA des ventes hors séjour », 20 % by default. See `specs/resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md`.
- Plugin SDK 2.25.0: `ctx.invoiceIssuer` and `ctx.standaloneSales`, inert without plugins.
- **Console — push a plugin version to the fleet** (spec `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 11, wired 2026-10-18). « Versions › Plugins » lists each installed plugin with what the instances run and the registry's published versions; a push goes to the instances that have the plugin, one at a time, through the instance's own `scripts/switch-plugin-package.js` (same checks as a download), with a health check on the plugin's loaded version (`/api/version?plugin=<id>`) and a rollback to the snapshot otherwise.
- **Website, set up in two pastes:** Paramètres › Intégrations › « Site internet (WordPress) » shows the API URL and the key to paste into WordPress, masked with « Copier », and « Régénérer la clé ». The WordPress plugin (1.15.0) derives the rest: the default lodging is picked by name from GuestFlow's list, the booking and CGV pages are found by their content or created in one click, a reverse proxy on the local network is recognised without a setting, and the connection is tested when the settings page opens.
- plugin:hourly-resources The resource cards of Options & ressources show the evening rate, the external rates, the free first hour and the schedule (SDK 2.8.0 `ctx.resourceCatalogueLines`).
- **SDK 2.11.0: `ctx.onActivate(fn)`.** A plugin can recover from its own data each time it goes from off to live (activated, or installed). Google Vacation Rentals 1.0.2 uses it to re-send everything to Google at once (spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md` rule 17).
- **Pool open all year** (specs/settings-input-guards.md): « Piscine » offers « Toute l'année » or « Saison » on the Séjour tab; the switch is now kept after saving, and a season whose closing day is before its opening day is refused (« La fermeture suit l'ouverture. »). No migration: a year-round pool is stored as 01-01 to 12-31.
- **A shadow runs like production** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). `node scripts/start-shadow.mjs --db <copy> --port <n>` copies a database, builds the client and the plugin packages and serves them from one server process (`--dev` keeps the Vite dev server, which is 3 to 6 times slower). `e2e/perf/measure-pages.mjs` measures page loads, with optional CPU throttling. +5 tests.
- **Several social links in the thank-you e-mail** (specs/settings-input-guards.md): « Lien Instagram » becomes « Réseaux sociaux » in Paramètres › Emails & notifications — up to 8 links among Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, YouTube, Pinterest, LinkedIn and X, in the order chosen, each checked against its network. One link renders the J+1 e-mail exactly as before; several are listed one per line.
- **Guest emails in text and HTML** (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rules 37–39, UI/UX review decision 15): every email sent to a guest now leaves as `multipart/alternative` — the text unchanged, plus a sober HTML part derived by the server from that same text (the sender's name in serif, paragraphs, lists, the « Libellé : valeur » recap as two columns, addresses, emails and phone numbers as plain links; no image, nothing loaded remotely, no tracking, dark-mode colours, 600 px column). « Textes des emails » previews either part (« Texte | HTML »). Account emails and notifications stay plain text. References and bare 10-digit codes are not turned into phone links.
- **Paramètres — summary page, four groups and search** (`specs/settings-rationalization.md` rules 24–27; UI/UX review 2026-10-18, proposal 20 and decision 12). « Paramètres » opens a summary: each page on one row with a state line computed by the server (`GET /api/settings/summary`: « 2 logements », « Envoi non configuré », « 12 actifs · 4 à configurer »…), in four groups (Établissement, Séjours, Paiements et emails, Compte et système), with a search that understands wifi, iban, smtp, 2fa. The sidebar submenu shows the four groups and unfolds only the open page's group (at most 9 rows instead of 15). Intégrations moves next to Plugins.
- **Envoi — the sending server deduced from the address** (rule 28, decision 14). The server recognises the provider from the address's domain or its MX records (DNS only, `POST /api/settings/smtp-detect`) and fills host and security at save when none is configured and a password is there; a configured host always wins, a provider not found opens the manual fields. Server, login and security now sit under « Réglages avancés »; the disabled test button says what it waits for.
- **Franchise threshold** (`specs/vat-optional-mode.md` rules 35 to 44). Under "Sans TVA · art. 293 B", Settings › TVA & exercice gains a "Seuil de franchise (€)" field, empty by default: GuestFlow ships no legal amount, the operator types its own. The Suivi financier then shows a card: the turnover collected during the calendar year (each payment on its date, platform commission and penalty added back, tourist tax and caisse interne left out), the projection to 31 December from the reservations in progress, a gauge, the level (under the threshold, threshold close at 80 %, projection beyond, exceeded) and last year as a caption. From "Seuil proche", a "Seuil de franchise" line joins "À traiter" on the home. Read-only; invisible for a VAT-registered installation.
- **« Sans TVA »: a dated VAT regime** (`specs/vat-optional-mode.md`). Paramètres › TVA & exercice › « Changer de régime… »: an installation that is not subject to VAT (neither a company nor with an accountant) runs without any VAT, and can change regime on a date — reservations made from…, stays starting from…, or a correction of every reservation. A preview dialog announces what changes (reservations, tourist tax, open payment links, accounting months) before confirming; the history is permanent and a change not yet in use can be cancelled.
- Without VAT, the prices the client pays do not move: the PDF quote, the pricing summary, the payment links, the financial follow-up and the accounting export lose their VAT lines, and the chosen legal mention (art. 293 B or art. 261 D 4° of the CGI, in French and English) is printed instead. The proportional tourist tax follows the price paid; a frozen or declared tax never changes.
- The regime is a core setting, independent of the accounting plugin; each reservation keeps the regime under which it was sold. Plugin SDK 2.10.0 (`coreModule('vatRegimeModel')`), accounting export 1.0.16.
- A regime change recalculates and saves the proportional tourist tax of the stays concerned (frozen or declared tax excepted) through a targeted write (only the tax and the amounts that depend on it change, the rest of the fiche stays identical); the preview announces exactly what will be saved, and open payment links whose amount changes are abandoned. A change that rewrites collected money or accounting months asks for a confirmation whatever its scope; a change dated in the future can be cancelled until its date comes.

## Changed
- **« Validation en deux étapes »: the activation as every site does it** (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rules 38–53, replaces the « Second code » card): « Mon compte » shows a status and « Activer », which opens one dialog in three steps — password, authenticator app, backup codes. A computer shows the QR code with the key folded behind « Saisie manuelle de la clé »; a phone gets « Ouvrir dans l’appli d’authentification » and « Copier la clé » (the QR code folded). The entry carries « GuestFlow » and « company · email » in the app. The code is sent at its 6th digit and a wrong one says the tries left (then a clock hint); an activation expires after 15 minutes and « Annuler » leaves nothing behind. The email code is a fallback link under the app. « Terminé » waits until the backup codes are copied, downloaded or ticked as saved. Turned on, the card says the method, its date and the codes left (warning from 3), and « Changer d’appli » swaps the app without ever leaving the account unprotected. The dashboard card opens the dialog directly. The name is the same everywhere (card, dashboard, journal, Paramètres › Utilisateurs); the settings search still finds « Second code » and « 2FA ».
- Paramètres › Plateformes: new « E-mails aux voyageurs » settings per platform — « Adresse e-mail relais » (no season e-mail to a relay address) and « Où laisser un avis » (FR / EN wording of the J+1 review sentence). The guest e-mail sequence no longer recognises Airbnb, Booking or Gîtes de France by name.
- Push notification toggles are served by the server with their labels; a plugin declares its own with `ctx.pushChannel` (SDK 2.6.0).
- A payment provider words its own errors (`describeError`); the insurance refusals no longer name Neat.
- Platform chips read the server's display name (« Gîtes de France »); `reservation.platform` is titled « Plateforme » everywhere.
- The settings search words and the « Code portail » field of plugin features come from their plugins; the start assistant's options question uses neutral examples.
- plugin:neat Declares its « Souscriptions Neat » push toggle itself.
- plugin:online-payment Words « Qonto à connecter d'abord » itself, and brings its settings search words.
- plugin:sas Draws the « Code portail » field of Établissement, with its search word.
- plugin:hourly-resources Brings its settings search words to Options & ressources.
- plugin:tariff-recipes Brings its settings search words.
- plugin:linen Brings its settings search words.
- Dependencies: control-plane server (body-parser, nodemailer, qs), control-plane client (undici, vitest mocker) and server (ip-address) bumped to their fixed versions; `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities everywhere.
- **Boot log of paid payment links left without a stay** (`plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 27): `[integrity] N lien(s) de paiement payé(s) sans séjour`, nothing deleted. The refusal to delete a stay carrying money is part of the reservations fixes.
- **`X-Forwarded-For` trust documented** (spec `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 35, audit 2026-10-28): `TRUST_PROXY_HOPS` without `GUESTFLOW_PROXY_SECRET` logs a `[security]` warning at boot. Behaviour and production defaults unchanged.
- **Tests — a single test file run by hand (`node --test <file>`) no longer opens, migrates or creates `server/guestflow.db`, `server/.env.local` or `server/uploads`, and the scratch directories of a run are removed with it** (`server-test-isolation.md` rule 4, audit R2). The preload keeps the deploy root private too; the spec-coverage gate recognises `<spec> rule N` without the `specs/` prefix (`spec-rule-coverage.md` rule 15); E2E: the forced first-login flow is tested again, and five timing flakes are hardened.
- **SMTP: STARTTLS is required with a login** (spec `admin-account-management.md` rule 16, audit R22 E22-2). On a transport without implicit TLS, a login now requires the upgrade: instance SMTP, platform sender and console mailer. A server with no TLS on 587 now fails with `SMTP_TEST_FAILED` instead of sending the password and the mail in clear. A relay without a login keeps working.
- **Wording** (audit R3, spec `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rule 36). Confirmations, alerts, empty states and tooltips drop « Veuillez », « Voulez-vous », « Êtes-vous » and « votre » : « Supprimer cette réservation ? », « Forcer l'enregistrement ? », « Aucune saison. ». +1 client test.
- **A GuestFlow major no longer switches the plugins off** (decision D24, specs `plugins-phase-4-registry.md` rule 62, superseding rule 34, and `self-update-and-releases.md` rule 54; audits R34-CON-H1 = R34-P1). Every plugin declared « GuestFlow <4.0.0 », so installing 4.0.0 would have left all sixteen unloaded at boot (« Incompatible avec cette version. »), with no compatible version to offer: the site's booking engine, payment links, sas, accounting export and intakes off until a new release. A plugin is now judged on its SDK range and on the floor of its GuestFlow range; a package already installed with « <4.0.0 » keeps loading on 4.0.0 and later while SDK 2 is served. The sixteen manifests write a floor only (one patch bump each), the package build refuses a ceiling, and `check-plugin-versions` (every PR, the release `verify` job, the release skill's pre-flight with `--core-version X.Y.Z`) refuses a release whose own plugins its core or SDK would refuse.
- plugin:guest-app A stay places at most 6 card orders per hour, whatever its sessions or addresses (`GUEST_CARD_ORDER_RATELIMIT_MAX` / `_WINDOW_MS`, rule 57): each card order mints a payment link at the provider. A refused card order spends nothing; the note stays open. +1 server test.
- **Arrival SAS: the breakfast offer price is computed by the server.** The offer page shows the price of one morning for the covers served, and the mornings page shows the number of mornings, covers, breakfasts and the amount, all taken from the arrival preview (and the same for the « Restauration » meals). The dialog no longer multiplies or rounds anything. `sas` plugin 1.3.0.
- **Clients: a directory that reads at a glance** (specs `clients-upcoming-past-directory.md` rules 11-22, `clients.md` rule 15). « À venir » opens first; « Passés » and a new « Sans séjour » view (stay-less clients, with « Nettoyer les clients ») sit beside it, each with its count. Rows are grouped by when (En séjour, 7 / 30 prochains jours, Plus tard; years for the past), say where and when the guest comes (« Arrive dans 9 j », « Parti il y a 3 sem. »), show the platform and the number of stays, and the e-mail and phone are links. A search looks in the three views and offers the one that matches. One « Trier » select (date de séjour / nom) replaces the column headers, so sorting also works on a phone. The per-row delete icon is gone: deletion goes through the client sheet. `GET /api/clients/directory` takes `view` and answers `groups`, `counts`, `orphanCount` and `elsewhere` (`items` is kept for one release).
- Payments: the end-of-stay complement can be collected only once the check-out is done (the departure SAS committed, or the stay's last day reached without the SAS plugin). Before, « Marquer perçu », the merged card of a complement deferred to the departure, « Encaisser en une fois », a bank reconciliation and « Tout solder » could mark it during the stay or before arrival. They now answer « Complément de fin de séjour encaissable après le départ. » (409 `END_OF_STAY_BEFORE_CHECKOUT`); the fiche shows the mark closed with « Après le départ. » and « Tout solder » leaves it out. The departure SAS settles it as before; notes during the stay are still collected by card as they come. Stored payments are not changed.
- Reservations: an unlocked fiche (admin « Déverrouiller cette fiche » on a started stay) now accepts only the changes that leave the final price and the tourist tax unchanged — above all the amounts expected from the platforms (payout, commissions, penalties). A save that would move them answers « Fiche déverrouillée : le prix final doit rester à X. » (409 `UNLOCKED_PRICE_CHANGED`), the ledger's solde editor refuses to move the price there too, and the unlocked banner reads « Fiche déverrouillée : tout reste modifiable sauf le prix final. ». The refusal of a sale after the end-of-stay complement is collected no longer suggests unlocking: « … décocher « payé » d’abord. ». On a platform that remits the tax with a brut entered, the withheld tax and a brut correction move the final price: they are entered on the locked fiche, not under the unlock. On a started, locked fiche, the solde editor now points to « Réduction (%) », the only price field that fiche accepts.
- **Home and planning without a date picker** (spec `dashboard-planning-scroll.md`, 2026-10-28). The home reads its day lists in one call (`GET /api/planning/today`) and folds an empty « Départs du jour » / « Arrivées du jour » to one line; with no arrival today it shows the next arrival with its date and distance. The planning is a timeline of days that scrolls into the past and the future (one `GET /api/planning/days` per 14-day window, empty days folded into « Rien de prévu · du … au … », sticky day headers, « Aujourd'hui » as a bar action). The date cluster of both bars is gone.
- **No date more than five years ahead** (Adrien's decision of 2026-11-02; specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md rule 13, readings of specs/establishment-closures.md rule 18 and specs/finance-dashboard-redesign.md rule 51): a stay, a devis, an iCal date change, a closure or the end of a « Personnalisée » finance window past today + 5 years is refused with « Date trop lointaine : 5 ans au plus. », shown under the date on the fiche, the devis, the closure dialog and the finance period before any request. A row already stored past the horizon keeps saving as long as its dates are not edited (restore, devis extension and conversion carry their stored dates). The prod snapshot holds no such row.
- **No date more than five years back either, and the server judges every entered date** (Adrien's decision of 2026-11-02; specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md rule 13, second reading): a date before today − 5 years is refused with « Date trop ancienne : 5 ans au plus. » on the stays, devis, iCal date changes, closures and the « Personnalisée » finance window, and the same window (today ± 5 years, a real calendar day) now guards on the server every other date an operator types: payment marks and the arrival payment, installment payments and due dates, a settled note's date, refunds, platform penalties, cancellation compensations, calendar notes, season date ranges, the devis validity override, manual tariff-journal entries, the email simulations, laundry skips, additions and extra trips (linen 1.2.2), school-holiday periods (school-holidays 1.0.5) and resource bookings (hourly-resources 1.3.4). Several of these took any text before (a calendar note on « abc », a season, a school-holiday zone or a booking on 30 February). A date a row already stores is never re-judged, so older rows keep saving; platform dates (iCal, OTA intakes, Qonto, education.gouv.fr) keep their own rules. Plugins read the helpers through SDK 2.20.0 (`coreModule('stayDates')`). The prod snapshot holds no entered date before the window.
- plugin:linen A skip, a manual addition or an extra trip is dated within five years of today, back or ahead; a date already stored stays editable.
- plugin:school-holidays A period's zone dates are real calendar days within five years of today; an imported period keeps saving with its dates.
- plugin:hourly-resources A resource booking is dated on a real day within five years of today; an edited booking keeps its stored day.
- An unknown variable in an email template is now refused at save (422 « Variable inconnue : {{x}} »), like a stay text or a mention, instead of being dropped at send time.
- The email readiness list asks only for what a stored template uses (Solio: nothing), takes the company address when a property has none, and the texts and translation CSV files survive Excel: no text turned into a formula, accents kept from a « CSV (point-virgule) » file.
- **Email texts page opens faster** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). The first preview no longer waits for the 400 ms typing debounce, a folded section's fields are no longer mounted (136 → 52 inputs), and each section's « Email » selector shows its own email instead of logging out-of-range warnings. +6 client tests.
- **Emails › Modèles loads faster** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28).
