The list no longer carries the French and English body of every template (about 60 % of its size); the editor reads them when it opens, with a loading state, and « Enregistrer » waits for them. +4 server tests, +3 client tests.
- plugin:gate-access The key window now comes from the core (`utils/stayWindow.js`), same computation; the plugin asks for SDK 2.26.0.
- **Built files are cached for a year** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). The server serves the content-hashed files of the client build with an immutable cache, so a page no longer revalidates each of its scripts at every load; `index.html` is still revalidated, which is how a release reaches an open browser. +2 tests.
- **Suivi financier — the banner is a light card** (spec `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 52, superseding rule 31; Adrien's choice of 2026-11-05 among four variants). White card with a fir-green top rule, the revenue figure in ink, the figure grid on white with hairlines, the year-over-year pills and « i » definitions in their light-surface style, the cumulative curve in the chart palette with last year dashed. The ink banner's `palette.chart.onInk` tokens are removed.
- Suivi financier: « revenu moyen / nuit » and RevPAR now read the revenue of the period's nights (each stay spread over its nights, options included, tourist tax excluded) instead of the revenue attributed to the period — in the banner, the logements strip, the « Logements » tile and table and the comparison with last year (`specs/finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 53). The tile « Encaissé » is renamed « Déjà payé » (rule 54); « Encaissé » stays for money received in a period (accounting synthesis).
- Suivi financier: last year's pills name their span when only part of the period compares, the open nights name the occupied ones, per-night amounts keep their cents under 100 €, the occupancy chart is drawn on 0-100 %, and a first of the month reads « 1er » in the comparison notices (dashboard and booking pace).
- Suivi financier: last year's pills stay short on a partial comparison; the notice under the banner says once « Comparé à l'an dernier sur N mois sur M ». The occupancy chart is named « et N-1 » only when it draws last year, and its copied data is in percent. « Total déjà payé », « Déjà payé » and « Payé ou passé » replace « encaissé » on the stays' money. A repeated `fiscalYear` is refused with « Exercice inconnu. » and the code `UNKNOWN_EXERCISE` (dashboard, its tables, stay profile), the page then offers « Exercice en cours », and the exercise shown is always in the selector.
- Suivi financier: the revenue badge reads « vs N-1 » without « sur N mois comparables », the notice under the banner says the span once. Profil des séjours: a refused exercise offers « Exercice en cours », as the Suivi financier does.
- Tourist tax: a stay whose tax is frozen at 0 € no longer appears « à déclarer » (monthly extraction, tax report, synthesis, data checks). « Déclarée » refuses a stay whose platform remits the tax itself and a value that is not oui / non. A stay recorded as `Direct` or with an empty platform counts as direct.
- Tax report: a period before the year 1000 or of 9999 is refused instead of failing, a repeated query key is refused, the period, view and filters live in the page's URL (kept when coming back from a fiche), the opening month follows the server's day, a first refusal opens the default month with its reason, the CSV is named after the last day it covers, dates ticks in the server's day and keeps the platforms' amounts in their own column, and the « Par nuit » register marks the nights outside the period.
- Tax report, round 2: a platform's stay whose tax is frozen at 0 € is listed as the platform's with the amount it stated; « Déclarée » accepts a tick sent back on a platform's stay and refuses a new one on a stay frozen at 0 €; the filtered CSV's platform total is left empty so each column adds up; a refused selection no longer replaces the one on screen in the URL; short platform tags show whole on a phone; the totals bar shows every figure at one size on one line.
- Tax report, round 3: a platform's stay frozen at 0 € is attached at its last night and listed only when the platform's mode collects the tax; the list CSV leaves the platforms' counts empty so every column adds up; long windows are read in chunks; on a tablet the totals bar shows every figure in rows instead of folding, labels clamped to two lines; the table switches to cards whenever it would scroll inside its card; platform tags wrap instead of being cut.
- Tax report, round 4: a stay whose tax we collected at arrival stays hidden even on a platform that collects; an iCal key reads as its platform's name; a filtered CSV counts a stay's nights on its first listed row; a tick in cards mode keeps the keyboard focus; the « À reverser » figure keeps its divider beside it when it wraps alone.
- **One request instead of two for identical reads** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). Reads of the same path started at the same moment (the version and the property list, read by the shell and by a page) share one request; any write resets this so a read after a write is always fresh. One request fewer on every page. +7 client tests.
- Paramètres › Intégrations has one tab per plugin (Site, Google Agenda, Neat, Météo-France, Portail, Gîtes de France, Booking) instead of one long page. The open tab is in the address (`?tab=`); « Configurer » on the Plugins page, the Google sign-in return, old `#plugin-…` links and the Paramètres search (« neat », « sowel »…) open the right tab. A draft survives a tab switch, a modified tab carries a dot, a plugin still to set up an amber one, and Save stops on the first refused tab and opens it (specs/integrations-tab-per-plugin.md).
- **Pages load on demand** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). Every page is a separate chunk loaded when opened, the app bar and menu stay while it loads, and a chunk that fails after a release (open tab) reloads once then shows « Page indisponible. ». The JavaScript loaded at startup drops from 637 kB to 285 kB gzip. +7 client tests.
- **Lighter Planning and reservation fiche** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). Planning days carry only what the cards render (51 → 20 kB on the shadow); the reservation fiche reads its property's stays as six fields each, names the options of its option groups by id and reads the resource availability once (244 → 93 kB); the email log preview is full screen on a phone. +8 server tests, +6 client tests.
- **Lighter reads on four screens** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). « Réservations à venir » returns only what its cards show (about 80 % less); a reservation fiche no longer reads the property's pricing seasons (about 60 % of its detail), the option catalogue it never used, nor the whole client list unless the client search is opened; the email pickers (history filter, send and compose dialogs) no longer carry template bodies; the side menu no longer re-reads the properties at every page change. +13 server tests, +4 client tests.
- **Laundry cards**: a day with nothing but bath mats no longer draws a card while their stock is 0 (untracked); the shortage alert shows the first spell (« du mer. 13 au lun. 23 nov. ») and mentions later ones.
- **Booking intake joins the plugin catalogue** (spec `booking-extranet-intake.md` §10, 2026-10-18). Shipped on `master` in v3.10.0 on a minimal engine, « Saisie Booking » now runs on the full plugin engine: its own manifest and package, installed and switched from the Plugins page. The v3.10.0 « Modules » card and its seed batches are gone (spec `plugin-engine-backport.md`, superseded). Plugin SDK 1.1.0: `ctx.core.clients.update`, `ctx.core.reservations.platformCommission` and the `planningDateRange` core module.
- plugin:gate-access SAS « Portail » step: a « Partager » button sends the access link to the other occupants.
- plugin:linen The laundry planning refuses a period longer than 750 days; a manual addition with an infinite quantity counts as 0.
- plugin:hourly-resources The resources planning no longer passes prices, phone numbers or notes to the reception role, and refuses a period longer than 750 days.
- **Airbnb intake joins the plugin catalogue** (spec `airbnb-mail-intake.md` rule 0, note of 2026-10-26). Shipped on `master` in v3.16.0, « Saisie Airbnb » runs on the full plugin engine: its own manifest (1.0.0), installed from the Plugins page, its mails to check under « À traiter » on the home, its own « Airbnb » tab in Intégrations. Plugin SDK 2.3.0: `sdk.mailbox` (read-only IMAP shared by the mail intakes) and the client `IntakeBanner`.
- plugin:website-booking `ROOSTVIEW_API_KEY`, the read-only public API key of master's v3.16.0, is created by the plugin with `PUBLIC_API_KEY` (spec `roostview-api-key.md`).
- plugin:gites-de-france-intake The IMAP server is deduced from the sending server (smtp.… → imap.…) and the fiche banner is the one the other intakes use.
- plugin:booking-intake The fiche banner is the one the other intakes use.
- plugin:neat On a locked fiche, « Réessayer maintenant » and « Résilier chez Neat » stay inactive.
- **Master v3.17.0–v3.18.0 merged into `inte/plugins`** (2026-10-27): the Airbnb checkout-chores rules
  (spec `airbnb-no-checkout-chores.md`) and the platform cleaning fee of the tariff recipes (spec
  `tariff-recipe-platform-cleaning-fee.md`). No channel is recognised by its name any more: the chores
  rules read a platform setting, « Sans corvées de départ » (`platforms.forbidsGuestChores`, Paramètres ›
  Plateformes), and the cleaning-fee computation moved into the `tariff-recipes` plugin. Plugins `sas`
  1.6.0 and `tariff-recipes` 1.3.0.
- plugin:sas On a « Sans corvées de départ » platform, the SAS no longer reminds about the dishes or the bins, and cleaning not done is no longer billed at departure.
- plugin:tariff-recipes A recipe can declare the cleaning fee a platform charges; the application preview reports it.
- **Master v3.21.0 merged into `inte/plugins`** (2026-11-04): the tourist tax of guests added on a reversing
  platform (spec `platform-added-guests-tourist-tax.md`, migration in the v3.21.0 section). The accounting side
  lives in the `accounting-export` plugin (1.0.24), and a paid solde keeps the tax it collected.
- plugin:accounting-export The tourist tax of guests added on a reversing platform is booked with the complement; the solde keeps what the platform reversed.
- **An acompte or an installment paid with several methods is paid whole** (spec `mixed-method-payments.md` rule 44, 2026-11-09): the acompte all on the books or all in the caisse interne, an installment on the books, never with a reste or a geste commercial. These are the amounts a cancellation keeps; a mixed acompte used to requalify money never booked.
- The start assistant's proposed capacity (two per double bed, one per single bed, 1–50) is now computed by the server (`GET /api/onboarding/default-capacity`); the field still follows the beds until typed by hand, and a property saved without a capacity takes the proposed one.
- **Options & ressources, read by category and edited in a side panel** (spec `options-resources-ux.md`, 2026-10-28). The flat 7-column table becomes one foldable section per category (« Sans catégorie » first, the fiche's order) with a search, a property filter and category chips; a row shows the name, the traits that make an option special (offered / included per property, internal, planning card, insurance at 0), and the price it really sells at — « 30 € – 80 € · selon logement », the base price no longer shown when every property overrides it. The editor is a 560 px side panel (a full-height sheet on a phone) in sections: Général, Logements et prix (one table: Proposée · Prix · Inclus · Offert per property), Planning, Visibilité, then the type-specific blocks; every previous setting stays reachable. Archiving moves into the row's « ⋯ » menu with a confirmation; the system options say why they cannot be archived. Resources become cards that show their stock and, through the hourly plugin, the evening and external rates and the schedule. `/options` and `/resources` redirect to their tab. New generic components `SidePanel`, `GroupedList`, `SearchField`; `PricedItemsPage` is removed. New endpoints `GET /api/options/catalogue-view` and `GET /api/resources/catalogue-view`.
- The quote PDF draws every word with embedded fonts: Source Sans 3 (Regular, Semibold, Italic) for the body and one full Source Serif 4 Semibold for the titles, both covering Latin Extended, Vietnamese, Greek and Cyrillic. A guest named « Łukasz Wójcik », « Çağrı Yılmaz », « Nguyễn Thị Ánh », « Γιώργος » or « Анастасия » now prints as typed instead of garbled Helvetica glyphs; CJK, Arabic, Hebrew, Thai and emoji print as empty boxes without breaking the line (specs/ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md rules 27-30).
- Long texts stay in their boxes on the quote PDF: the company name shrinks on its header line, a wrapped client name or address pushes the next lines down instead of overprinting them, and a long logement name fits its pill.
- **Plan comptable, one « Comptes » card** (specs/settings-input-guards.md): the default commission account and the cancellation-indemnity account join the other accounts, grouped as Ventes, Taxes, Commissions et produits divers and Journal. Storage and validation are unchanged.
- **A hosted customer no longer sees or calls the plugin lifecycle** (spec `plugin-operator-provisioning.md`, 2026-10-28). On an operator-managed space (`GUESTFLOW_MANAGED=1`, or a licence carrying `activePlugins`) there is no Paramètres › Plugins page, `/parametres/plugins` and the address of a module that is off read « Page introuvable », the start assistant has no « Plugins » step, and every plugin lifecycle route (install, activate, deactivate, uninstall, update, rollback, status, refresh, page) answers the exact unknown-route 404 for every role, a support session included. `GET /api/plugins` lists only the live plugins as `{ id, name, icon, setup }`; a plugin's own settings are unchanged. Solio, development and the E2E server keep the Plugins page unchanged. +16 server tests, +8 client tests.
- **Plugin removal matrix** (spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md`). A nightly and on-demand workflow removes each plugin from the code in turn and checks that the server suite, the build, the production server on a database still holding its data, the core API, the core pages (desktop and phone) and the saved fiches all keep working, then a sweep deactivates, uninstalls, purges and reinstalls every plugin on a running server. `node scripts/plugin-removal.mjs cell <id>` runs one cell locally on a copy of the tree. The test suites name the plugins they need and skip when one is not shipped.
- **Plugins — phase 1: the plugin SDK, and five plugins moved out of the core** (spec `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md`, 2026-09-28). Météo-France, school holidays, Google Agenda, tariff recipes and the Sowel gate access now live in their own folders (`server/src/plugins/<id>`, `client/src/plugins/<id>`) and register into the core: routes (same URLs), tables, settings, jobs, reservation events, email variables, SAS data and screen slots. Nothing changes for the operator. New on **Paramètres › Plugins**: uninstalling one of these five can **erase its data** (« Effacer aussi ses données », the list of what goes, « Désinstaller et effacer »); prices, payments and the tariff change journal are never touched — a recipe's seasons simply become « Manuelle ». The keypad code of the property now belongs to the SAS: it shows in the arrival SAS even without Sowel, and its field in Établissement follows the SAS plugin. Each plugin's screens are downloaded by the browser only when used. +37 server tests, +18 client tests, +1 E2E.
- **Four more plugins leave the core: Linge, Export comptable, Site WordPress and Arrivée et départ guidés (sas).** Nothing changes while they are installed; uninstalled, each leaves no trace on screen and none on the server any more (six server paths used to answer with their plugin off). Linge, Export comptable and Site WordPress offer « Effacer aussi ses données ». The planning and the sas become hosts: plugins add day cards, bar buttons and sas steps (`specs/plugins-phase-2-hosts.md`).
- **New page Suivi financier › « Indemnités d'annulation »**, in the core: the cancellation compensations are entered, banked, reopened and deleted there, with or without the accounting export. Comptabilité links to it.
- **Paiement en ligne (Qonto) leaves the core and becomes the first payment provider.** The core keeps what a payment means (the amount asked, the link record, the request email, the paid bucket, the devis conversion) and talks to a provider through one interface; Qonto's connection, webhook, settings page and poll live in the plugin, at the same URLs. Without a connected provider, the fiche shows no payment request button and the dashboard no « Relancer » (`specs/plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md`).
- **« Adresse du site public » moves to Paramètres › Conditions générales**: it builds the CGV link of the emails and outlives the payment plugin.
- **Assurance annulation Neat leaves the core and becomes the `neat` plugin.** The core keeps the quote and its engine; a plugin may set the insurance unit price through a small, closed interface (the quote post-processor). Neat's pricing, subscription pass, settings, tables, Intégrations card and the chip of the insurance line live in the plugin, at the same URLs (`specs/plugins-phase-3b-neat.md`).
- **Without an active Neat, the cancellation insurance is offered nowhere**: no tile on the fiche or the devis, no block on the website, no line in Paramètres › Options. A line already on a stay or a devis stays visible at its price, read-only. Reactivating Neat brings the option back with its settings.
- Uninstalling Neat with « Effacer aussi ses données » first warns about the subscriptions still active at Neat, which stay in force there.
- **Neutral seeds** (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` §3.C). A new database gets no catering catalogue, no pool season, a free baby cot, neutral guest emails and no extinguisher check. The extinguisher check becomes a setting of the SAS plugin; Lodgify counts as a direct sale through « Compté comme vente directe » on Paramètres › Plateformes; the push subject follows the company email; the unsubscribe page speaks for the company.
- **Repository**: the README and the WordPress plugin name no deployment. Solio's site moves to a private repository in a follow-up.
- Public booking form: first and last names accept letters of any script with their marks, space, hyphen, both apostrophes and dot, and nothing else; other characters are refused on the field (`422`, `reason: 'nameInvalid'`, specs/public-api.md rule 23). Narrows `/public/v1/booking-requests` on these two fields only. Platform intakes, the back office and stored clients are not judged. Plugin `website-booking` 1.8.10.
- **Bank reconciliation — linking dialog, filters and « Tout confirmer » (online-payment plugin 1.4.0, SDK 2.9.0; lot 1 of `specs/reconciliation-assisted-matching.md`).** The fiche's « Rattacher des virements » lists the credits that can pay it: the plausible ones first (settled within 15 days of an échéance and within 5 € of it, or carrying the client's name), every other available credit folded and paged — the old cut at the 40 newest credits is gone — with a server-side search by name, date (`12/03`) or amount (`596,58`). Several credits can be ticked at once; the server previews the allocation (oldest credit onto the earliest due échéance, an installment plan in its own order), a manual split is validated field by field, and « Rattacher » writes everything or nothing. The page's counters now filter the list (kept in the URL, chip + Échap to leave), and « Tout confirmer » confirms the view's proposals in one batch, each in its own transaction, « à vérifier » left out unless ticked. From a credit, « Rattacher à des fiches » ties it to several échéances. New routes under `/api/payments/reconciliation`: `reservations/:id/credits`, `allocation-preview`, `links/batch`, `confirm-all`; `candidates` and `GET /` take `transactionId`/`filter`. The fiche's plugin block no longer carries the credits. New generic client component `FilterChip` (exported by the client SDK).
- Recording a payment from the fiche is now checked by the server: no date in the future, no 0 € bucket (a converted quote carrying a 0 € acompte could be « paid »), the amount the operator saw must still be the stored one, and a caution is returned only after it was received and not before. « Enregistrer » no longer writes the caution state; only its row does. A past or ongoing fiche keeps its payment and caution actions.
- An open payment link whose amount the fiche changes is withdrawn at save (and deactivated at the provider); its row then offers « Renvoyer la demande ». If the guest pays the old link anyway, the payment is recorded on the fiche at its real amount. A link paid just before the save refuses the change.
- The live preview of the fiche now prices « Sans acompte » like the save does.
- The fiche shows the payments as stored (the same « Reste à percevoir » as Finance); the live figures appear only once the fiche is modified, never on a past one. A stay imported without a price reads « Prix à renseigner ». Receiving a platform payout records the transfer with the mark, without saving the whole fiche — past stays included — and a stay priced under an earlier rule records its payment with a warning instead of refusing it. Undoing a received acompte keeps its amount. A paid solde of a stay without acompte is no longer raised when the stay costs more: the difference is owed as the arrival complement.
- A payment already recorded can no longer be marked again (a second tab would have re-dated it and moved its accounting entry); the transfer and commission of a payout are only accepted on its reception, on a platform stay, and a commission above the billed amount is refused. « Virement reçu » is typed when the commission is unknown. A payment recorded on a stay whose amounts drifted is now split on its stored amounts in the export (never booked whole on the tourist-tax account). The sticky total bar shows the stored figures with the stored ledger; an undone payment proposes its date again when redone.
- « Encaisser en une fois » and the arrival check-in payment now split a payment whose amounts drifted on its stored amounts too, like the fiche and online payments: a solde is never booked whole on the tourist-tax account any more. The Comptabilité page warns about any entry of the month that is (« À vérifier avant l'envoi au cabinet »). On an unmodified fiche the pricing summary shows the stored figures, like the ledger and the total bar.
- On an unmodified fiche whose stored amounts differ from today's calculation, the « Résumé tarifaire » shows the stored figures and due dates only (no per-night or per-option detail), and says why; it never mixes them with the current calculation.
- **The price of a resource booking comes from the server** (specs/resource-supplements-total-server-side.md): the booking dialog of Calendrier › Ressources shows the total, the day/evening bands and the offered and billed minutes of a new preview (`POST /api/resource-bookings/preview`), computed by the very function the commit stores, so the amount shown is the amount kept. The amount follows each change of range within a moment, reads « Prix indisponible » with « Réessayer » when the server cannot be reached, and « Enregistrer » waits for a fresh price. A booking saved at another price than the one shown is announced in the dialog. The four known disagreements between the dialog and the stored price (ranges off the slot grid, fixed-price resources, bookings tied to a stay, the offered-minutes fallback) now resolve to the stored price; billing is unchanged.
- The « Planifier » step of the arrival SAS shows each resource's evening supplement as the server answers it, evening credit included (a line already sold with 10 € of evening in it now shows 10 €, not 20 €), and the hours left to place come from the server too. The page-bottom sum of the blocks is gone.
- plugin:hourly-resources The booking price and the evening supplement of the « Planifier » step come from the server.
- SDK 2.2.0: `api.previewResourceBooking` and the generic `useLatestPreview` hook (the arrival recap preview now runs on it).
- **Plugin compatibility:** every plugin manifest states the lowest GuestFlow version it needs (`>=X.Y.Z`), and the package build refuses any other form. The « below 4.0.0 » ceiling this review first set (specs/plugins-phase-4-registry.md rule 34) is superseded by rule 62 (decision D24): a plugin is judged on its SDK range and its GuestFlow floor, so a GuestFlow major no longer switches it off.
- **SDK contract:** `server/src/plugins/sdk/coreModules.json` lists what `coreModule()` exposes; a test and `check-plugin-versions` fail when a listed module or name disappears without an SDK major bump. SDK 1.2.0 (specs/plugins-phase-4-registry.md rule 35).
- **Qonto library:** moved back from the online-payment plugin to `server/src/utils/qonto/`, shared by the plugin and the control plane, which no longer imports a plugin file (specs/plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md rule 22).
- **Recette mode:** `GUESTFLOW_MAIL_OUTBOX=<dir>` outside production now catches every email, guest emails included (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 26, README › Recette).
- **Pool season moves to the property** (spec `guest-email-texts-made-simple.md` rule 34). « Piscine » and its two dates (day and month) are set on the property's Séjour tab; Réglages › Emails no longer holds them. The property's Séjour tab also asks « Ménage de fin de séjour » and « Linge de toilette » (« Selon les options » keeps today's behaviour).
- The rollback schema floor now also covers the 16 tables owned by the plugins of v3.18.0, and the documented rollback of an instance holding property documents includes moving `data/private-documents/*` back to `data/uploads/` (`specs/self-update-and-releases.md` rules 39, 44, 45).
- **The arrival SAS recap is priced by the server** (specs/sas-arrival-total-server-side.md): its lines, its total, « Total à percevoir à l'arrivée », the sale-step amounts and the « settle in one gesture » decision now come from a new preview (`POST /api/reservations/:id/sas/arrival/preview`), computed by the very function the commit runs — the figure the operator collects is the figure the books keep. The recap follows each « Offrir » within a moment, shows « Total indisponible » with « Réessayer » when the server cannot be reached, and « Valider » waits for a fresh total.
- The missing linen elements are priced from « Facturables » by the server (they were stored at the amount the browser had computed), and a complement adjusted on the fiche now shows its adjusted amount on the recap, with the computed one beside it.
- A check-in recorded on a total other than the one the recap showed (a price changed elsewhere meanwhile) is announced in the dialog and noted in the fiche's history.
- **The departure SAS recap is priced by the server** (specs/sas-departure-recap-server-side.md): the end-of-stay lines, « Sous-total arrivée », « Total à percevoir », « Rien à percevoir » and whether a settlement is asked now come from a new preview (`POST /api/reservations/:id/sas/departure/preview`), computed by the very function the check-out commit runs. The dialog sends intentions only (missing linen by item, the ménage as a yes/no, the extinguisher quantities, stored lines by a server-issued key) and renders the answer; the figure the operator collects is the figure the books keep.
- The missing linen and the end-of-stay ménage are priced by the server from « Facturables » and the property (they were stored at the amount the browser had loaded), and the lines already stored on the fiche are kept by the server instead of being sent back.
- The operator's « Ajustement » line is no longer shown as an offerable line at check-out: an end-of-stay or arrival total adjusted on the fiche shows « Montant ajusté sur la fiche (calcul : … ») under the total, and offering a recalled arrival line leaves the adjusted amount.
- A check-out recorded on a total other than the one the recap showed is announced in the dialog and noted in the fiche's history. The plugin SDK moves to 2.1.0 (additions only: `planDepartureSas`, `resolveSasDepartureItems`, `buildDepartureSeed`, the `departureSasPlan` module).
- **SDK 2.13.0**: a client created or updated by a plugin (`ctx.core.clients.findOrCreate` / `update`, the `client` of `ctx.core.reservations.update`) goes through the client dialog's validation and fails with `INVALID_CLIENT` (a 400 answer inside `reservations.update`, rolled back); the new `reuseByEmail` option refuses to attach to an existing client by e-mail, which the Gîtes de France intake sets from its sender check. `isValidEmail` now rejects commas, semicolons, angle brackets and quotes.
- **SDK 2.14.0**: the client SDK exports `Contribution` (error boundary and `Suspense` around one plugin component, the same as `<Slot>` does), and every host that mounts a plugin component itself uses it. The `sas` plugin is 1.7.3 and asks for `sdk ^2.14.0`.
- **Bounded numbers in the settings** (specs/settings-input-guards.md): the acompte delay runs from 0 to 30 days, the solde and annulation delays from 0 to 60. A minus sign is removed as it is typed, a text that is not a number brings the previous value back when the field is left, and an out-of-range value turns the field red and is refused by the server instead of being silently replaced (a negative delay used to be saved as 7). A delay of 0 reads « à régler à la réservation ». The commission and payout fields of Paramètres › Plateformes behave the same way, and the old per-platform commission endpoint now refuses a negative or excessive rate instead of clamping it. A stored delay outside the new bounds is left alone until it is edited.
- **A template without its English version is never sent to an English-speaking guest** (operator decision, specs/email-language-fr-en.md rule 9). The manual send is refused with « Version anglaise manquante pour ce modèle. », the send dialog says so and marks the template, Emails › Modèles badges it « EN manquant »; the automatic passes skip it and the history shows why. It used to leave in French with English dates and amounts. Every shipped template has its English version.
- **One amount format in every guest e-mail** (specs/email-language-fr-en.md rule 10): « 1 234,56 € » with the thousands grouped, « €1,234.56 » in English, and no « ,00 » on a whole amount — the caution reads « 500 € » like the « 45 € » of the stay texts.
- Deactivating a plugin that an active plugin requires is refused (409 `REQUIRED_BY`, « Nécessaire à … : à désactiver d’abord. »), as uninstalling already was — e.g. « Réservation depuis le site » while « Google Vacation Rentals » is active.
- Docs and gate: `check-spec-coverage --changed` fails on a rule number used twice in a spec's §3 (`spec-rule-coverage.md` rule 17); nine specs renumbered, citations repointed.
- **Pricing summary: the saved amounts look like the summary, and a platform price far from the grid is flagged** (spec `reservation-payment-ledger.md` rules 50-64). When the amounts saved on a fiche differ from today's calculation, the « Résumé tarifaire » now shows them in the usual card (options, resources, nightly detail, tourist-tax case, cascade, échéances) instead of a reduced one, with no notice about the calculation; « Actualiser tarifs » stays the one way to re-price. A single alert « Prix {plateforme} à vérifier » appears on an upcoming platform stay whose paid price is more than 15 % below or 25 % above (and at least 30 € away from) the « Prix plateformes » grid of its channel, with a « Voir la grille » link. `POST /api/reservations/calculate-price` returns `platformPriceCheck`; `storedSummary` is shaped like the quote.
- **Textes des emails: writing one sentence at a time** (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rules 41–45, UI/UX review of 2026-10-18). Variables are offered on demand — « + Variable » or the `[` key opens a list filtered as you type — instead of a wall of chips under every field. A mistyped variable is refused under its own field with the closest variable as a one-tap « Remplacer », and the preview keeps the last valid email instead of disappearing. One language at a time: FR | EN at the top, the English field with the French above it, and the server's « N à traduire » beside the switch. A click on a sentence of the preview opens its field (beside it on a computer, in a sheet on a phone); the preview is tagged inside its rolled-back transaction only, and a test guarantees that no tag reaches an email.
- **One name per email, in the order of the stay** (rule 44): « Accès et arrivée », « Préparation du séjour », « Merci et avis »… replace « J-2 », « Séquence — J-2 arrivée » on Emails › Modèles, Textes des emails, the send dialogs and the exported file. Emails › Modèles is a timeline grouped by moment; « Manuel » and « Modèle livré » are no longer repeated on every row; a shipped email can be switched off but no longer deleted (« ⋯ » holds the editor, the texts, the mode — switching to automatic asks first — and, for an email of one's own, « Supprimer »).
- **« Quand ? » in one sentence** (rule 43): « Envoyé 2 jours avant l'arrivée », edited as « Envoyé [n] jours [avant / après / le jour même de] l'arrivée », refused live beyond 90 days, with the day it would leave for the next stay. The sequence emails, whose days the sequence computes, show their moment as a fixed sentence.
- **Suivi financier, charts readable at a glance** (specs/finance-dashboard-redesign.md rules 43-46, specs/booking-pace.md rules 14-15; UI/UX review of 2026-10-18). The « faits marquants » no longer cut their text at 900 px; « meilleur mois » is chosen among the months already over, and before any the leading month is named « en tête des réservations ». « Revenu par mois » writes each month's total above its column, marks the current month and abbreviates months on a wide screen. Every chart colour is a theme token with checked contrast (« à venir » in a deeper honey, last year's bar outlined).
- **« Réservations à venir, à date » on a phone** stays a chart: the columns scroll under a fixed axis, set on the current month, and one tap (or one click) opens a month's curve with its figures in the header (no clipped tooltip on a phone). Month labels are reachable from the keyboard. The « — » row of écarts waits for a first comparable month.
- **Start of an exercise**: the cumulative curve of the banner stays, shorter, until the first month is over.
- **Second code: one field drawn as six cells, sent at the 6th digit** (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rules 32–34, UI/UX review proposal 23): the login's « Vérification » screen keeps one `one-time-code` input (autofill and paste work) under six cells, sends the code as soon as it is complete (or a whole backup code), with no « Valider » button, and says a refusal under the cells, the digits kept and selected. « Se souvenir de cet appareil (30 jours) » sits above the code. The console's operator login uses the same field and no longer sends a 5-digit code. The field takes the code out of whatever is pasted (« Votre code : 123 456 », « 123-456 », a backup code without its dash), the server reads codes the same way, and Enter on an incomplete code says why.
- **Plugins — the real state and « Configurer »** (`specs/plugins-phase-0-foundation.md` rule 25; UI/UX review 2026-10-18, proposal 19). A card reads « Actif · à connecter » while its plugin lacks something and offers « Configurer » (or « Réglages ») opening its settings place, Intégrations sections included (`#plugin-<id>`); « Désactiver » moves into a ⋯ menu behind a confirmation; a filter « À configurer (n) ». Plugin SDK 1.5.0 adds `ctx.setupStatus`; Google Agenda, Neat, Paiement en ligne and Vigilance Météo-France 1.1.0 declare it.
- **Start assistant — three questions instead of twelve plugins** (`specs/plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rule 43; proposal 13). Options payantes / site internet / encaisser en ligne: the server maps each « oui » to its plugins; a « non » switches nothing off; the full list stays in Paramètres › Plugins. `PUT /api/onboarding/plugins` now takes `{ answers }`.
- **SAS answered with the thumb** (specs/ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md rules 1-4): the main answer sits at the bottom of the screen on a phone, « Reporter au départ » is a neutral button instead of black on red, the header reads « 3 sur 9 » and never grows when a service is added, and a recap with nothing to collect says « Rien à percevoir » — decided by the server, never over a stay still owed or a deferred caution. Plugin `sas` 1.0.4.
- **Quotes list** (rules 5-9, 21): cards below 1200 px, amounts never wrap, one « ⋯ » menu per row (the PDF stays visible beside it on a large screen), « Logement · dates » on one line on a phone, no « Origine inconnue » badge. The amount comes from the server (`totalTtc`).
- **Reservation fiche under 1200 px** (rules 10-12): a total bar pinned to the bottom of the screen — « Vous percevez » for a platform stay, « Total du séjour » for a direct one, figures from the server (`headline` in every quote) — opens the full summary in a sheet; the action bar names the guest, the number and the dates.
- **Calendar** (rules 13-16): a closure hatches its days instead of drawing a bar on every week, stay bars are a soft tint of the platform colour with ink text, the legend is folded behind « Légende » with real names (« Gîtes de France »), and the property filter is a full-width « Tous | logements » control on a phone.
- **Quote PDF** (rules 17-20): fir green, ink and the app's serif instead of the navy band; every offered line in one « Offert avec le séjour » row; the total and the schedule on page 1; no « (expiré) » on the guest's document. On the fiche, an expired quote still open shows « Périmé · prolonger »: a confirmation first announces the new validity, total and schedule, then the quote is re-issued at today's tariffs; a converted quote can no longer be saved or re-dated. In the list, a stale quote reads « Périmé » and its PDF asks before downloading.
- **Home: the day first** (`specs/shell-and-home-day-first.md`, UI/UX review 2026-10-18 proposals 1, 14, 15): departures and arrivals come right under the date; the gate keys and the payment deadlines stay unfolded above them; every other alert waits in one « À traiter (n) » box, counted and ordered by the server (`GET /api/dashboard/attention`), each row unfolding in place. The three KPI tiles left the home — their figures are in Suivi financier.
- **One alert component** (`AttentionCard`) draws every dashboard subject, core and plugins (SDK 1.4.0: `ctx.attention`, client `AttentionCard`); AA text tokens `success.text` / `warning.text` for status chips; the non-existent `info.lighter` / `error.lighter` tokens are gone.
- **Shell**: on a phone the top bar shows the page title (the wordmark stays on the home); the installed version moved to the foot of the drawer; the drawer is 256 px with wrapping labels, keyboard-reachable chevrons with `aria-expanded`, and a named menu button.
- Home attention, after review: a subject the server cannot read is named (« 1 sujet indisponible ») and logged, unreadable gate keys stay pinned (« Lecture des clés impossible »); the payment deadlines are pinned only while a line is truly late, and a missing platform amount reads « montant à saisir »; a plugin package built for an older SDK keeps its own home alert.
- **Website booking engine — WordPress plugin 1.16.0** (UI/UX review 2026-10-18, decisions 2, 3, 4 and 16; specs/wp-booking-widget-redesign.md rules 29–33, specs/wordpress-plugin.md rules 28–29):
  - The chosen stay always reads as selected: hover styles only for a mouse, so a tapped departure no longer stays washed out on a phone — even under the legacy `gf-booking.php` still on the Solio Pi; past days, taken nights and free nights look different, with a legend. When the two months on screen hold no bookable arrival (taken nights, minimum stay, arrival days), « Prochaine disponibilité : mercredi 31 mars 2027 » offers to jump there.
  - Refusals land on the field: every missing or malformed contact field is marked under itself at once, the first one gets the focus, in the page's language; the server's refusals are mapped to the same fields. The engine announces each refusal as a `gf:refused` event so the Solio drawer can show the screen that holds the field (repo copy of the mu-plugin updated, not deployed).
  - On a phone (coarse pointer or narrow viewport, block under 900 px), the total and the button sit in a bar at the bottom; the button follows the step (« Choisir les dates », « Continuer », then the submit). The bar holds even in a theme that clips the page's overflow. On a wide block the summary follows the scroll in a right-hand column with its own button. The two times get a row of their own.
  - One accent colour drives the whole engine (the site's, else the theme's primary, else Maison fir green), darkened where it carries white text so it reaches 4.5 : 1; the stale MUI blue default and the calendar block's blue days are gone, and the site's own `.gf-btn` / `.gf-section` / `.gf-cta` are no longer touched.
- The VAT rate (Paramètres › TVA & exercice) is now the rate of the regime period in force: changing it recalculates the reservations of that period, not those of an earlier one. With a single period (every installation before its first regime change) nothing moves, to the cent.
- plugin:weather-alerts Nécessite Arrivée et départ guidés.

## Fixed
- **Security: turning the validation in two steps off, new backup codes and a change of app now ask the password AND a current code, and the confirmation password is limited** (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rules 47, 49): until now the password asked in « Mon compte » had no attempt limit, so a stolen session could guess it through `/api/auth/2fa/start|disable|backup-codes`. Five wrong passwords within 15 minutes now lock the three routes for 15 minutes (429 `REAUTH_LOCKED`, written to the account history), and removing the protection or minting backup codes needs a current code (app, backup or emailed) on top of the password; a wrong code counts toward the existing 5-code lock. The password change (`POST /api/auth/change-password`) is behind the same limit, and a successful password reset by link clears it. A switch to the email code during a first activation needs the enrolment's random token (not just the session cookie) and is never accepted on a change of app. Code mails are limited atomically (60 s between two, 6 an hour, 5 per activation), the emailed re-authentication code no longer cancels a login code, and « Renvoyer le code » at login waits 60 s and has its own hourly budget.
- **iCal — a cancelled stay no longer hands its fiche to a new booking on nameless feeds** (audit 2026-10-27, `ical-summary-fallback-cross-uid.md` rule 7). The same-summary re-claim is skipped when the summary names nobody (Airbnb « Reserved », Gîtes de France « BOOKED »): the new stay is created and the old one goes to the cancellation approval. A locked fiche no longer swallows the new booking behind a misleading date-drift alert.
- **iCal — two platforms' nameless stays on the same dates are no longer merged** (`ical-import-hardening.md` rule 15): both are created and the second is flagged in conflict.
- **iCal — the Booking-style « CLOSED - Not available » feed is a setting of the source** (`lodgify-decommission.md` rule 16): `booking-com`, `BookingTente` and any other spelling keep their stays; switch in the URL editor of « Plateformes & iCal ». Migration: new nullable column `ical_sources.feedMarksStaysClosed`, one-shot `ical_feed_marks_stays_closed_v1` ticks every existing « booking… » source.
- **iCal — the closure guard only drops the closure's own echo** (`lodgify-decommission.md` rule 17): a new booking partly over a closure is created and flagged, a stay moved onto a closure follows its feed (flagged) or raises its date-drift alert when locked.
- **iCal — a stay already cancelled in GuestFlow is never proposed for deletion** (`ical-cancellation-approval.md` rule 13): no alert when its feed drops it, and approving an older alert keeps the row and its refunds (`outcome = 'already_cancelled'`).
- **Platforms — saving an iCal source keeps its platform's spelling** (`normalize-platform-names.md` rules 22-23): `GitesDeFrance` no longer becomes `Gitesdefrance` (second catalogue row at 0 % commission, GdF intake at 0 %). The boot de-duplication now groups by slug and merges such pairs into the most-referenced row (never renaming an established spelling), settings and references — cancellation compensations included.
- **iCal parser** (`ical-import-hardening.md` rule 16): `BEGIN:VEVENT` with trailing blanks, `DURATION` without `DTEND`, and a named stay whose description says « non disponible » / « blocked » are now read correctly. The export format (`DTSTART:YYYYMMDD`) is deliberately unchanged.
- plugin:accounting-export Declares SDK `^2.5.0`: it calls `coreModule('installmentPlan')`, which SDK 2.5.0 added (#794) while the manifest still accepted any 2.x. A new test refuses any plugin whose `coreModule()` modules or dated names are newer than the floor of its `sdk` range (`server/src/plugins/sdk/coreModuleHistory.json`).
- plugin:gites-de-france-intake A mail for a gîte number no property is tied to yet waits `pending` (with its reason) and is applied once the gîte is mapped, instead of ending in a final error no pass ever retried.
- plugin:airbnb-intake A stay that already started is refused before the guest's client is created (no orphan client); one intake that throws no longer aborts the whole pass; the withheld tourist tax follows the fiche's own platform row, not a hard-coded `airbnb` key.
- **Mid-stay note — amounts computed by the server** (spec `mid-stay-notes.md` rule 22). The « Nouvelle note » dialog sends the ticked keys and the remainder it opened on; `POST /api/reservations/mid-stay-note/preview` returns its items and total, and the settlement derives the amounts from the stored remainder.
- **Start assistant — no implicit login change** (spec `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 37): saving « Entreprise » replaces the bootstrap login only when « Utiliser aussi cette adresse pour se connecter » is ticked (off by default). Once onboarding is done, `/demarrage` goes home unless reopened from Réglages › Système (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rule 46).
- **Dashboard — no « Lits : D:0 / S:0 / B:0 »** for a stay without bed counts (spec `bed-config-in-linen-card.md` rule 11).
- Dead code removed: `components/PageHeader.jsx`, `isKnownPlatformKey` in `constants/platforms.js`, 16 unused `api.js` methods; the `mockReservationForm` test fixture moved under `__tests__/`. Six Implemented specs whose files moved into plugins carry the current file map.
- A full payment link (admin, website and the e-mail announcing it) now charges exactly what its payment records: the acompte plus the solde, tourist tax included and arrival-complement lines excluded, with the tax on its own 0 % VAT line. It used to charge `finalPrice` (tax left out, complement lines kept in) while ticking the whole solde paid (specs/deposit-blocks-the-dates.md rule 18, specs/payment-links-vat.md rule 10).
- Stays paid through an installment plan count at their full value in Finance (revenue, per property and channel, booking pace, projection): the plan's collected and still-due totals join the « total de séjour », and « Encaissé » lists one row per collected installment (specs/direct-installments.md rule 45).
- The « Reste » of a stay (`remainingDue`) is the unpaid buckets, no longer `finalPrice − paid buckets` (specs/finance-operational-remaining-to-pay.md rule 7).
- Cancelling a stay paid through an installment plan books the requalification of what was collected: an avoir (stay at the sales VAT rate, tourist-tax shares at 0 %) and a VAT-free indemnity (specs/direct-installments.md rule 46).
- plugin:online-payment The bank reconciliation no longer inflates a platform's commission by the acompte the platform took: the solde credit is expected and written as the solde, the fiche payout being credit + acompte net (specs/qonto-bank-reconciliation.md rule 44).
- plugin:online-payment The installments are read through the core installments model (SDK 2.6.0); a failing read surfaces instead of expecting nothing; the manifest changelog is in version order (rule 45).
- plugin:online-payment The bank reconciliation matches a payout to its platform's stays whatever the stored spelling of the platform name (specs/qonto-bank-reconciliation.md rule 46).
- **Options with free units keep their value over every save** (specs/tariff-recipes/spec.md rule 56): a welcome-pack option (« Jus 6 € × 3, 1 offert ») no longer loses value at each save (12 → 8 → 5,34 → 3,56).
- **« Total perçu / Vous percevez » counts a rise after payment once** (specs/fiche-total-sejour-net-of-commission.md rule 11): solde 600 paid, stay raised to 700 now reads 700, not 800.
- **A platform with an acompte follows its statement once both échéances are paid** (specs/reservation-refunds.md rule 40): the solde follows the corrected brut and no complement is asked at arrival.
- **The sold night prices are kept only while the arrival date is unchanged** (specs/tariff-recipes/spec.md rule 57): moving the arrival reprices the stay at the current tariff, so a progressive tariff keeps its rank logic; extending or shortening the departure keeps the sold nights.
- **A platform « Compté comme vente directe » is a direct sale everywhere** (specs/plugins-phase-p-productisation.md rule 45): acompte and solde like direct for new stays (an existing stay keeps its stored schedule), extras not routed to the complement by default, the platform's own tourist-tax mode, one `saleChannel` helper for every money path, and the fiche reads the server's answer.
- **The per-night extra-guest supplement of a sold reservation no longer follows the live season price** (specs/tariff-recipes/spec.md rule 58).
- **An extra added on an arrival complement already collected is billed at check-out** (specs/mid-stay-extras-to-end-of-stay-complement.md rule 20), so the buckets always add up to the stay.
- **The live « Total HT / TVA » includes the extra-guest supplement**, as the stored summary and the export do (specs/single-vat-rate.md rule 13).
- **The fiche's headline is the same live and stored** (specs/ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md rules 31-32): the platform-handled tax estimate is no longer added to « Total du séjour », a caisse-interne complement no longer makes the stored headline differ, and the summary's closing cascade is served by the server.
- **A night no season covers is named** (`unpricedNights`, specs/tariff-recipes/spec.md rule 59): the fiche warns, and `hasUnpricedNights` lets the public flows refuse it.
- **The devis PDF's discounted season rows add up to the engine's total** (specs/devis-pdf-total-parity.md rule 13): no more 89,26 printed for 89,25.
- Home: the departures and arrivals lists switch to cards whenever their column cannot hold the table, so Note, Paiements and Caution are never hidden in a scroller at 900 or 1280 px; the month calendar shows Sunday at 900 px.
- Planning cards: the ARRIVÉE / DÉPART pills and the « Linge de lit non pris », « Caution à percevoir » and « Complément » chips use the soft status colours instead of filled chips.
- Tariff page: one month at a time on a phone, the property name as a caption beside « Gestion tarifaire », and a seasons table that fits at 1280 px.
- « Prix plateformes »: French amounts (« 1 075 € », « 15,5 % »), « Direct » capitalised, prices that never wrap, and the channel column kept in view while scrolling.
- Finance highlights write thousands like the tiles (« 1 300 € »).
- Reservation form: the children / teens / babies age ranges are helper texts, no longer cut; an option's switch and « Ajout automatique » sit on one row on a phone.
- Plugins page: the version stays on the name's line, long statuses wrap, and the guided arrival/departure card opens its « Facturables au SAS » settings.
- Planning on a phone: no empty bar around the laundry button, which now sits beside the date.
- Start assistant: even gaps between fields.
- Touch screens: short text buttons are 44 px wide, and the fiche's client chip 44 px high.
- plugin:tariff-recipes The recipe card prints its degressivity in French with one decimal; the recipes page lines up with the other pages.
- plugin:sas Departure: « Les serviettes et les draps sont-ils tous là ? » keeps « Oui » on the same side as every other question.
- **Website booking — stay rules** (audit 2026-10-27, `public-api.md` rule 12): the public booking request refuses a stay longer than the season's maximum (`409 MAX_NIGHTS`) or off its changeover days (`409 CHANGEOVER`), as the back-office does, with no override. `POST /quote` exposes `maxNights`, `maxNightsBreached`, `changeoverBreached` and `stayRefusal` (the sentence to print, FR/EN); the calendar's arrival windows only offer arrivals a stay can satisfy; `POST /booking-requests/:id/pay` re-checks the three rules on the saved devis. A night the engine reports as unpriced (`unpricedNights`) is unavailable on the site.
- **Website booking — availability re-check** (rule 15): `computeBlockedDates` reads reservations one day wider on each side, so a neighbour's late check-out or early check-in is seen by the exact-window checks of the quote, the request, the payment and the Google feed.
- **Website booking — options** (rule 14): the quote and the booking request accept only the options the public catalogue lists (no internal-only option, offered default or negative price); the admin API refuses a negative option price (« Le prix d’une option ne peut pas être négatif. »). The same option or resource twice in a request is a 422 instead of a 500 (rule 16).
- **Website booking — existing client** (rule 13): a request whose e-mail matches a client no longer changes that client's language; the typed identity, when it differs, is appended to the devis notes (« Client existant reconnu par l'e-mail — identité saisie : … »). Supersedes `site-english-version.md` rule 12.
- **WordPress plugin 1.17.1**: the drawer prints GuestFlow's `stayRefusal` and disables the request; the updater requires the archive name to carry the manifest version (newer than the installed one) and checks the archive's SHA-256 against the release's `SHA256SUMS` before WordPress installs it.
- plugin:website-booking Maximum stay and changeover days enforced on the public path, public catalogue options only, existing clients left untouched, neighbour edges in the availability re-check, duplicate lines refused.
- plugin:google-vacation-rentals Every party size from 1 is priced (a small party was sent the included-guests price when a per-person default option existed).
- Tests: the website-booking suites whose mocks let the SDK open `database.js` now use a temporary database instead of the checkout's `guestflow.db`.
- **Own email change asks the current password** (and a current second-step code when it is on); the old address receives a notice. « Mon compte » opens the confirmation dialog only when the email changed. Spec `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 59 (audit M1).
- **Login timing no longer reveals whether an account exists** (dummy hash for unknown, inactive or support accounts, rule 61, audit L1), and **the email 2FA login code is rate limited** like every code mail (rule 62, audit L3).
- **Support cannot redirect notifications through the company email** (the resolved recipient is guarded on `PUT /settings` and `PUT /onboarding/company`), **the start assistant asks the password to adopt the login email**, **other sessions end after an email change**, and **a refused login code mail says when to retry** (rules 18, 59, 62).
- **Support journal keeps writes whose client disconnected**: logged on `close` as « Interrompu », never twice (rule 60, audit M2).
- **A support session can no longer change the keypad code, the notification recipient or the IBAN/BIC printed on devis**; a value sent back unchanged still passes. Rule 18 amended (audit M3).
- **A Booking event wholly over held nights is created and flagged** (spec `lodgify-decommission.md` rules 6 and 18). It was skipped as an « echo » with no row and no alert, which hid a Booking stay sold on nights GuestFlow already held. The `echoSkipped` counter and its « écho(s) ignoré(s) » wording are gone.
- Bed counts: an explicit 0 (single, double or baby beds) is kept when a reservation is saved instead of being sent as empty.
- The back button ignores a `?from=` value that is not an in-app path (URL, `//…`, `javascript:`) and goes to the default page instead of failing.
- A reservation link that points to nothing now shows "Réservation introuvable." with a way back, instead of an error with a "Réessayer" button that could never work.
- Settings pages (Platforms, number fields, attention cards, two-factor setup) no longer scroll sideways at desktop width: the screen-reader-only text boxes are 1px.
- **A new stay's own night blocks count against closures** (spec `establishment-closures.md` rule 17). A check-out at 17:00 or later the day a closure starts (or a check-in at 10:00 or earlier the day it ends) is now refused.
- **Converting a devis checks the dates** (spec `devis-accept-to-reservation.md` rule 9). Accepting a quote over nights another channel sold meanwhile is refused (409 `DATES_UNAVAILABLE`) instead of silently creating a double booking; the paid-link path still converts and flags the conflict.
- **A duplicate is set aside only when its nights sit inside its keeper's** (`calendar-property-parity.md` rule 26): a hidden copy one night longer than the visible stay used to be set aside and freed that night for sale; it is now flagged as a date conflict instead.
- **The fiche no longer overwrites a background change** (spec `reservation-fiche-concurrency.md`). Saving a fiche opened before an import, a mail intake or another tab changed the stay is refused with « Fiche modifiée entre-temps » and a choice to reload or keep the draft.
- The trailing-punctuation trim of a link in the guest e-mail HTML is linear (10 000 closing parentheses after a link used to cost ~0.5 s, 40 000 about 9 s).
- `release.sh` builds the server part of the archive from tracked files only and refuses a staging tree that carries a secret or a runtime file (`.env.local`, databases, `licence.jws`, keys, uploads), so a manual release can no longer ship a developer's secrets (specs/self-update-and-releases.md rule 42).
- `npm test` gives every server test process its own throw-away database and makes opening the checkout's `server/guestflow.db` an error; three tests that used to migrate it now set their own (specs/server-test-isolation.md).
- **Approving an iCal date drift is guarded** (spec `ical-sync-override-locked-dates.md` rule 11). The new nights are checked against other stays and closures (a clash is flagged and notified), the payout deadline of a platform stay follows the new departure, and a stay that is no longer a live reservation is refused.
- **The iCal export publishes early check-in / late check-out nights** (spec `ical-export-closures.md` rule 12). A check-out at 17:00 or later now blocks the next night in the feed, as GuestFlow's own availability check does. Text values are escaped and lines folded at 75 octets per RFC 5545 (rule 13).
- **iCal feed import is bounded and public-only** (spec `ical-import-hardening.md` rule 17). Feeds over 5 MB are refused; loopback, private and link-local addresses (literal, resolved or reached by redirect) are refused unless `GUESTFLOW_ICAL_ALLOW_PRIVATE=1` is set for a dev box.
- plugin:accounting-export The journal no longer books an unexplained residue on the VAT account: per-line contribs are used only when they add up to the entry in both directions (spec `accounting-books-the-money-collected.md` rule 15, audit 2026-10-28; réservation #13 of October 2026: VAT 265,38 → 82,47 €, #22298 of September 2026: 37,13 → 19,47 €). A month already imported by the accountant needs a correcting entry on their side.
- **A save no longer rewrites a stay that was not edited** (spec `reservation-save-keeps-pricing.md`, audit 2026-10-28). « Enregistrer » on an untouched fiche kept re-pricing it with today's engine: paid stays moved by a few euros, snapshots and custom option ids were rewritten, the history blamed the operator. Now the money, the lines and the snapshots stay put unless a pricing input changed; Enregistrer is disabled on a clean form.
- **One way to price a stored stay.** Installment plans, restamped buckets, the trop-perçu baseline, the contrib capture and the tourist-tax declaration replay the stay exactly as its fiche saves it (tariff snapshot, deposit opt-out and overrides, commissions, platform settings, mid-stay state).
- **Money collected above the stay total is reported.** A collected arrival complement left above what the stay now asks (a line moved out of it) is a trop-perçu, not an anonymous « Complément d'arrivée » line.
- **The fiche warns** about a platform commission far above the channel's rate and about a tourist tax stored differently from what is now computed (« Recalculer »).
- **A custom line renamed alone saves its text only** (Adrien, 2026-10-29): same line, new name, one history row, nothing re-priced; an amount or any other pricing input still re-prices. Inside the extras window of a started fiche a rename is refused.
- **Trop-perçu on a locked fiche.** A collected arrival complement left above the stay total (a line or a tax moved out of it) shows as « Trop-perçu » with « Rembourser » on a past fiche too, measured against the stored figures; the refund is booked on the poste it came from (prestations, tourist tax) and defaults to the caisse interne when the complement was taken there. Direct channels only: a platform stay changes nothing (rule 38).
- **An adjusted complement is no longer read as an edit:** an untouched save of a past stay with an adjusted complement (#22275) was refused as « passée ou en cours ».
- **Rollback to the previous release keeps payment links working** (specs/self-update-and-releases.md rule 39): the provider-neutral rename of `payment_links.qontoPaymentLinkId` / `qontoPaymentId` no longer drops the old names. Both pairs now exist and stay equal (triggers, plus a copy of what the previous release wrote at boot), and a test fails if any column of the v3.18.0 schema disappears. Migration: additive columns only, no data touched.
- Managed spaces: a removal order from the console uninstalls the module without erasing its data, and a module whose data count cannot be read is reported as unknown instead of empty.
- Plugins: role allowlist entries and webhook exemptions now apply only while the plugin is live, and role entries only on the plugin's own routes.
- Plugins: installing a plugin again after erasing its data no longer leaves it active without its tables, a plugin whose live start failed once now starts or fails cleanly on the next install, and a failed erasure reports what was really erased.
- Plugins: installing a plugin on top of an installed but deactivated requirement is refused, like activating it.
- Plugins: one failing plugin contribution no longer takes the whole page down; an outdated plugin bundle shows a "Recharger" prompt.
- Plugins: activating a plugin after a partial erasure recreates its tables first, and a plugin that failed at boot starts again when installed at the same version.
- Plugins: an install that needed a restart now runs its install and activation hooks at the next boot.
- **Security: property documents are no longer reachable without signing in** (`settings-rationalization.md` rule 32): they are stored in `private-documents`, outside the public `/uploads` tree, and downloaded through an authenticated route; files uploaded earlier are moved at boot, and « Retirer » deletes the file. Photos, logo and favicon stay public.
- **A website quote can no longer be paid at a stale price** (spec `public-online-payment.md` rules 14-15, audit 2026-10-28). Re-pricing a devis (operator save or the guest's request sent again) now retires its open payment links and deactivates them at the provider. A link paid for another amount than the quote asks (re-priced quote, or a full link minted before the tourist tax joined the amount) books the amount really received, leaves the shortfall due and alerts the admin instead of ticking the stay fully paid. Two concurrent `pay` calls mint one link; a second paid link of the same devis is a refund alert, not a second booking. The retirement leaves a history row, the save answers `retiredPaymentLinks`, a link paid before its retirement is converted and booked, and a stale link paid after a manual tick is no longer reported as a double payment.
- plugin:website-booking **Contact fields of a website request are capped** (spec `public-api.md` rule 20, audit 2026-10-28): first/last name 100 characters, phone 40, e-mail 254, refused with a field error; the WordPress block sets the matching `maxlength`.
- plugin:booking-intake The amount scan of a Booking page line is bounded (an 80 KB line used to cost ~15 s).
- Closures dialog: a slow « conflicts » answer that came back after the dialog was closed and reopened, or after its dates were made invalid, no longer paints the old closure's red line over the dialog.
- **Console — a late « Réessayer » on the closing mail never promises a date past the erasure, and its failure no longer reopens the closing** (audit R10 N1, N2, specs `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rules 41–42 and `control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rule 46). After day 60 of the 90, the fresh export's link was valid 30 days and the mail promised a « jusqu’au » date up to 29 days after the erasure deleted the archive; it now expires the day before the erasure, and from the erasure day « Réessayer » is no longer offered (a stale click mails nothing and says why). A failed fresh export turned « Export complet » red with a « Réessayer » that always answered « Client déjà archivé. » and journaled « Déprovisionnement interrompu »; the failure and its reason are now on the mail step only. +7 console tests.
- **A devis can no longer be moved onto closed nights** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 55, audit R10-1, 2026-10-31). A devis holding an open payment link could be re-dated onto an establishment closure; its link stayed payable and a payment confirmed the guest on closed nights. Creating a devis, or changing its dates or logement, onto nights a closure covers is now refused (409 « Logement fermé sur ces dates : devis non enregistré. », with the closure to open in the dialog); a devis already there keeps its other edits. And should a devis link still be paid for closed nights, the stay is booked without the guest confirmation and the admin is told « Paiement reçu sur des dates fermées … déplacer le séjour ou le rembourser ». +9 server tests, +1 client test.
- **Payments: beside an installment plan, only an échéance can be paid, and a payment received after a link was withdrawn is never hidden** (specs/payment-link-lifecycle.md rule 63, audit R10-M1): such a payment blocks every new request until it is settled (rule 56), but once a plan existed the fiche no longer showed « Marquer remboursé » / « Enregistrer sur le séjour », and the only way out was to delete the plan. The ledger now lists those payments on a row of their own (« Paiement après retrait », « À traiter »). A plan can no longer be made or resized while an acompte / solde / séjour link may still be paid: a payment waiting to be settled (409 `UNSETTLED_WITHDRAWN_PAYMENT`) a deactivation not yet confirmed by the provider (409 `LINK_DEACTIVATION_PENDING`), or a lapsed link's last check still owed (409 `LINK_LAST_LOOK_PENDING`). The échéancier card gives the reason and the fiche re-reads its links after such a refusal. The stay is checked again after the provider answered, and the plan is made under the stay's payment-link lock, so a link minted at the same moment never lands beside it. An échéance paid after its withdrawal is recorded on that échéance; an acompte / solde / séjour payment beside a plan can only be marked refunded (`INSTALLMENT_PLAN_IN_PLACE`), so it is never booked on top of the échéances.
- **Client tests — no more red CI on a busy runner** (audit R10 M-1, `specs/server-test-isolation.md` rule 11). `VatRegimeSwitchDialog.switch.test.jsx` asserted that `onStale` was called right after the notice appeared, while the dialog calls it from an effect that React runs in a later task: on a loaded GitHub runner the assertion ran first and turned the inte/plugins head red. That test and 20 others in 15 files with the same race (a value, row, button state or flag that lands after the awaited element) now await what they assert. `cd client && npm run test:slow-scheduler` replays a busy runner by delaying React's scheduler (`SCHED_DELAY`, 8 ms by default); the whole suite passes under it. Tests only, no behaviour change.
- **Client tests: « nothing shown » checks now wait for the answer that could show it** (specs/server-test-isolation.md rule 11, audit round 10): 29 absence checks in 24 client test files asserted right after `waitFor(api called)`, before the API answer had rendered, so they passed even when the component did show the thing once the answer landed. Each now awaits a `findBy*` on something the same answer renders or, when the answer renders nothing, settles that answer inside `act`; each was proven by a mock answer carrying the forbidden content, which now fails the test under the normal and the slowed scheduler. Tests only, no product change.
- **« Dates prises » and « Ouvrir » no longer drop a draft** (audit R11, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 39, 40,
  41, 46, 48, 49, readings of 2026-11-01). The « Dates prises » chip of the fiche's action bar leaves through the unsaved-changes
  guard, like a sidebar link: a dirty devis or reservation asks before it is left instead of reloading the form over
  the draft. An in-place « Ouvrir » inside an app dialog (the iCal drift approval, the « Devis non enregistré »
  refusal on a phone) closes that dialog first, as « Annuler » would, and goes through the same guard, so « Approuver
  quand même » is never left over another page. A save that keeps the fiche open shows the fresh marker: a devis moved
  onto free nights loses « Dates prises » at once. On a phone, « Ouvrir » in the closure dialog asks before a
  closure being typed is left (rules 40, 41, 49). +15 client tests.
- **Console — an export link never loses its archive to another link's purge, and a retry crossing midnight on the eve of the erasure mails nothing** (audit R11 N2 and note, spec `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rules 43–44). Archives were named per customer and per day, so closing, reactivating and closing again the same day reused the old link's file name, and the next day's purge of the old link deleted the live archive while the console showed the closing as done. Each export now has its own file, and neither the purge nor a reactivation or an erasure deletes a file that a valid link still points at. A « Réessayer » on the last day before the erasure whose export ended after midnight mailed a link already expired; it now sends nothing, keeps no trace of that export, and says the erasure is scheduled. The download keeps its plain `<slug>-<day>.tar.gz` name. +5 console tests.
- **« Transformer en devis » refused on held or closed nights** (`specs/payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 46, reading of 2026-11-01, audit R11-2). `POST /api/devis/from-reservation/:id` now applies the checks of a devis creation: 409 `DATES_HELD` when the stay's nights are held (by the stay itself — a platform stay, a direct stay with money — or by another one) and 409 `CLOSED_DATES` when a closure covers them. The devis was otherwise born on held nights, marked « Dates prises » by its own source and unpayable. When the stay itself blocks it (its nights closed, or held by itself), the fiche disables « Transformer en devis » with the reason as tooltip (« Séjour déjà payé : devis non créé. »), carried by `GET /api/reservations/:id` as `convertToDevisLockedReason`; otherwise the refusal shows in a « Devis non créé » dialog with the other stays or the closure to open, never the fiche itself.
- **Public payment: visitor wording when the nights are lost during the provider round trip** (rule 54, reading of 2026-11-01, audit R11 public L-1). Nights taken or closed while the provider answered are refused 409 `DATES_UNAVAILABLE` « These dates are no longer available. » in the visitor's language, instead of the operator sentence « … aucun lien créé ».
- **Rollback safety of « Dates plus disponibles »** (`specs/self-update-and-releases.md` rule 43, reading of 2026-11-01, audit R11 console N1). The template is seeded on the `datesTaken` anchor, so a rollback to v3.19.0 never offers it for every stay on its arrival day; a generic test now guards every default template the rollback floor does not know against the anchors its queue reads.
- **Payment links — round-11 audit fixes** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 32, 33, 58, 63, 64, readings of
  2026-11-01; `direct-installments.md` rule 50). An installment plan is no longer created or resized beside an
  acompte / solde / séjour link withdrawn for taken dates whose last payment read is still owed; should such a payment
  still land beside a plan, it is never booked on the deposit or balance: it stays on the link as a payment to settle,
  refund only while the plan is there, and the admin is alerted. A new acompte / solde / séjour request reads that link
  first (paid → booked and refused, in flight → refused 409 `LINK_PAYMENT_IN_FLIGHT`). The guest notice fails closed on
  a provider answer without a boolean `pending` (online-payment 1.5.0). A paid installment now withdraws the other
  requests' links on the stay's nights at once, and so does a payment held beside a plan. A new request also reads a
  taken-dates link whose deactivation is still owed, and every such link owing its read. The « dates déjà prises »
  alert is sent only while the dates are taken; « Enregistrer sur le séjour » on such a link says « lien retiré : dates
  prises ». +33 server tests.
- **The plugin payment-provider contract documents `pending`, and the self-update tests no longer read the shell
  environment** (audit R12, spec `plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 4, reading of 2026-11-01). The rule 4
  table now gives `getPayment(linkId)` as `{ paid, pending, paymentId?, paidAt? }`: `pending` is a required boolean
  for the read after a `dates-taken` deactivation, and an answer without it counts as in flight
  (`payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 64). A new test keeps that table and `utils/paymentProviders.js` on the same
  fields, `?` marks included, so `pending` cannot turn optional unnoticed. The `runVersionCheck` tests pass an explicit environment, so a shell carrying `GUESTFLOW_DISABLE_OUTBOUND=1`
  or `GUESTFLOW_MANAGED=1` no longer turns them red or, for the offline case, vacuously green; `buildVersionInfo`
  hands its environment to `selfUpdateSupported` as well, pinned by a `GUESTFLOW_SELF_UPDATE=off` case
  (`self-update-and-releases.md` rule 51, reading of 2026-11-01). +3 server tests.
- **« Transformer en devis » no longer drops unsaved changes** (audit R12-C1, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 46,
  reading of 2026-11-01). The devis is built from the stored stay, so converting a fiche with typed changes lost them
  and carried the old dates or party. While the fiche has unsaved changes the action is disabled with « Enregistrer
  d'abord les modifications. » (the tooltip on a wider screen, the reason beneath the item in the phone's « Plus
  d'actions » menu). This reason and the stay's own lock keep the action focusable, so Tab reaches the reason; a
  click does nothing. +4 client tests.
- **The server suite no longer follows the GuestFlow flags of the developer's shell** (spec
  `server-test-isolation.md` rule 12, review of PR #919). The `npm test` preload deletes every `GUESTFLOW_*`
  variable but the runner's `GUESTFLOW_TEST_*` knobs at start-up; tests that need a flag set it inline or pass
  an `env`. A shell exporting `GUESTFLOW_MANAGED=1` used to fail 50 tests; `GUESTFLOW_DISABLE_OUTBOUND=1` and
  `GUESTFLOW_SELF_UPDATE=off` could change outcomes too. The E2E servers, which set `GUESTFLOW_DISABLE_OUTBOUND`
  on purpose, never load the preload. +4 server tests.
- **Cancelling a stay paid through an installment plan reverses each installment bucket by bucket, to the cent** (specs `direct-installments.md` rule 46 reading of 2026-11-01, `reservation-refunds.md` rules 45–47, audit R13-M1). The retained avoir writes one line per paid installment and bucket (« Hébergement — échéance 1/3 », « Options — échéance 1/3 », …, plus one tourist-tax line) from each installment's frozen split — or, for an installment paid before the split was frozen, the stored-money split the export spread it with — instead of the whole stay part on « Location gîte ». The export splits a retained avoir HT / VAT line by line, as each encaissement was split, so the cancellation month nets to 0,00 € on every 706 / 4457 / 4671 account (stay #22326's shape: 70600000, 70600010, 44571100 and 46710000 at 0); acompte avoirs, one line per bucket, are booked byte for byte as before. The refund register no longer offers the options and resources the cancellation already requalified, spreads a bucket amount by largest remainder so no cent is offered twice, and the refund dialog captions that money « requalifié » rather than « déjà remboursé ». Export comptable plugin 1.0.21. +9 server tests, +3 client tests.
- **The server suite no longer follows the app variables of the developer's shell either** (spec
  `server-test-isolation.md` rule 12, reading of 2026-11-01, audit R13 L-1). Besides `GUESTFLOW_*`, the `npm test`
  preload now deletes every other variable the server reads (`PUBLIC_SITE_ORIGIN`, the `*_RATELIMIT_*` knobs,
  `QONTO_*`, `VAPID_*`, `TLS_*`, `PORT`…), and a `NODE_ENV` other than `test`; `PATH`, `TZ` and the runner's
  `GUESTFLOW_TEST_*` knobs are kept. The list lives in `server/src/tests/setup/appEnvNames.js`, and a guard test
  derives the app's reads from `server/src/` so a new `process.env.X` missing from it fails; it also reads
  `env?.X` and lowercase names (pm2's `pm_id` is now stripped), and fails on any access it cannot read a name off
  (an alias of `process.env`, a computed or backtick key, `Reflect.get`…) outside the few files pinned by count. A
  shell exporting the production `PUBLIC_SITE_ORIGIN` or `PUBLIC_API_RATELIMIT_MAX=5` used to fail 4 tests. +8
  server tests.
- **Refunds on a cancelled stay reduce the indemnity (audit R14-M1).** A cancelled stay now offers to refund only
  the money it actually collected, never the unpaid remainder; the share beyond any non-requalified money (a paid
  solde keeps its 706 + VAT refund) is booked as a debit of the cancellation indemnity account (75880000, 0 %), so
