  « Location gîte » and VAT stay exact. A refund above what was collected, or an indemnity share in caisse interne,
  is refused. The indemnity share is split HT / VAT cumulatively over the stay, so a fully given-back indemnity nets
  75880000 and VAT to the cent at any compensation rate; what the caisse interne gave back of the sale no longer
  leaves room for a later line to be booked as a sale. The franchise turnover subtracts an indemnity refund at face
  value, a VAT regime switch leaves indemnity lines at 0 %, and reopening a kept acompte's card warns when refunds
  already reduced it. Accounting export plugin 1.0.22 (specs/reservation-refunds.md rule 48).
- **The fiche ledger no longer shows a kept acompte as a refund (audit R14-C1).** A cancellation's retained avoir
  reads « Acompte conservé » (« Échéances conservées » for a plan) · « Requalifié en indemnité le JJ/MM » instead
  of « Remboursement · Rendu le … · retained »; a refund taken from the indemnity reads « Rendu sur l'indemnité ».
- **A client test no longer turns red on 2026-11-02** (spec `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rule 39). The
  stale-range test of `EmailSequenceSimulation` moved « Au » to the literal `2027-01-01`, which is the default end
  (today + 60 days) on that day, so no second simulation started; it now moves it to today + 90 days. Test only.
- **The server-suite guard on app variables also sees an `env` taken whole** (spec `server-test-isolation.md`
  rule 12, reading of 2026-11-02, audit R14 L-1). The test that keeps `appEnvNames.js` equal to what `server/src/`
  reads now fails on `const { X } = env`, `'X' in env`, `{ ...env }`, `const e = env`, an `env` handed to a call
  whose callee does not name that parameter `env` (`Object.keys(env)`, `read(env)`), and `require('process').env`
  / `require('node:process').env`. The few existing uses, whose `env` is a plugin-range context or the Neat
  environment, are pinned by count with their reason. Since the review of #924 it also fails on `e = env || {}` /
  `e = env ?? {}`, on an `env` returned whole (`return env`, `=> env`, `f().env.X`), and on a call argument whose
  callee only shares its name with a function declaring `env` elsewhere: the callee is now the one the call reaches
  (the file's own function, or the one of the module it is imported from). The names only a plugin reads are keyed
  by plugin id (`PLUGIN_ENV_NAMES`), so the plugin removal matrix no longer turns this test red in 5 of its 16
  cells. Tests only: one server test added, 22 new probe forms.
- **A refund recorded on a cancelled stay can be deleted from the fiche again.** The fiche of a cancelled stay offered
  « Supprimer » on no refund, so a mistyped amount stayed in the journal and on the compensation card. It now offers it,
  outside the read-only fields, on the refunds the cancellation never read: those made after it, those made before a
  cancellation that had nothing to keep (a stay paid in full at booking), and those given back in the caisse interne.
  Never on « Acompte conservé », which the server now refuses to delete too (« L'indemnité d'annulation ne se
  supprime pas. »: deleting it left the indemnity booked while the revenue reversal was gone), nor on a refund the
  cancellation already read (« Remboursement antérieur à l'annulation. »). Each refund now records whether its stay
  was already cancelled, so a refund written in the very second of the cancellation is ordered right.
- **Caisse-interne money on a cancelled stay stays off the books, and can be given back.** An acompte collected in the
  caisse interne was requalified as if it had been booked (706 and VAT debited on a sale never booked, 7588 credited
  with money off the books); it no longer is. The register of a cancelled stay now offers what the caisse interne
  collected, beside the indemnity, given back in the caisse interne only (« Encaissé en caisse interne : rendu en
  caisse interne seulement. » by transfer): a solde of 640 € held in cash could not be given back at all. Cash collected
  on no line of the register (an end-of-stay complement) is not promised in « Dépasse l'encaissé ».
- **The commissions a payment provider withheld are requalified at the cancellation.** On a direct stay collected
  through Lodgify / Stripe, the commissions on the paid acompte and solde stayed as sales revenue with collected VAT
  once everything was given back (stay #22226: 6,20 € HT and 0,62 € of VAT). The cancellation's avoir and indemnity
  now include them — the solde's only up to what the books still hold of the solde once the refunds made before the
  cancellation took their share — the register never offers them back from the indemnity, and 706, 4457, 4671 and
  the client account end at 0 (7588 keeps the commissions, 622600 their cost). The fiche reads « Acompte et
  commission conservés » when the solde's commission is added. Past cancellations' exports do not move.
- **The server-suite guard on app variables also sees an `env` as a ternary branch and `process` handed whole to
  a call** (spec `server-test-isolation.md` rule 12, reading of 2026-11-02, audit R15 L-1). The test that keeps
  `appEnvNames.js` equal to what `server/src/` reads now fails on `x ? env : {}` / `x ? {} : env`, on
  `read(process)`, on `x || env` / `x && env` / `await env` (review of #927), and on a call whose callee shares its name with a nested function declaring `env`: the callee
  is now the declaration of the innermost block around the call. The spec no longer says the guard « fails open
  in one case »: it is a heuristic that catches the probed forms, and the forms it knowingly lets through are
  listed. No new hit or pin in `server/src/`. Tests only: 9 new probe forms, 3 new quiet forms.
- **The server suite now traces what the app reads from the environment** (spec `server-test-isolation.md`
  rule 13, review of #927). Under `npm test`, each test process runs with `process.env` behind a recording
  Proxy; at the end the runner fails when code of `server/src/` read a variable that `appEnvNames.js` does not
  list, naming the line and the test files, and prints the lines that took the environment whole. It sees the
  forms rule 12's grep guard knowingly misses, but only where the suite runs that code against the real
  environment: not through a copy of it, not in a function the tests only call with an injected `env`, not on
  a path no test runs. About 1 % of the suite's time. Tests only: one server test file added.
- **A refund made before a cancellation is no longer kept again as an indemnity (round 15).** Cancelling a direct stay
  now requalifies only what the earlier book refunds left of the acompte or of the paid installments, bucket by
  bucket (a paid solde absorbs those refunds first): acompte 274 €, 100 € given back, then cancelled → 174 € kept,
  not 274 €. Once the rest is given back, every revenue, VAT and tourist-tax account and the indemnity account net
  to 0 to the cent. A refund the cancellation deducted can no longer be deleted (« Remboursement déduit de l'acompte
  conservé. »): deleting it used to offer its amount back as sale money.
- **A cancelled stay's refund lines never add up to more than is left.** After a free line, or with a paid solde,
  the lines offered could exceed what the stay still held, and asking for all of them was refused with a line's old
  amount (« Taxe de séjour : dépasse l'encaissé (12,00 €) » with 2 € left). The lines now add up to what is left at
  most, and a refusal names the amount really left.
- **A legacy acompte is cancelled on the split it was booked on, and a paid solde absorbs earlier refunds as one sum.**
  An acompte without contribs used to be reversed on the accommodation alone, swapping 21,82 € between the 706
  sub-accounts of stay #14; it now reverses the stored-money split the export credited (one shared util, SDK 2.19.0).
  A refund made before the cancellation of a stay whose solde was paid comes off the solde first, as one sum read from
  what the solde collected: 50 € given back on #22226, then cancelled → the 126,38 € acompte is kept, and giving back
  the sale money returns 119,56 € in all, as without the refund (it used to keep 76,38 € and give 50 € more).
- **A cancelled stay's line offers at most what that line collected.** A line paid at arrival and never collected is
  no longer offered, nor does it take part of a requalified bucket, so giving everything back leaves every 706
  sub-account at 0. The VAT of a cancellation adjusted for earlier refunds now nets to the cent when a paid solde
  absorbed part of a bucket.
- **The avoir of an acompte reverses the HT and VAT its entry booked, to the cent.** A legacy acompte cancelled with
  no refund left 706 and 4457 a cent apart on about one stay in seven (one in three at 20 %); they now net to 0.
  Contribs a cent or two off the acompte snap onto its largest bucket, so the cancellation never keeps more than was
  collected. Cancellations whose contribs add up to the acompte book the same lines as before.
- **Giving back a cancelled stay's lines one at a time no longer re-offers cents.** The kept money is spread over
  the lines as they stood at the cancellation, so a refund on one line leaves what the others offer unchanged, and a
  legacy stay with a paid solde offers every cent it collected.
- **Cancelled stay — a refund the paid solde absorbed can be deleted again** (spec
  `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 55, reading of 2026-11-02, audit R16-L1). A book refund made before the
  cancellation is refused deletion (409 `REFUND_NETTED_BY_CANCELLATION`) only when it moved the `retained` avoir: the
  requalification is rebuilt without it and compared with the avoir. A refund the paid solde absorbed whole is now
  deleted by the API and offered « Supprimer » on the fiche; the avoir and the indemnity do not move. On a stay whose
  provider withheld a solde commission, the commission counts by its total, not by its per-bucket spread. +9 server
  tests.
- **Cancelled stay — the fiche's « Supprimer » on an absorbed refund is now tested end to end** (spec
  `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 55 reading, audit R17-L1). Two server tests read the fiche through
  `reservationsController.getById` on the real database and hold it to the DELETE route (100 € absorbed: offered and
  deleted; 700 €: neither). A failure reading the cancellation's netted refunds is now logged instead of silently
  hiding « Supprimer ». +2 server tests.
- **Clients — a deletion that fails after the operator moved on is reported** (`specs/clients.md`
  rule 18). A forced deletion failing once its confirmation was closed, or replaced by another
  client's, now shows an error toast instead of failing silently. Closing « Nettoyer les clients »
  while its list loads no longer lets the late answer reopen the popup.
- **Clients — a late answer no longer swaps the client in the delete confirmation** (audit R18-C1,
  `specs/clients.md` rule 17). When the impact of a client the operator had just left arrived after the
  confirmation of another client had opened, the dialog showed the first client's name, stays and
  blocker while « Confirmer la suppression » deleted the second. The confirmation and the sheet's stay
  list now show only the answer of their latest request; a deletion that ends after the operator moved on
  no longer closes or marks the next confirmation.
- **Stay dates are validated on every internal path** (spec `reservations-backend-mvc.md` rules 11-12, audit R18-1, 2026-11-02). A reversed or zero-night stay, a day the calendar lacks (`2027-02-30`) or a non-padded date (`2027-2-3`) was created at 0 €, freed its nights for a second booking and reached the iCal export as an invalid event. Reservation create / update (plugins included), the price preview, restoring a set-aside duplicate, an approved iCal date drift and devis create / update / extend / convert now answer 400 `{ error, code: 'INVALID_STAY_DATES', field }` before pricing or availability. The fiche and the devis show « La date de départ doit suivre la date d'arrivée. » under the dates and refuse the save. The iCal import is unchanged: it already drops or repairs such events. No stored row needed repair. +30 server tests, +5 client tests.
- **Clients — the late-answer guards are now pinned by tests** (audit R19-S1, `specs/clients.md`
  rule 17). Two client tests cover the orders the suite missed: client A's stay list answering after
  client B's sheet has loaded, and A's deletion succeeding after B's confirmation opened. Removing
  either guard now fails the suite. No behaviour change.
- **Reservations — a stay lasts at most 366 nights** (`specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rules 11-12).
  A departure typed far in the future (`9999-12-31`) made the price preview compute night by night for
  about 100 s and stalled every other request of the server. The quote, the reservation and the devis now
  refuse a stay longer than 366 nights at once (« Séjour trop long. »), the iCal import's own cap, and the
  fiche shows the message under the dates without asking for a quote.
- **A date typo no longer freezes or crashes the server** (audit R19; specs/finance-dashboard-redesign.md rule 51, specs/establishment-closures.md rules 18-20): a « Personnalisée » finance window is at most 750 days (« Période trop longue : 750 jours au plus. », refused before any request), and its cumulative curve always stops on the window's last day — « Au » typed as 9999-12-31 used to loop until the process ran out of memory. A closure must be two real days at most two years apart (« Date invalide. », « Fermeture trop longue : 2 ans au plus. »), and closures are expanded only over the nights asked, so a long closure no longer costs every public availability read, the fiche's occupied dates or the Google Vacation Rentals feed (plugin 1.0.3) about 3 s. The occupied-dates endpoint takes a window of at most 750 real days. A link to a refused « Personnalisée » window now opens on the period selector, the message under its dates, instead of an error with no way to change the period.
- **Fiche: no false « Fiche modifiée entre-temps » after a bank reconciliation or a payment poll** (spec `reservation-fiche-concurrency.md` rules 8-9, audit R2, 2026-10-29). Writes under `/api/payments` now answer with the stays that request itself wrote (`X-Stay-Versions`) and the open fiche adopts its own version; a foreign write is still detected. A failed « Enregistrer quand même » no longer leaves the next save without its version check. +2 server tests, +6 client tests.
- **Refund dialog: a free line with an amount and no label is refused** (spec `reservation-refunds.md` rule 41, audit R2, 2026-10-29). « Libellé manquant. » disables the save, so the total shown is the total recorded instead of silently dropping the line.
- **Refund from the fiche: the trop-perçu can no longer be refunded twice** (spec `reservation-refunds.md` rule 42, audit R2, 2026-10-29). The fiche re-reads its stored ledger after a refund is created or deleted, and the server refuses (409) a trop-perçu refund beyond what is still over-collected. +3 server tests, +2 client tests.
- The client sheet badges cancelled and set-aside stays (« Annulée », « Écartée »), and a client delete the server will refuse shows its short reason in the dialog instead of the confirmation.
- **Touch targets of 44 px on a phone** (spec `ds-components.md` rule 29, audit R2, 2026-10-29): switches (« Forcer en complément », options, e-mail toggles), clickable chips (hourly slots), all inline links, the sidebar rows on a touch tablet and the /finance month columns. Also fixes an invalid `<div>` inside `<p>` in the pricing summary and the reservation search.
- plugin:tariff-recipes The « (voir le site) » link has a 44 px touch target.
- **Release install:** `npm ci` can no longer be pointed at an unchecked lockfile through `workspaces`, and an npm alias or a tarball that is not the package's own is refused; the npm cache is bounded.
- **Console:** « Désinstaller » and « Effacer les données » now send the action the operator confirmed; the console answers 409 when the module's data changed in between, and never erases without the typed slug.
- **Releases:** a plugin package published in a previous release is no longer re-shipped (a rebuild that differs fails with « bump the version »); the release workflow's actions are pinned by commit SHA.
- **Console:** an unset `GUESTFLOW_RELEASE_PUBLIC_KEY` is logged at boot and kept as a host alert.
- **Release install:** the dependency smoke test loads the module by absolute path, archives containing a `node_modules` are refused, and `server/package.json` must be named `guestflow-server`.
- Deleting a client (forced or not) or a property now refuses, like the per-row routes, when it would remove a cancelled or set-aside stay or the CGV acceptance proof of a converted devis; the client delete preview lists cancelled stays too.
- An iCal sync that changes nothing no longer moves a stay's version, so a fiche left open across the 5-minute pass is not refused as stale; the stale check is also repeated right before the write, after the payment-provider wait.
- **The trop-perçu of a stay is measured against its stored price** (spec `reservation-save-keeps-pricing.md` rules 8-11, audit round 2). A reduction confirmed after the solde was paid still shows « Trop-perçu » with « Rembourser » after a reload and on a locked fiche; engine drift no longer shows as a trop-perçu on an unlocked past fiche; the refused save (409) carries the caisse-interne default; the detail line of a complement excess no longer says « 0,00 € dus ».
- **The stored trop-perçu counts what was really collected:** paid installments, the paid bucket at its captured or stored amount (whichever is larger), the mid-stay extras collected, an adjusted complement left out of both sides, and only free « Trop-perçu » refunds reduce it; the live ledger goes down when the edit absorbs it (spec rules 12-13).
- **The refund split of a stored trop-perçu follows what left the stay** (tourist tax, options, resources, then accommodation), and the live figure compares the posted fiche with the stored stay on one refund basis (spec rules 14-15).
- **Payments — recording a payment, an installment, a refund, a cancellation or a platform penalty no longer fails with « database is locked » when another writer commits at the same moment** (`plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 29, audit R2): these read-then-write transactions now take the write lock up front and wait for it (5 s) instead of failing at once.
- An approved iCal cancellation of a stay that carries money is no longer counted twice in the booking pace; a redirect with a large body no longer holds a feed until the timeout; the import's overlap checks and the drift approval use the nights a stay really takes (early check-in, late check-out).
- **Plugins**: switching a plugin on brings up the plugins that were waiting for it and reloads the page; a plugin still waiting names the requirement still missing; the hooks owed after a restart run requirements first; an install or activation hook that never settles is given up on after 120 s instead of holding the plugin lock.
- **Plugins, audit round 2**: activating, deactivating or uninstalling a plugin is refused (409 `UPDATE_IN_PROGRESS`) while an install is running; a plugin component that throws, or whose bundle is gone after an update, no longer takes the home, the fiche, the planning or the settings pages to the error screen; the activation hooks owed by an install that restarts the app (including a requirement installed in the same plan) now run at the next boot; a dependant revived after its requirement is failed when its migration throws and waits for enabled requirements; every member of a dependency cycle is reported and refused; "Tout activer / désactiver / installer" carries the list of plugins the operator confirmed and the server refuses with 409 `CONFIRMATION_STALE` when that list has changed (the console's module switch does the same, 422).
- Payment links of a quote converted into a stay now follow the stay: cancelling it abandons them, re-pricing the fiche retires the stale ones, and a payment on a cancelled stay is flagged for refund instead of ticking it paid. An acompte link and a full link of one record can no longer both stay payable; a payment landing while a link is being replaced is never overwritten, a retired link paid after its dates were taken still converts the quote, and an unreadable provider no longer deactivates a link that may be paid.
- A devis link paid after its stay was re-priced is checked against the stay: the trop-perçu shows on the fiche and the admin alert states the real direction and amount. Links of a converted devis are retired when it converts, when its stay changes, and by a one-time migration for those already stale. A full link is retired once the acompte is paid, and only the duplicated acompte part is refunded when both were paid. A refused or failed mint no longer cancels the other valid link, and the public status of a cancelled stay answers cancelled.
- WordPress plugin 1.18.1: the payment return page stops polling and reads « Cette réservation est annulée. » when the stay was cancelled.
- Push notifications now reach only active, non-support administrators: a deactivated account, a demoted admin or an ended support access no longer receives guest data, `/api/push/subscribe` is refused in a support session, and a user's devices are deleted on deactivation, on losing the admin role and when a support access is revoked.
- A password-reset link mailed before an email or password change (admin reset included) is voided in the same transaction as the change.
- **Suivi financier** — a window the server refuses is no longer a dead end: a custom date with a 5-digit year, 30 February or a missing date says « Deux dates à choisir. » before any request, and every refusal (month outside the exercise, unknown period or logement) keeps the period selector (and « Tous les logements ») under the error. A link the device's day refused now loads once the server's day accepts it instead of spinning (audit R20-C1, C3).
- **Fiche de réservation** — a date with a 5- or 6-digit year or an impossible day says « Date de séjour invalide. » under the dates and asks no quote, occupied dates, availability or welcome pack for it; the fiche's reads re-run when the server's day lifts a refusal (audit R20-C2, C3).
- **Emails › Simulation** — « Du » opens on the local day, not the UTC one (audit R20-C4).
- **Applying an Airbnb or Gîtes de France mail no longer erases the planned moments of a planning-card option** (specs/airbnb-mail-intake.md rule 35, specs/gites-de-france-mail-intake.md rule 35, specs/plugins-phase-1-sdk.md rule 38): both intakes rebuilt the fiche's options without their `cardOccurrences` and `cardPersons`, so a breakfast planned on two mornings for 3 covers came back « à planifier » with the sold portions, on the auto-apply and on « Appliquer » alike. Their lines now start from the new `ctx.core.reservations.updateBody(id)` (SDK 2.21.0), the very body the core's update spreads the patch over, so the intakes cannot drift from the fiche's save again. Amounts are unchanged. Plugins: Airbnb 1.1.6, Gîtes de France 1.4.8 (both `sdk ^2.21.0`).
- **A payment mark stores the day it validated, and a payment flag must be a real true / false** (specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md rule 66, audit R20-M1): `PATCH /api/reservations/:id/payment` judged the first ten characters of a paid date but wrote the raw value, so a hand-made request could store `2026-11-01<b>x</b>` as a paid date (exported as is), re-date a note settled in the stay with an array (the note then fell out of the accounting journal), write a return date beside an unticked caution return, or record `depositPaid: "false"` as a received acompte. A non-string date is now refused (« Date invalide. »), the stored date is the judged `YYYY-MM-DD` (or the row's own value when it is sent back unchanged), a date sent with an unticked mark is never written, and every payment flag (and the « caisse interne » of a note) accepts only `true`, `false`, `1` or `0` — anything else is a 400 `INVALID_PAYMENT_FLAG` with nothing written. The fiche, the dashboard, the finance page and the bank reconciliation already send these shapes: nothing they do changes.
- **The bank reconciliation start date is held to the date window** (specs/qonto-bank-reconciliation.md rule 48, audit R20-P1): « Mise en service » on the « Rapprochement bancaire » card was only checked by a `YYYY-MM-DD` regex, so `2027-02-30`, `0002-10-31` or `9999-12-31` were saved, leaving the reconciliation silently empty or asking Qonto for the account's whole history. The server now refuses an impossible day or one outside today ± 5 years; the stored value keeps saving. The field hints the same window from the server's day.
- plugin:online-payment The reconciliation start date is a real calendar day within five years of today; the date already stored stays accepted.
- **Test suites — no date is left for the five-year window to drop, and the suite passes after midnight** (audit
  R20-S1 and R20-S2, `server-test-isolation.md` rules 6 and 7). Since the date window of 2026-11-02, a past
  date a test hands to a write path is refused once it is five years old: 136 server tests in 43 files would
  have gone red from 2031-01-02, and one client test from 2031-06-02. They now build their dates from today,
  as does the bank reconciliation fixture. `stored-split-only-with-contribs` no longer fails between 00:00 and
  01:00 in Paris: it read « today » as the UTC day, as three other suites did. `check-test-dates.mjs` now also
  names a past date down to today − 5 years, and a « now » a test only injects no longer exempts its dates,
  since the window reads the real clock. No product change.
- **Accounting: re-pricing a paid stay no longer changes entries already booked when it carries a free-text line** (specs/reservation-save-keeps-pricing.md rule 19): such a save lost what each free-text line had collected, so the journal fell back to another split and moved revenue between 70600000 and 70600010, sometimes 0,01 € of VAT, on the acompte and solde entries, totals and cash unchanged. The stored amounts now follow the line through the save, like a catalogue option's. Lines already reset are not repaired: none in the production copy of 2026-10-22 nor in the inte shadow holding data to 2026-10-28; live production was not read.
- **Accounting: « Utiliser les tarifs actuels » and a change of logement no longer make booked entries move at the next save** (specs/reservation-save-keeps-pricing.md rule 20): catalogue options and resources lost what they had collected when priced without the stored snapshot. They now keep it, and the price still refreshes. Lines already reset are not repaired: none visible in the same two copies, where a paid catalogue line without a contrib only sits on a stay whose accommodation has none either.
- **Mail intakes (Airbnb, Gîtes de France): « Appliquer » writes a fiche once.** A second tab or a lost response no longer creates a new client, overwrites what was typed since or piles up custom lines: an intake already written answers its status and writes nothing, and a dismissed one is refused (« Saisie ignorée »). The rent choice and the retry of an intake in error are unchanged.
- Reservation, devis, price preview and SAS writes refuse a bad option, party or amount number with a 400 instead of storing `Infinity`, billing a free line or answering a 500: typed people and nights must be whole numbers (nights at most 366), quantities, party numbers and custom amounts finite, SAS pieces and units at most 999. Stored lines sent back unchanged are not re-judged. The check-in, check-out and complement-deferral marks only accept true / false / 1 / 0.
- **A value of the wrong JSON type is a refusal, not a crash or a stored guess** (specs `public-api.md` rule 22, `express-5-upgrade.md` rule 8, `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 67, `control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rules 47-49). `/public/v1/**`: `{"toString":1}` on a count, a time, a guest field, the message, the language or the return path answered a 500 with a logged stack, and `true` or `[1]` created a client named « True » or accepted the terms; they now answer the documented 422 envelope, and a body the JSON parser refuses answers that envelope too (same codes). Strings and numbers the site sends are unchanged, so the WordPress plugin needs no update. `/api/auth/login` and `/api/auth/reset` answer their normal 400. Console: an impossible day, a non-text field and an oversized or malformed body answer the field's 4xx instead of 500, a template can no longer be saved as « null », `/plan` requires its add-ons list, and the identity fields are capped at 200 characters (254 for the address).
- **Typed counts read true everywhere:** the arrivals table, the SAS recap, the pricing summary and Google Calendar print the people served or the nights instead of « x1 » (Google Calendar plugin 1.2.7); a count the engine clamps shows the clamped number in the field; a forced save on a devis stops asking; the mid-stay note's « Annuler » restores the opening state, its rows respect the arrival floor and the capacity, and « En fin de séjour » only shows once something was added (specs/non-card-option-served-persons.md rules 23, 29-33).
- Stay note: a number typed on an unsold line now sells that number (it used to sell 1, the stay or the party); typed nights stop at 366.
- Reservation form: a slow answer can no longer replace a newer one for occupied dates, client search or resource availability; the cots left now follow the dates, moving the dates never silently drops a cot, and a move onto a range with no cot left is refused at save instead of overselling them.
- Phone: a switch near the screen edge no longer scrolls a dialog sideways nor takes taps meant for the row's text.
- **Console: a rename can no longer lose an instance** (`specs/hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 45). After a failed address change, a second one was accepted and recorded as done on a directory nobody had moved: a later deprovision skipped the export and left the unit running, the erasure left the database, and the next customer of that address opened it. A rename now waits for the one before it (409 `RENAME_PENDING`, button disabled with its reason), a retry starts from the slug the instance really left even after two renames on one day, a rename finding no directory for a live instance fails, and a creation stops on a database already at its address. A customer stopped there cannot be closed on that database (409 `ADDRESS_HELD`) and leaves for a free address without moving it or redirecting it.
- **Console: input shape** (`specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rules 48, 50). `trial` takes a real boolean (`"false"` used to grant a trial); a template key read through the prototype (`constructor`) answers 404 on the preview too.
- plugin:google-calendar A network failure while renewing the Google access token no longer writes the refresh token to the logs: every log site prints Google's answer, or message, code and status (audit R22 E22-1). After installing, search the production logs for `refresh_token`; if found, disconnect and reconnect Google and purge those logs.
- **Wrong JSON types on the authenticated API** (audit R22; `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 68, `reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 20, `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md` rule 7, `express-5-upgrade.md` rule 9). `{"toString":1}`, `true`, `{}` or `[1]` on a field used to answer 500 with a logged stack, or be stored as `[object Object]`: each now gets the field's refusal, with a last-resort `400 INVALID_FIELD_TYPE`. A password or code that is not a string is a wrong one and counts; a stay's times are `HH:MM`, its ids positive integers, its ticks true / false / 1 / 0, stored values sent back unchanged exempt; a planning card's `done: "false"` no longer ticks it; a plugin setting can no longer be overwritten by an object; a secret's field of spaces keeps it.
- **False crashes in the log** (audit R22, public findings 1-2). A compressed body that does not inflate, a dropped upload and a badly encoded path parameter are refused without an `[unhandled]` line.
- **Guest names** (audit R22, public finding 3). A first or last name with no letter or digit is refused as empty on the public form; control characters are never stored.
- **Missing rows** (audit R22, verification of #961). A season on a property that does not exist and a dismissal of a fiche whose number is not digits answer 404, not a 500 with a logged stack.
- **School holidays label** (audit R22-P2). A label that is not text now gets « Le libellé est obligatoire » on create and update; an object was stored as « [Object object] ».
- **Mail intakes: an IMAP socket error stays inside the pass** (spec `airbnb-mail-intake.md` rule 36, audit R22 E22-4). A network reset or socket timeout no longer reaches `uncaughtException`; the real cause is logged and the pass rejects as before.
- **Growth after solde and arrival complement are paid is claimed.** One adult more on a direct stay left 42,40 € asked of nobody; the end-of-stay complement now claims it, booked as a prestation on 70600010, once the solde's contribs are captured and the pre-arrival money is settled.
- **A live stay refuses a book refund above what it collected** (`REFUND_EXCEEDS_COLLECTED`), as a cancelled stay does. The refund dialog disables the save on « Virement » and « Espèces »; the caisse interne stays a warning.
- **A collected complement keeps its entry**, and a paid line removed from the fiche no longer rewrites a booked entry: tax that rises, a change of party or a removed line leave the journal byte-identical.
- **Cancelled-stay register:** a paid complement reads what each line collected while the solde is unpaid, a line lowered after payment offers what it collected, a removed paid line is offered under its name, and giving everything back nets every account to 0.
- **A solde ticked before its acompte keeps what it collected** through a save: the acompte still owed shrinks first, a rise goes to the complement, and the stored split is written from its first entry.
- **The added guests' supplement is accommodation revenue from its first entry**, in every platform mode and order.
- **The added guests' tax is out of the pre-arrival money** on a plan, a link, a plan's avoir and the acompte's ceiling: 46710000 no longer books it twice.
- **Saving a platform stay keeps `clientGrossAmount`** unless the body carries a number: legacy stays no longer lose turnover from a closed month.
- **An offered planning-card line has a floor** on the persons it stored at arrival; a NULL count is never compared.
- **A cancelled plan gives back everything it collected:** with a guest added and a complement paid, 42,40 € stayed on no line of the refund register. It now offers the installments and the complement, and every account nets to 0.
- **An acompte ticked after a collected complement keeps its entry** when its engine split fails: it takes the stored split at once, so a later change of party no longer moves a booked entry.
- **An installment with no frozen split books its tourist tax** on a stay with added guests: the addition was taken off a share that no longer held it.
- One reading of a number at every write boundary (audit R22): a party that is negative or fractional, a repeated option or resource line, money past the usable range and a wrong JSON type are refused, never clamped, and the price preview now refuses what the save refuses. Stored values sent back unchanged are not re-judged.
- Catalogue numbers no longer store Infinity or negatives that reached a stay's money: seasons (price, net targets, minimum of nights), properties, options, resources, the SAS billable prices and breakfast counts. `POST /properties/:id/pricing/progressive-preview` holds `maxNights` to a stay's length: one request could stop the server.
- SAS: a sent number of people served is a whole number from 1 to 999; `-5`, `0` and `0.5` billed the whole party.
- **Push: the subscription is validated on arrival** (spec `pwa-push-notifications.md` rule 15, audit R22 E22-5). `POST /api/push/subscribe` answers 400 `INVALID_SUBSCRIPTION` unless the endpoint is a public https address on the default port and the keys have the right size. The endpoint must read as the same host to the URL parser and to web-push's legacy one, so a login, a `%` or a character such as `}` in the host is refused. No allow-list of push hosts; stored rows are not re-judged.
- **Qonto: one token refresh at a time** (spec `qonto-settings-in-app.md` rule 18, audit R22 E22-3). Concurrent calls on an expired token shared one refresh instead of reusing the same refresh token, which could fail one of them, possibly a guest's payment, or leave the connection dead if the provider revokes on reuse.
- **Added guests' tourist tax, declaration** (spec `platform-added-guests-tourist-tax.md` rules 5, 19-26, audit R22). Ticking a row on the day its complement was collected no longer splits it: the tick records whether the declared row held the addition. The added row reads the adults stored with its amount, a gap with nobody added says so and claims no adult, its own tick is cleared when the complement is unticked, and ticking a row that no longer exists answers 409. On a percentage property the comparison reads the occupants of the paid party, not the persons billed. The line is renamed « Complément de taxe de séjour » on the fiche, SAS recap, departure recall, J-2 email and declaration page. The declaration page reads the month on screen again after a tick, and a tick repeated from a tab left open keeps the first date.
- Payment marks: a save adjusting a paid arrival or end-of-stay complement below what a book refund needs is refused, naming the refund to delete first (`reservation-refunds.md` rule 52 reading).
- **Stock of cots and per-stay resources** (`specs/devis-accept-to-reservation.md` rule 10, `specs/calendar-property-parity.md` rule 31, `specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 21). Converting a devis and « Rétablir » now judge the stock like a new stay and answer 409 past it, instead of promising the same cot or item twice; a devis paid through its link still converts. The stock out on a range is now the peak of one night, so stays that never meet no longer refuse a long stay across them.
- **Check-in from 10:01 to 10:59** (`specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 22): it no longer blocks the night before in the overlap check and the closures, as the calendar and the iCal export already read it.
- **Blank time on a stay update** (rule 23): it keeps the stored time instead of writing 15:00 / 10:00.
- **Reservation number refusals** (rule 24): over 64 characters or with control characters, the message names the field, and the fiche's field stops at 64. **A devis's money refusal** answers French text and its code, as a stay's does (rule 19).
- **Every way out of a dirty page asks first** (`ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rules 40-42): the browser or phone « Retour » on a fiche or a logement, a reload of a fiche, the top-bar search, the « Retour » arrow of settings pages, the CGV draft, and « Se déconnecter ».
- **Technical errors read in French** (rule 44): failed connection, an answer that is not JSON, an expired session, and the code-only refusals such as `SMTP_NOT_CONFIGURED`. The account dialog prints a sentence under its field (`admin-account-management.md` rule 20).
- **A note on a started stay with no arrival marked says where the sale goes** (`mid-stay-notes.md` rule 23), from a server flag; rule 5bis corrected.
- An unknown logement reads « Logement introuvable. » with the way back; the tax page's tile reads « Réservations (mois) »; on the Plugins page an older list no longer overwrites a newer state (rules 43, 45, 39).
- Console: a customer whose address holds another customer's database no longer offers « Télécharger la licence » (`hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 48).
- Console: a failed address change no longer lets other actions reach the wrong place. « Restaurer à une date » is refused until the address change is finished, and a blocked « Réessayer » keeps the instance running. A fleet update skips that customer (« Changement d’adresse à terminer d’abord. ») instead of stopping for everyone, and the plugin push and the catalogue read its space at the address that still answers. An address with a folder on it is refused before a rename starts. A customer refused at creation because another customer's database sits at its address no longer writes a licence there, reads or orders an erasure of its modules, opens its support access or files its accounts under its own name. An archived customer gets no support sign-in link (specs/hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md rules 46-51).
- **Qonto: a new consent subscribes the webhook on the account it names** (`settings-one-save-and-automatic-webhook.md` rule 10, audit R23 E23-1). After a switch to production, or a consent on another organisation, the old account's record answered « skipped » and no webhook ever arrived on the new one: paid links were seen only on the return page or at the poll. A successful authorisation now forgets the record, then lists, adopts or creates. An instance that switched before this version needs one new consent.
- **Qonto: two webhook checks at once share one call** (same spec, rule 8, E23-5), so they can no longer create two subscriptions.
- **Qonto: a token refresh overtaken by new credentials or a new consent stores nothing** (`qonto-settings-in-app.md` rule 19, E23-4).
- **Qonto: a host variable that does not name Qonto always wins** (`qonto-settings-in-app.md` rule 5, reading of 2026-11-05). A loopback fake or dead port held only until an environment was stored. A variable naming a real Qonto host still gives way to the chosen environment. Under `npm test`, a dial to a real Qonto host is now refused and fails the file (`server-test-isolation.md` rule 14).
- **iCal and push: reserved addresses are not public** (`ical-import-hardening.md` rule 17, E23-6, R23-I7): the benchmark, documentation and special-purpose IPv4 ranges, and any IPv6 outside global unicast.
- **Push: a name is judged where it is dialled** (`pwa-push-notifications.md` rule 15, E23-7): a name answering a private address opens no connection, counts as failed and keeps its row.
- Authenticated API, wrong input shape, last class (audit R23): a `null` no longer clears a property's access code or Wi-Fi password; resource bookings, options, resources, seasons, closures, iCal sources, email templates and mentions, stay texts, arrival facts, compensations, calendar notes, cancellation reasons, platform penalties, installment lists, Qonto credentials, Neat selection, reconciliation writes and SAS billable labels answer the field's refusal instead of a 500 or `[object Object]` stored as text.
- A malformed `propertyId` on a closure no longer closes every property (400 `INVALID_PROPERTY`); `DELETE` of an unknown closure answers 404.
- `POST /api/properties` refuses an unusable `pricePerNight`, an unknown `touristTaxMode` (it silently zeroed the tax) and a `photo` that is not the stored one (a deletion could remove any file in uploads).
- A platform named « constructor » no longer answers 500; a role listed twice no longer logs the user out; a plugin secret no longer takes a boolean or a number; control characters leave a client's text, an option's title and a property's name.
- A logement's « Enregistrer et quitter » leaves only after a successful save. An untouched new fiche no longer asks on leaving. « Ouvrir la gestion tarifaire » asks on a dirty logement. « Mon compte » shows French refusals, the downloads say « Connexion au serveur impossible. », a failed SMTP test says why in French.
- A wrong JSON type on `PUT /settings`, `/email-texts`, `/properties/email-hooks` and a platform's review wording is refused, it used to be stored as text. The fiche's draft client loses its control characters, and a pasted tab in a client's text becomes a space.
- « Déclarée »: a plan whose tax rides the complement waits for the complement too. A move to a platform that takes the tax on arrival is refused once the tax is collected (409 `TOURIST_TAX_ALREADY_COLLECTED`). The preview lifts the frozen tax when the party or the dates change, as the save does. The « + compl. » chip is read-only while the switch is locked.
- Refunds: a live stay refuses a refund on a line above what that line collected (`REFUND_EXCEEDS_LINE_COLLECTED`), the dialog blocks from the server's figure; free lines stay judged on the total and the caisse interne is not judged.
- Payment marks: un-ticking a payment, the arrival undo and the reconciliation's « Défaire » refuse while a book refund needs that money, naming the refund to delete first.
- End-of-stay complement: growth on a fully paid plan with its complement paid is claimed at the end of stay; a future stay whose end-of-stay complement is paid refuses growth, as a started stay does.
- Cancelled stays: the options and resources a plan collected beyond the lines still naming them, and a paid end-of-stay complement, are offered on the refund register, so giving everything back nets every account to 0.
- **A form refused for its input no longer locks the visitor out** (decision D18; `public-api.md` rule 9, `public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md` rule 6): a `422` on `POST /public/v1/booking-requests` gives its hit back to the 5-per-hour budget. Any other answer still spends it; the broad limit and the proof of work stand.
- **Public refusals follow the visitor's language** (`site-english-version.md` rule 8): the `429` of the public limiters, the `404` of an unknown `/public/v1` path and the API key's `401` / `403`. Code and status do not move.
- **One address reads as one address** (`clients.md` rule 12, `public-api.md` rule 21): an address holding a non-ASCII character is stored in lower case, so rules 7 and 12 of the public request see it, and format characters leave a public `guest.email` instead of making a second client that reads the same.
- **Public names and free text hold nothing invisible** (`public-api.md` rule 20, `public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md` rule 22, `site-traffic-analytics.md` rule 15): a name of Hangul fillers alone is `required`; control and bidi characters leave the message and the attribution before the length check and the fingerprint.
- Qonto: a token refresh overtaken by new credentials or a new consent no longer records a failure on the connection, even when that refresh fails (`qonto-settings-in-app.md` rule 19).
- **« Déclarée » waits for a stay paid and over** (specs/tourist-tax-declared-checkbox.md rule 8, D16): a new tick, main row or added guests' row, is refused with 409 unless the stay is paid and its end date is past; the page greys the checkbox and says so. Withdraws D14's « a tax not yet collected can be declared », the plan row stays where it is. Stored ticks are not rewritten; unticking stays possible.
- **A plan pins the tourist tax it collected** (specs/tourist-tax-matches-the-office-calculation.md rule 19, D17): a plan fully paid, or a paid installment holding a tax share, freezes the tax as a paid solde does, so a rate move no longer asks the guest for more on an unrelated save. A party change still re-prices.
- **« Taxe de séjour en complément » cannot go on over a tax already collected** (specs/force-item-to-complement.md rule 20): 409 `TOURIST_TAX_ALREADY_COLLECTED` and a caption on the fiche; it billed the tax twice and rewrote a booked entry.
- **An added tax grown after the tick has its own row** (specs/platform-added-guests-tourist-tax.md rule 29): the declared row keeps what it told the commune, the difference is offered for declaration.
- **Reservation fiche — a new client typed on an unsaved fiche counts as an edit** (spec `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rule 40, reading of 2026-11-05, audit R24-1). On a calendar-prefilled new stay or devis, « + Créer un nouveau client » left « Enregistrer » grey and leaving the page dropped the typed client without a warning; the client now joins the compared state, so Save lights up and every exit asks. +1 client test.
- **Password change — a wrong current password no longer signs the operator out** (rule 44, reading of 2026-11-05, audit R24-C1). The 401 `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` of `POST /auth/change-password` is the form's refusal: « Mot de passe actuel incorrect. » shows and the session stays; a session really gone still signs out. +2 client tests.
- Reservation page: a client typed on a fiche no longer follows the page to the next fiche it loads (« Retour » after a devis created in place).
- Public quote: the Roostview price watch is no longer refused for the visitor's times, which it never sends (`specs/roostview-api-key.md` rule 7); the times are judged before the stay rules (public-api rule 24 reading).
- Option editor: with « Gratuit » or « Dégressif participants », the refusal that keeps a property's free units (« Prix requis : unités offertes ») now shows under that property's row instead of only « 1 champ à corriger » (tariff-recipes rule 69 reading). +2 client tests; +2 server tests pin that a SAS never refuses to re-open a mark it did not settle.
- Qonto settings: while a webhook-check failure stands, the badge names its real repair (« Connexion » for a refused scope, the public address when it is missing) and « Test » runs the check again, so its advice can clear it, in the core and in the console (settings-one-save-and-automatic-webhook rule 15 reading).
- Company logo: a PNG whose transparency stream is damaged no longer makes every devis PDF hang; the logo is stored as the image library writes it again, and the PDF re-encodes a stored logo before drawing it. A JPEG named `.png` (or the reverse) is accepted and stored under the extension its bytes hold (settings rule 8 correction).
- Property photo: over 5 Mo or sent under another field, the refusal is French (« Photo trop volumineuse (max 5 Mo). », « Fichier non enregistré. »), never multer's English text (settings-input-guards rule 60 reading).
- Devis PDF: an issuer address ending with a newline (or holding an empty line) no longer prints a blank line (ui-review-2026-10-19-operations rule 49); English quotes keep the French decimal comma, recorded.
- Console: « Réessayer » on a failed « Export complet » now cancels the open invoices and their Qonto links, as the main « Déprovisionner » does (`control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rule 51).
- Console: a rename is recorded before the address changes, so a restart while it waits leaves « Réessayer » instead of a new address with nothing pending (`hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 52).
- Console: no licence download for a customer held off its address, on the page or the route (rule 53); no « Reprendre » on a closed customer's failed creation step (rule 54); the probe measures the old address during an unfinished rename, so no false « ne répond plus » (rule 55).
- Bank reconciliation: a bank account picked after a first read is read from 13 months before the start date, instead of only from the last read; an account that already has credits stays incremental (`qonto-bank-reconciliation.md` rule 49, online-payment 1.5.5).
- Payments settings: a failed webhook-subscription check stays shown until a check succeeds, instead of being erased by the next successful poll or reconciliation read (`settings-one-save-and-automatic-webhook.md` rule 15).
- Payments settings: the origin of the last Qonto failure is always named in French, from a label the server sends (`qonto-settings-in-app.md` rule 21).
- **A WEBP or GIF logo no longer stops every devis PDF** (specs/settings.md rule 8, corrected): the logo upload takes a PNG or a JPEG only, judged on its name, its type and its first bytes, and the image must decode whole; a refused file leaves the stored logo as it was, and a new logo removes the former `company-logo.*` files of the other extensions. A logo already stored that the PDF cannot read (WEBP, GIF, damaged) gives the devis without its logo and a `[devisPdf] logo` line in the server log, instead of « Erreur lors de la génération du PDF. ».
- **A damaged property photo is refused by name** (specs/settings-input-guards.md rule 59): « Image illisible. » (400) on creation and update, nothing stored, instead of a 500 that dropped the rest of the form.
- **A long document name is stored, shortened, and an upload failure never shows the server's path** (specs/settings-input-guards.md rule 60): a name over about 240 characters used to answer `ENAMETOOLONG` with the absolute uploads path; any failure other than size or type now answers « Fichier non enregistré. ».
- **A template that names `{{constructor}}` or `{{toString}}` reads « Variable inconnue »** (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 11, reading of 2026-11-05), not the text of a native function.
- **The colour of an iCal source passes the platform colour check** (specs/platforms-and-ical-rework.md rule 17): a sent `platformColor` that is not a readable `#rrggbb` is refused with 400 `INVALID_FIELD`; a stored colour sent back passes. Any text used to reach the calendar chips as CSS.
- **A resource booking ends after it starts** (specs/resource-supplements-total-server-side.md rule 23, reading of 2026-11-05; `hourly-resources` 1.3.7): an end at or before the start is refused on the preview, the create and the update, unless both times are the stored ones; with a minimum duration of 0 it used to be stored at 0 €.
- Options: saving an option keeps the free units each property's row holds (the Lodge welcome pack); a save that would drop a row holding some is refused on its price (audit R24-DB-1, specs/tariff-recipes rule 69).
- Refunds: « Sans acompte » saved on a paid acompte is refused while book refunds would exceed what stays collected, as an un-tick is (audit R24-DB-2, reservation-refunds rule 52).
- Complements: an adjustment equal to the engine's figure is written, so the stale amount of an un-ticked complement can be set to 0 (audit R24-DB-3, reservation-payment-ledger rule 67).
- plugin:sas A re-opened arrival or departure SAS that would take back a payment past a book refund is refused, 409 REFUND_BLOCKS_UNTICK, nothing written (audit R24-DB-2).
- **The website no longer sells an arrival or departure time the house cannot take** (specs/public-api.md rule 24, specs/public-online-payment.md rule 17, specs/plugins-phase-1-sdk.md rule 40): the quote, the booking request and the online payment judged the nights only, so an arrival inside the cleaning gap after a departure that day, or a departure leaving no cleaning time before an arrival, was paid in full and then converted with a date conflict for the operator to settle. The three now run the back office's check with the visitor's times (the property's defaults when none is sent) and answer `409 STAY_TIMES_UNAVAILABLE`, « Horaires indisponibles à ces dates : modifier l’heure d’arrivée ou de départ. », before any request or payment; the drawer prints it on every plugin version. Plugin: Réservation depuis le site 1.8.12 (`sdk ^2.22.0`, SDK 2.22.0 adds `blockedDates.stayTimesRefused`).
- **A phone number pasted with a no-break space or an invisible mark is accepted on the website** (specs/public-api.md rule 25): format characters leave it and every space separator reads as a space before it is judged, and the cleaned number is stored.
- plugin:online-payment Declares SDK ^2.21.0, the SDK whose `takeBackRefusal` its 1.5.3 reconciliation undo already reads (audit R24-P2).
- Test suite: the route census and the catalogue closed-values suite judge only the plugins the tree ships, so every cell of the plugin removal matrix is green again; the SDK core-module contract lists every name a plugin reads, five names added; two added-guests tourist-tax suites no longer fail on set days of the month (audit R24-S1, R24-S2, R24-P1, R24-P2).
- Reservations: « Créer un devis » on a stay not saved yet keeps the client typed with « + Créer un nouveau client », and the devis it opens counts as unsaved work: « Enregistrer le devis » is enabled, and Back, « Annuler » or a reload ask before the draft is dropped.
- Settings: a logo replaced by another file of the same format shows at once in Paramètres → Établissement, without a reload.
- Online payment: the « Adresse publique manquante » advice and the Qonto and Google public-address messages point to Paramètres → Système (URL publique) instead of a menu that does not exist, and « Test » now re-runs the webhook check whenever Qonto answered, the link provider still pending included, so the advice can be cleared.
- Bank reconciliation: without Qonto credentials the 8-hour read waits instead of recording a « Dernier échec » that repeats the badge (online-payment 1.5.6).
- Options: switching an option's price type without sending its price judges the stored price under the new type, so a 115 € option can no longer become 115 % of the stay unchecked (« 100 % max »); the per-logement prices kept are judged the same way (spec `options-resources-ux.md` rule 34).
- Logements: a property's option list naming the same option twice is refused (« Liste d’options invalide. ») instead of failing with a server error.
- Devis: after the first save of a devis opened by « Créer un devis », Back returns to the stay fiche instead of showing the saved draft again as unsaved work, which could write a second devis and a second client (spec `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rule 52).
- Online payment: « Connexion » without Qonto credentials names the « Paiements en ligne » page instead of a menu that does not exist.
- Options: saving « Lit bébé » (or any engine-derived option) from the Options side panel no longer switches its automatic billing off; a catalogue save (option, resource, property) now keeps every field it does not send, so a partial save can no longer turn a property's acompte off, blank its name or empty a resource's logements.
- Logements: setting a property's option list with an unknown property or option is refused (« Liste d’options invalide. ») instead of failing with a server error.
- Options: the refusal on a row holding free units now names the way out the app has, a price (0 € included) (« Prix requis : unités offertes ici (0 € accepté) »).
- plugin:sas A complement paid before check-in (payment link, fiche tick, bank reconciliation) is left untouched by the first arrival SAS: « Valider et terminer » no longer un-ticks it, defers it to the check-out or refuses the check-in over an unrelated refund; the recap seeds its mode from the stored flags on the first run too (specs `defer-arrival-complement-to-checkout.md` rule 21, `single-payment-at-check-in.md` rule 18, audit R25-A1).
- plugin:sas The departure SAS no longer re-stamps the date or caisse interne flag of an end-of-stay complement paid elsewhere (spec `recall-unpaid-arrival-complement-at-checkout.md` rule 10).
- plugin:sas A reception user meeting the SAS refund refusal reads a short message naming no refund amount, date or id; admin unchanged (spec `reception-role-checkin-only.md` rule 16, audit R25-A2).
- SAS: an end-of-stay complement already collected outside the departure SAS (ticked on the fiche, single payment, growth paid before arrival) no longer takes the cleaning, missing linen or extinguisher charges of the departure: preview and commit answer 409 `END_OF_STAY_ALREADY_COLLECTED`, the recap says why and « Valider et terminer » stays closed. A sale at the arrival SAS that would land on that collected complement is refused the same way; before, it was written and billed nowhere (sas 1.7.14).
- Options: an option saved as « free » or per-participant progressive drops its per-logement prices even when the request does not send them, so the quote stops billing the old price; a row holding free units keeps its « Prix requis » refusal.
- Reservations: on a started fiche still locked whose end-of-stay complement is collected, removing a discount (or any allowed price adjustment that raises the stay) is refused like on a stay not yet started (`MID_STAY_EXTRAS_CLOSED`) when no unpaid payment can take the rise on; before, the price rose and nothing asked for the difference. A checked-out stay whose solde (a platform payout above all) is still open accepts it, the rise landing on the solde. A direct stay paid by installments with one still unpaid accepts it too, the unpaid installment taking the rise.
- Booking intake and gate access: the missing public address now points to Paramètres → Système, the page that holds it, instead of « Paramètres › Société » and « Réglages → Système » (booking-intake 1.3.11, gate-access 1.3.3).
- Finance: « Tout solder » on a row whose only due is the end-of-stay complement before the check-out is drawn disabled with « Après le départ. » (tooltip, and a caption on a phone) instead of doing nothing in silence; the fiche's merged card of a deferred complement stays open when its end-of-stay part is already settled.
- **A discount typed by mistake on a checked-out, fully collected stay can be taken back** (specs/mid-stay-extras-to-end-of-stay-complement.md rule 20, reading of 2026-11-06, audit R27-RES-1): on a locked fiche whose end-of-stay complement is collected, lowering the « Réduction (%) » (5 → 20 %) left a « Trop-perçu 90 € À rembourser » and every attempt to raise it back was refused « Complément de fin de séjour déjà encaissé ». The rise is now refused only for the part neither an unpaid bucket, an unpaid installment nor the trop-perçu still open (net of the refunds already made, caisse interne included) claims: 20 → 5 % and 20 → 10 % pass, 20 → 0 % (30 € above what was collected) is still refused.
- **Taxe de séjour: the « Liste / Par nuit » switch is drawn once on a tablet** (specs/tourist-tax-report-v2.md §6, audit R27-C1): between 600 and 899 px it showed both in the page bar and under the pickers; the bar now shows it from 900 px only.
- **A complement deferred to the check-out no longer asks again for an end of stay already collected** (specs/defer-arrival-complement-to-checkout.md rule 22, specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md rule 69, audit R28-C-M1): a stay left « Perçu en fin de séjour » over a 0 € (or paid) arrival complement drew « Complément de fin de séjour · À percevoir au départ » for money already counted in « Total perçu »; its « Marquer perçu » moved the end of stay's accounting entry to another month and « Annuler » erased the collection. The fiche merges the two complements while the arrival one is still to collect, leaves an end of stay settled apart on its own paid card, and a mark of the complement never re-dates nor clears it. Collected together by the merged card (same date, same caisse), the two stay on one paid card: its « Annuler », its caisse switch and the reconciliation's « Défaire » carry both, and the reconciled credit stays « Rapproché » on it.
- Reservations: « Percevoir en fin de séjour » is refused while the end-of-stay complement is already collected (books or caisse interne): `PATCH /payment` and the arrival SAS's « Plus tard » answer 409 `DEFERRAL_END_OF_STAY_SETTLED` « Complément de fin de séjour déjà encaissé. », nothing written, and the fiche draws the button closed with that reason. Before, an end of stay collected on its own and a complement marked the same day read as one collection, so « Annuler » cleared both and a credit reconciled on the end of stay was reported as missing (sas 1.7.15).
- **The merged « Complément de fin de séjour » card keeps one reading in every state** (specs/defer-arrival-complement-to-checkout.md rules 24-27, specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md rule 69, audit R29): a deferred arrival complement with no end-of-stay lines stays « Complément de fin de séjour · Perçu » once collected, so a credit reconciled on it stays « Rapproché » instead of coming back as « Saisi, rien sur le compte » (stays 9, 11 and 22291 show their merged card again); an end of stay collected by a « paiement unique » that leaves the arrival part out keeps its own card; the end of stay the merged card holds can no longer be marked on its own (409); and un-ticking the merged card now dissolves the single payment and the departure SAS ownership of the end of stay, so a re-opened departure SAS never un-pays what the fiche collected.
- **Accessible names and touch targets** (audit R3, specs `settings-rationalization.md` 34, `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` 37). The four icon buttons of « Utilisateurs » and the default-hour field of an option grid are named. On touch: client e-mail and phone links, the devis number, « Modifier : objet », « Changer la couleur » and the laundry weekday toggles reach 44 px. +3 client tests.
- **Load failures are shown** (audit R3). « Indemnités d'annulation », « Paiements en ligne », « Historique des emails » and « TVA & exercice » show an error with « Réessayer » instead of a false zero, an endless spinner, an empty list or nothing. The email history also ignores a late response of an older filter. Specs `cancellation-compensation.md` 30, `qonto-settings-in-app.md` 17, `email-history-rolling-window.md` 11, `vat-optional-mode.md` 45. +9 client tests.
- **A customer contact email starting with `-` can now be provisioned** (specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md rule 43): the console passed `--email` as a separate argument, which the strict script parser refuses; it now uses the `--email=-x` form like the name and company.
- **Security: a release lockfile entry must now be spelled exactly as npm will fetch it** (specs/self-update-and-releases.md rule 49): the check that each dependency comes from its own registry tarball compared a raw string prefix, so a `resolved` URL with `..` segments passed the check while npm downloaded another package. The URL must now be canonical, on `registry.npmjs.org`, without query or fragment, and end with the package's own `<name>-<version>.tgz`.
- **Mail intakes (SDK 2.15.0):** a pass no longer downloads and holds every mail of the sender from the last 30 days. Known Message-IDs are skipped before the body is downloaded, mails are processed one by one, a pass reads at most 200 (newest first), and messages that are not intakes or are too large are remembered as `ignored`. The four mail-related plugins (website-booking 1.8.6, booking-intake 1.3.8, airbnb-intake 1.1.3, gites-de-france-intake 1.4.5) now require SDK 2.15 so an older core refuses them instead of running without the core hardenings.
- **Gîtes de France and Airbnb intakes in a published package:** the intake service no longer requires `./manifest.json` (absent beside the code in a built package, it made the pass, the dashboard line and every fiche block fail with a 500). Plugins read their version with the new `sdk.manifestVersion(__dirname)`.
- **Mail intakes:** a flood of forged mails can no longer push a genuine DKIM-verified booking out of reach (the 200-mail cap applies to unauthenticated mails only, with a warning when hit); envelopes are fetched in bulk; two overlapping passes join instead of failing with a SQLite error; non-intake mails no longer inflate the "mails lus" count and are purged after 31 days.
- **Refunds — no more trop-perçu on a platform stay's live preview, right poste after a save, fresh lines in the refund dialog** (audit R3, `reservation-save-keeps-pricing.md` rules 16-17, `reservation-refunds.md` rule 43): the fiche no longer offers « Rembourser » on an Airbnb or Gîtes de France stay while its gross is being corrected; a complement excess caused by a removed activity stays booked on « Activités » after the save; the dialog opened by « Enregistrer et rembourser » lists the sold lines as saved.
- **Refunds — « + Nouveau remboursement » warns while a trop-perçu is open** (`reservation-refunds.md` rule 44): « Un trop-perçu de X € est ouvert. » with an action that reopens the dialog on the trop-perçu; a sold-line refund stays independent of it.
- **Security: adopting the company email as login in the start assistant ends the account's other sessions** (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rule 64), as changing the email in « Mon compte » already did.
- **Plugins:** a plugin whose requirements include a dependency cycle can no longer be activated (`REQUIREMENT_CYCLE`, on the action and on its card); a dependant whose revive migration fails reads "Erreur" instead of an unexplained "Incompatible"; when an install plan owes a restart, the later installs wait for the boot so requirements always run first.
- Devis: a quote for a stay starting today or tomorrow is valid, and due, on the day it is issued; it was born expired, its PDF asked for payment before its own date, and « Prolonger » left it expired again. Legacy PDFs without a stored validity use the same rule.
- Reservations: a devis id sent to the stay save, payment, arrival-payment, installment removal or platform penalty routes answers 404 « Réservation non trouvée » and writes nothing.
- Fiche: the live summary panel files a deferred complement collected with the end of stay (or at a 0 € end of stay) under « Complément de fin de séjour », as its ledger card does.
- Fiche: a refused « Percevoir en fin de séjour », « Reporter au départ » on a note or note settlement shows the server's reason and leaves the fiche as stored; the note dialog stays open.
- Dashboard: a deferred arrival complement already collected reads « Complément OK », no longer « Complément reporté ».
- A still-valid devis moved to earlier dates no longer asks for payment after the guest's arrival: its validity date is capped at two days before the new start, never before today.
- After a refused note settlement, « Annuler » no longer removes from the fiche a sale that was already saved.
- A devis moved to earlier dates and then back gets its full validity again, instead of keeping a deadline of today for a stay weeks away.
- While a devis's dates are being edited, its « Valide jusqu'au » chip and its payment deadline show the date the save will write; after the save the chip follows it without a reload, and a quote the save re-issued no longer reads « Périmé ».
- A note settlement whose follow-up refresh fails no longer leaves « Nouvelle note » stuck with every button disabled, and a payment that was recorded is no longer reported as refused when only the refresh failed.
- **A devis never asks for its acompte after its solde** (D23, specs `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 19 and `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rules 5 and 56, audits R33-M-M1 = R33-DB-M1, R33-DB-L1). A devis with an acompte for a stay 31 to `30 + validity` days out had its solde due before its acompte, on the PDF and on the reservation it was converted into (dashboard « Solde à demander » then « À annuler » before the acompte fell due). Such a devis now owes one payment, due on its validity date: its live quote, save, read, PDF, e-mails, payment link, the site's quote, « Transformer en devis », the conversion and the converted stay's later saves all agree. Reservations keep rule 47.
- **The live quote of a new devis is the devis its first save stores** (audit R33-M-L1): « Nouveau devis » now shows the deadlines « Enregistrer » writes.
- **A page load overtaken by another no longer fills the fiche** (audit R33-C-L1): a devis re-read landing after « Accepté » had opened the new reservation left the devis's data on it.
- **An open acompte link can no longer be paid on a devis that now owes one payment** (audit R33 N1, specs `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 20): a devis stored with an acompte inside that window (issued before the rule, or whose logement's « Solde dû … jours avant » changed) kept its acompte link payable, and paying it booked the money on a 0 € acompte and asked the full price again. The devis is now re-stored as its single payment and the link withdrawn; an acompte link paid before that converts the devis with its acompte paid.
- **A converted devis keeps the deadline it promised** (specs `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rules 21 to 23, `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 57; audits R34-RES-L1, R34-RES-L2). A direct devis with no acompte owes one payment on its validity date; once converted, the stay lost that deadline at its first priced save when the stay was more than `30 + validity` days out (start − 30 replaced it, with no message), and deleting the converted devis did the same for any stay (an acompte the guest was never asked for appeared). The stay now keeps the promised deadline for the dates quoted, and a converted devis cannot be deleted while its stay exists: « Ce devis porte l'échéance de la réservation qui en est issue : il ne peut pas être supprimé. ». The devis of a cancelled stay holds no deadline any more and can be deleted as before. Decision D27: rows stored before the fix keep the promised deadline too, so a converted stay already overdue stays overdue.
- **Devis: an OTA devis no longer hands a direct deadline to its stay** (specs/deposit-blocks-the-dates.md rule 24): a stay converted from a devis on Booking (or another OTA) and then switched to Direct took the devis's validity date as its solde deadline, months before an identical direct stay. Only a direct devis's promise is kept now.
- **Devis: the stored schedule always matches the fiche and the PDF** (specs/deposit-blocks-the-dates.md rule 25): after a change of « Solde dû … jours avant », a devis outside the single-payment window kept its old solde date in the e-mail tokens and the conversion while the fiche and the PDF read the new one; a legacy devis with no validity date is stamped with the date it reads, so its conversion and its stay read one date. Re-stored at boot and after a logement save.
- **Devis numbers are never handed out twice** (specs/public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md rule 35): a devis deleted from its fiche, or with its client or logement, gave its number back to the next devis of the month. Every deleted devis now retires its number.
- **Refund register with a payment in several methods** (specs/mixed-method-payments.md rules 54 and 55): a geste that the accommodation cannot hold (a complement paid before the solde) comes off the other lines, so no line offers more than it collected; the lines of a split payment add up to the money received to the cent (a pool with a caisse-interne share read one cent short).
- **Console e-mails greet the mail server with the sender's domain** (specs/admin-account-management.md rule 21): login codes, export links, closing and renewal mails no longer say `EHLO [127.0.0.1]`.
- plugin:hourly-resources A paid resource booking is no longer deleted in one click: its payment is taken back first, from the booking (« Retirer le paiement », confirmed), and a delete asks for confirmation (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rules 34, 44, audit R35).
- A resource, a client or a property is no longer deleted (even forced) while a resource booking on it is paid or invoiced; a client booked on a resource is no orphan of the cleanup (rule 34).
- plugin:hourly-resources The VAT rate of a sale outside a stay is frozen at the payment: changing « TVA des ventes hors séjour » or a VAT period no longer re-books a paid sale in the accounting export nor its later invoice (rule 35).
- plugin:hourly-resources The notes or client of a paid booking save after a tariff change; an invoice whose lines no longer match the paid total is refused before Qonto (rule 36); `paid` is the truth after a rollback (rule 37).
- plugin:online-payment An invoice Qonto numbered is kept when « marquer payée » fails, and the hourly job retries the mark (rule 38); Qonto idempotency keys are UUIDs covering the billing address (rule 39); the HT lines always add up to the TTC charged, or the invoice is refused before any call (rule 40); the invoice PDF is fetched over https within the Qonto timeout (rule 42).
- plugin:hourly-resources A registered client's address goes on his invoice (rule 41); a « Payé » without method is refused for that reason (rule 43); the arrival is recorded on its day, also for a guest who paid at booking time; dialogs: invoice retry and re-issue after a Qonto cancellation work, refusals are shown, a refused payment never creates the booking twice, the planning reloads after the last step (rules 28, 44).
- plugin:hourly-resources The erase warning says which sales leave the accounting export (rule 21).
- **Accounting export: a stay paid with several methods and refunded in full leaves every account at 0** (specs/mixed-method-payments.md rule 56): the refund giving back the last of its book money now debits what is left on each sale account (706, 706010, 709, 4457, 4671) instead of its lines, so no tourist-tax cent stays on 4671 and the geste's rabais no longer stays on 709. A partial refund keeps its lines; the refund that empties the stay's sale accounts closes them, live or cancelled. accounting-export 1.2.2.
- plugin:hourly-resources « Valider l'arrivée » of a guest who paid at booking time no longer makes a Qonto invoice nobody asked for: the invoice comes only from « Créer la facture » or the ticked box; the arrival endpoint refuses an `invoice` that is not a boolean (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rule 45, audit R35b).
- plugin:hourly-resources A booking created by its dialog whose payment was refused is previewed as itself when its slot is moved, never counted against its own slot (rule 44). After « Retirer le paiement », the next save prices the booking at the live tariff, as shown in the dialog (rule 36, recorded).
- **Cancellation after a full refund of a solde paid with several methods books no phantom indemnity** (specs/mixed-method-payments.md rule 57): the cancellation reads the solde at its book share, less its geste, so its caisse-interne money and its geste no longer turn into a 7588 « acompte conservé », and the caisse lines still offer the caisse money afterwards.
- **Accounting export: a stay paid with one method and refunded in full also leaves every account at 0** (specs/mixed-method-payments.md rule 58): the closing refund applies to every stay from the month after the plugin version is installed (`stayClosingFrom`, stored once like `ttcSplitFrom`), so months already exported keep their cents. accounting-export 1.2.2.
- **A book refund dated before the stay's last book payment is refused** (specs/reservation-refunds.md rule 53): 400 `REFUND_BEFORE_PAYMENT` « Date antérieure au dernier paiement (JJ/MM/AAAA). », shown in the refund dialog; caisse-interne refunds and payments are not judged.
- A stay whose only money is a settled mid-stay note (a platform stay before its payout is ticked) is no longer deleted: the fiche, its client and its logement refuse the delete, an approved iCal cancellation keeps it as cancelled, and it holds its nights (spec `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 58, audit R36-DB-M2).
- A stay holding a caution not returned yet is no longer deleted, nor its client or logement; an approved iCal cancellation keeps it as cancelled (rule 58, audit R36-DB-L1).
- Deleting a logement, or an iCal sync, no longer deletes a client who booked a resource session; the session keeps its buyer (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rule 49, audits R36-DB-M1, R36-RES-L1).
