- A converted devis that holds no promise for its stay (an OTA devis, a direct devis converted with an acompte outside the window) can be deleted again; a direct devis owing one payment stays protected, D27 unchanged, judged with the « Solde dû » delay of the logement the stay is on now, as the stay's schedule is (spec `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 26, audit R36-RES-L2, verification R36b L1).
- plugin:hourly-resources A booking can no longer be linked to a stay through the API (400 BOOKING_STAY_LINK), and a stored stay-linked booking takes no payment (rule 46, audit R36-DB-L2); the invoice PDF is joined to the card batch even while Qonto refuses « marquer payée », which is retried for 7 days only (rule 47, audit R36-EXT-1). Requires SDK 2.26.
- plugin:online-payment The folded invoice lines sent after Qonto refused a negative discount never carry a 0 € or a negative HT line (rule 48, audit R36-EXT-2). Requires SDK 2.26.
- Test suites: the `removal: online-payment` cell is green again and the removal guard reads a sibling plugin path from a plugin's own tests (spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md` rule 21, audit R36-S1); the client suites share one 30 s test budget and a 5 s wait for every `findBy` / `waitFor` (spec `server-test-isolation.md` rules 16, 17, audit R36-S3, verification R36b); the per-field input fuzz is split in two files under the per-file cap (rule 15, audit R36-S4).
- plugin:guest-app « Retrouver mon séjour » no longer opens a stay whose number the core generated (D28, spec `guest-app.md` rules 6 and 54): a stay sold on an own channel (judged by the platform's mode, never its name) and a platform stay still carrying the core's `AAAAMM###` number answer like an unknown stay, an open lookup session on such a stay dies, and « Régénérer le lien » also ends the stay's lookup sessions. A surname was enough to walk a month of sequential direct numbers and hold the gate key and the note. +5 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app An order asking more persons than the stay can still be served is refused (409 `PERSONS_EXCEEDED`, the persons left named) instead of being billed short or confirmed at 0 € — per-person options on the note, by card (no link minted), at 0 € and in pickup mode, a pending card order's persons, the breakfast line's persons; a priced article the engine adds nothing for (a line the fiche offers) is refused, never confirmed free (rule 55). The core's `midStayOrderCommit` refuses the same order (`ORDER_PERSONS_EXCEEDED`, SDK rule 47 reading). +8 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app « Adresse de l'application » can no longer be GuestFlow's own address (rule 58): refused at save against the public URL's host and the saving request's Host and Origin, written only by the plugin's route (the generic settings route refuses it), and a stored own host is ignored; the core's loader never hands GuestFlow's public host to a plugin's web app (SDK rule 48 reading). The guest app took the whole back office over on that host. +6 server tests, +1 client test.
- plugin:guest-app Every refusal of the guest API answers in the guest's language, the rate limits' included (rule 56); « EN » re-reads the orders and the booklet in English at once (rule 19); the wifi join QR escapes the backslash (rule 18); the delivery times are real instants, right on the nights the clocks change (rule 41); the reception no longer reads the orders list and its amounts (rule 45); « Effacer aussi ses données » names the booklet pages and photos and deletes the photo files (rule 59); the operator texts of GuestFlow's guest-app screens and of the studio are impersonal. +10 server tests, +1 guest app test.
- Plugin version check (`check-plugin-versions`): a core client file a plugin's web app builds in (the studio's `@guestflow/…` imports and what they import) now asks for that plugin's bump in the pull request, and the release skill builds the plugins' web apps before it judges their packages (registry rule 28 reading). +3 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app « Retrouver mon séjour » judges the core's number by its exact shape (`20YY` + month + 3 digits, or `AAAA-MM-###`), pinned to the core's generator: a genuine 10-digit Booking number whose digits 5-6 read as a month opens its stay again instead of « Nous n'avons pas trouvé ce séjour » (spec `guest-app.md` rule 54, amended). +2 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app A per-person resource can no longer be sold in the app (rule 23, amended): the engine billed it quantity × party, so « 1 × Vélo » for 4 people cost 4 persons. A switch already on sells nothing. +2 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app « Effacer aussi ses données » deletes the photo files only once the erase has committed: a rolled-back erase keeps them (rule 59, amended). The free unit or free hour of a priced article is pinned by a route test: confirmed at 0 €, the next one billed (rules 25, 55). +4 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app A line the fiche offers is no longer confirmed at 0 € beyond the free units or minutes the stay has left: the free allowance explains a 0 € line only up to its quantity or hours, the rest is refused 409 `ITEM_UNAVAILABLE` on the quote and the order (spec `guest-app.md` rule 60). An option with 2 free units offered for 1 took an order of 4 units at 0 €. Plugin 1.0.2, SDK 2.26. +3 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app In English the quote's line labels and the refusals naming an article (its quantity cap, the persons left, an article not billable) use the article's English title, as the shop does; the order still stores the French title (rule 61). +4 server tests.
- plugin:guest-app The plugin switched off while a guest has the app open shows « Application indisponible » / « App unavailable » from any page, never the raw `PLUGIN_INACTIVE` code; `gate-access` off still leaves the app open; the studio reads « Module inactif. » (rule 63). The studio's booklet search refuses a too-short or too-long query with a French sentence instead of its refusal key (rule 62). +3 guest app tests, +2 studio tests, +1 server test.
- plugin:guest-app The booklet import script (`specs/guest-app-livret/import.mjs`) reads the operator's password without echoing it (rule 64). +2 server tests.
- plugin:hourly-resources A Qonto client is reused only for the name and billing address it was created with: a registered client whose address or name changed after an invoice, or a booking re-invoiced with a corrected address, gets a Qonto client carrying what the invoice states (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rule 50). Plugin 1.5.2, SDK 2.26. +3 server tests.
- A caution still held when a stay is cancelled can be marked returned on the cancelled fiche (« Restituer », outside its read-only fieldset): `PATCH /payment` takes `cautionReturned` and its date alone on a cancelled stay, every other write stays refused; once returned, the client and logement deletes no longer ask for an impossible return and answer the cancelled-history refusal (spec `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 59). +6 server tests, +3 client tests.
- Deleting a converted devis that still holds the stay's open payment link (or one owing the provider a deactivation or a last read) is refused 409 `DEVIS_HOLDS_LINK` while the stay lives, instead of silently withdrawing the link the guest was about to pay (spec `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 27). +4 server tests.
- A page left open while its plugin is switched off reads « Module inactif. » instead of the raw `PLUGIN_INACTIVE` code (spec `ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md` rule 53). +2 client tests.
- CI: the server unit job runs in two shards (`--test-shard`, through `npm test`'s own command), each under its 15-minute cap, after the suite reached 13.8 min and a run was cancelled at the cap; a test file cut off no longer leaves its app child running (spec `server-test-isolation.md` rules 18-19). +6 server tests.
- The check-in and check-out sas follow the caution rules of the fiche. A caution never received can no longer be marked returned at check-out (409). At check-in, « non reçue » also clears the return.
- On a cancelled stay, the caution still to give back reads « À rendre », no longer « À rendre au départ ».
- Each plugin-removal cell now gets 35 minutes, since the server suite has grown.
- The check-in sas no longer re-freezes a caution already received: the amount collected stays the one frozen at receipt, even after the property's caution changes (sas 1.9.2, SDK 2.24).
- The check-in and check-out sas read every tick as the fiche does (true, false, 1 or 0): any other value, such as the text "false", is refused with 400 `INVALID_PAYMENT_FLAG` and nothing is written; it used to mark a payment collected or a caution returned (sas 1.9.2, SDK 2.24).
- A control-plane test file cut off before its cleanup no longer leaves its GuestFlow instance running or its temp tree on disk.
- **Planning: « Aujourd'hui » reaches today even when a window request releases today's window on the way** (specs/dashboard-planning-scroll.md rule 33): after a long scroll down, the button could do nothing and leave the page far from today. The timeline is now reopened on today in that case, and the button's enabled state follows layout changes, not only scroll events.
- **A failed reservation search says so, and the search is for the administrator only; a role outside its surface never mounts the forbidden page** (specs/reservation-number-and-search.md rule 17, specs/reception-role-checkin-only.md rule 15, specs/plugins-phase-2-hosts.md rule 34): the top-bar search read « Aucune réservation » when the server failed, and was offered to reception and accountant, whom the server refuses. It now reads « Recherche indisponible. » on failure and is rendered for an administrator only. A typed URL outside a role's pages is redirected while rendering, so the page's calls no longer leave (no 403 burst, no error toast on the landing page); the accountant's shell reads the platform colours (`GET /api/properties/platform-colors` joins its allowlist) and skips the property list.
- **A slow answer for a month, a week or a resource already left no longer replaces what is on screen** (specs/cancellation-compensation.md rule 31, specs/ds-sweep-planning.md rule 31): the « Indemnités d'annulation » card could show 0,00 € for a month that holds a received compensation, and the resource planning could show a taken slot as free, when the answer of the previous month or week landed last. Only the latest request writes the page, success or failure. Plugin `hourly-resources` 1.3.2.
- **Tourist tax: no year or month past the current one can be picked** (specs/ds-sweep-finance.md rule 24): choosing next year, or this year after a later month of last year, asked the server for a future month and left a load error that « Réessayer » could not clear. The year list stops at the current year and a year change clamps the month.
- Refunds: the arrival complement's history window counts only the lines that sat in the complement, so a removed complement activity is booked on « Activités » even when a solde option was dropped earlier.
- **Mail intakes (SDK 2.16.0):** on a mailbox where no DKIM verdict can be had, the 200-mail cap now reads the newest half and the oldest half of the unread mails, so a forged flood delays a genuine booking instead of burying it (a mail waits up to 30 days under a very heavy flood, and is lost only after about 30 + 3000/(r - 200) days of a sustained flood of r forged mails per pass above 200); after two capped passes in a row the home says so (« Mails Airbnb non lus : N (afflux). » or the Gîtes de France line); `verifyDkim` lets the first header of the trusted server decide, tokenizes only that one and refuses the verification when it is over 4 KB (never falling through to a header below it), and a batch of 500 envelopes yields to the event loop (it blocked the API for up to 5 s with 64 KB headers). Spec rule 31(a) of both intake specs now states the real guarantee. `airbnb-intake` 1.1.4 and `gites-de-france-intake` 1.4.6 ask for SDK 2.16.
- Payment links: a bucket collected at arrival (the fiche's single payment or the check-in) retires its open link at once, and a link paid after that never silently re-marks the bucket or sends a second confirmation (it alerts when the arrival was dated earlier).
- Payment links: a full link retired because the acompte was ticked, paid after the stay was re-priced, books what was received with the amount-mismatch alert instead of a fixed « acompte paid twice » refund; an acompte ticked the very day of the payment is the same money and raises no refund.
- Bank reconciliation: a stay paid through the payment link of its devis is expected as a link receipt, so the Qonto payout is proposed.
- Payment links: a link paid after a payment at the door (card or cash at arrival) alerts even the same day, and the re-pricing alert of a full link reads the solde, so it states the real amount to chase or refund.
- Plugins: an update, update-all or rollback now refuses a version that closes a dependency cycle (`REQUIREMENT_CYCLE`) or, for an active plugin, requires a plugin that is installed but off (`REQUIREMENT_INACTIVE`); the bundled-row sync at boot no longer rewrites a row with a cycle-closing manifest; two installed plugins that require each other can be uninstalled one after the other; `online-payment` 1.4.4 asks for SDK 2.15 and the hardening-floor test discovers the plugins that rely on newer core code.
- Plugins: when a core update makes an active bundled plugin require a plugin that is installed but off (weather-alerts over an off SAS), the dependant is switched off at boot instead of running over it, and the home says « <Plugin> éteint : nécessite <Plugin>. » until it is switched back on; a rollback to a version whose requirement is not installed is refused (`REQUIREMENT_MISSING`). Migration: new nullable `plugins.off_reason` column.
- A devis open in the editor no longer loses the guest's new website message (nor the « modified after sending » flag) when saved: a save built on an out-of-date devis is refused with the « Fiche modifiée entre-temps » dialog (reload / save anyway / cancel), like the reservation fiche.
- Two overlapping iCal syncs of one source (5-minute pass, « Synchroniser », « Synchroniser tout ») now share one run, so a single empty answer from a platform can no longer be counted twice and wipe the source's mappings.
- Google Agenda plugin 1.2.3: a push and a delete of the same stay run in order, so a cancellation arriving while the stay is being pushed no longer leaves its event in the calendar. A Google call that never answers is now aborted after 20 s and retried at the next sync, instead of holding the stay's queue for minutes.
- A devis naming a client that no longer exists is refused (« Un client est requis pour enregistrer le devis. ») instead of failing with an internal error. A price calculation or a calendar note on a logement that no longer exists answers « Logement non trouvé ».
- Saving, or adding a document to, a logement that no longer exists now answers « Logement non trouvé ». The save used to report success while saving nothing, and the document attempt failed with an internal error. Neither leaves a stray file behind any more.
- Booking intake: « Ignorer » refuses a fiche number that names no stay. Such a dismissal used to hide the stay that later received that number.
- **Audit round 42: an id that names nothing, or a field nobody judged, is refused before anything is written.**
  - Painting a period into a new season on a logement deleted from another tab answers 404 « Logement non trouvé », no longer 500 (`specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 26 reading).
  - A property document's type is one of the page's three and its name one text of at most 200 characters; a repeated, bracketed or oversized field answers 400 and the uploaded file is removed, no longer 500 with an orphan private file (`specs/settings-input-guards.md` rule 63).
  - On a property create or update, the e-mail hooks, Wi-Fi and acompte switches and the cleaning / towels / bed-linen answers are judged with the rest of the form: no more « [object Object] » in a guest e-mail, no logement half-saved or created by a refused request (rule 64).
  - Bank reconciliation « Écarter » and « Hors réservation » refuse a stay or a credit that does not exist, 404, nothing stored (online-payment 1.7.5, SDK 2.26; `specs/qonto-bank-reconciliation.md` rule 51).
  - The Google Vacation Rentals, Airbnb, Gîtes de France and Booking settings refuse a logement that does not exist in their id-keyed maps; a deleted logement's stored line still saves (google-vacation-rentals 1.0.5, SDK 2.11; airbnb-intake 1.1.9 and gites-de-france-intake 1.4.11, SDK 2.21; booking-intake 1.3.14, SDK 2.15).
  - Booking « Ignorer » answers a pending proposal only: an intake already applied keeps its status and banner, 409 (booking-intake 1.3.14, SDK 2.15; `specs/booking-extranet-intake.md` rule 39).
  - A cancelled stay with an installment plan no longer shows « Reste à percevoir » for the installments its cancellation wrote off (`specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md` rule 73).
  - The e-mail preferences page declares `lang="en"` when it speaks English (`specs/guest-email-sequence.md` rule 37).
- **Audit round 43: the cancel dialog shows what the cancellation will write; cautions, refusals and plugin keys read true.**
  - « Annuler le séjour » shows « Conservé en indemnité » and « Solde abandonné » as the server computes them for the cancel itself (`GET /reservations/:id/cancel-preview`): a stay with an installment plan, a refund before the cancellation, a caisse-interne acompte or withheld commissions no longer reads 0,00 € or the gross acompte; the confirm waits for the figures and a refusal is shown instead (`specs/payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 60). The dashboard's « conservé si annulation » reads the same figure.
  - A cancelled stay whose caution was never received reads « Caution · Sans objet », no longer « À recevoir à l'arrivée » (`specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md` rule 74).
  - The closure refusal of a stay and the season overlap refusals write their days as 04/10/2027, no longer 2027-10-04 (`specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 27).
  - The logement maps of the Booking (booking-intake 1.3.15, SDK 2.15), Gîtes de France (gites-de-france-intake 1.4.12, SDK 2.21), Airbnb (airbnb-intake 1.1.10, SDK 2.21) and Google Vacation Rentals (google-vacation-rentals 1.0.6, SDK 2.11) settings refuse a logement written with leading zeros (« 01 ») « Logement inconnu. ».
  - The indemnity stated before and after a cancellation includes a commission withheld on a paid solde, as booked.
  - Operator e-mails (new reservation, dates taken, payment) and the tariff recipe warnings (tariff-recipes 1.4.5, SDK 2.5) write days as 04/10/2027.
- **Cancelling from the fiche** (`specs/payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` §6, audits R44-C-L1, R44-C-L2): a failed cancel keeps the typed reason and an unticked « Prévenir le client par email » (a retry re-ticked it and could mail the guest); « Annuler le séjour » and « Supprimer » wait for unsaved changes to be saved, « Enregistrer d'abord les modifications. », as « Transformer en devis » does.
- **`PUT /reservations/:id` on a stay that does not exist answers 404 « Réservation non trouvée »** (`specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 26 reading, audit R44-C-L3), never a 500 from the line inserts.
- **The cancellation notice states the sum the cancellation kept** (`specs/payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 61, audits R44-DB-M1, R44-M-L1, R44-RES-L1). The e-mail worked out « l'acompte de X reste acquis » as the whole acompte when paid: after a book refund it overstated the sum kept, on a plan it said nothing of the échéances kept. `{{retainedDepositAmount}}` now reads the cancellation's own `keptFromGuest` (the indemnity booked plus a caisse-interne acompte kept), stored on the cancel entry (« Somme conservée ») and answered by `POST /cancel`; a reminder on a live stay reads what cancelling it now would keep. The seeded wording names « la somme … déjà versée » / « the amount … already paid ».
- **The fiche history writes the single payment the French way** (`specs/single-payment-from-the-fiche.md` rule 9 reading, audit R44-RES-L2): « 281,98 € — CB / Chèque le 01/09/2026 — solde, complément de fin de séjour », never an ISO day, a dot decimal or `balance` / `endOfStayComplement`, with no « vide → »; the same for its undo, its dissolution, the adjusted total and the sas's folded line (sas 1.9.3, SDK 2.24). Lines stored before read back the same way; the cancel entry's amounts read « 142,16 € ». Two refusals (mid-stay note, refund stay cap) write a comma decimal.
- **The sum a cancellation notice states reads every refund right** (`specs/payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rules 61 and 62, verification R44 L1-L2). A caisse-interne acompte kept by a cancellation now gives back only what the caisse refunds leave over once every other caisse money (a mixed solde's caisse share, a complement paid in the caisse) has absorbed them; a notice sent after a refund made since the cancel states the sum kept less what that refund took from it, never below 0. The email editor labels `{{retainedDepositAmount}}` / `hasRetainedDeposit` « Somme conservée » / « Si somme conservée ».
- A caisse refund made before a cancellation, and counted in the sum kept, can no longer be deleted from the cancelled fiche.
- **Cancel dialog shows the sum the guest is told is kept** (spec `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 60 reading, audits R45-C-L1 / R45-RES-L2). The cancel preview answers `keptFromGuest`; when it differs from the indemnity (an acompte kept in the caisse interne), the dialog adds « Somme conservée (indiquée au client) », the dashboard `cancel_due` row names it, and both success messages state it.
- **Cancel history « Motif » reads French** (rule 23 reading, audits R45-RES-L1 / R45-DB-L1): a cancellation confirmed with no typed reason read `unpaid_balance`; the derived keys now read « Acompte impayé », « Solde impayé », « Annulation manuelle », the stored row untouched.
- **Cancellation notice no longer blames non-payment** (rule 63, audit R45-DB-L2): the seeded French and English notice opened with « Faute de règlement du solde » / « As the balance was not paid » for every cancellation, including a guest who paid and phoned to cancel.
- **Legacy SAS single-payment history lines read French** (spec `single-payment-from-the-fiche.md` rule 9 reading, audit R45-PL-L1): lines stored before sas 1.9.3 as rendered text (« 144.95 € … le 2026-09-01 — solde, complément ») now read « 144,95 € … le 01/09/2026 — solde, complément d'arrivée »; idempotent, stored rows untouched.
- Suivi financier: switching KPI tiles quickly no longer crashes the page, and a slow answer for a logement already left no longer takes over; the same guard protects the booking-pace card (no more wrong units), the Rapprochement « Actualiser », the Devis and Options filters and the e-mail sequence simulation.
- Operator texts: the remaining « tu » imperatives are rewritten in the short impersonal style, and the wording guard now catches them.
- **Mail intakes (SDK 2.17.0):** a pass no longer keeps the sender-written envelopes of every unread unauthenticated mail in memory (it kept about 200 MB for 6 000 forged mails with 32 KB subjects); only small records are kept, and the Message-ID used as the row key and the subject are capped at 998 characters. The internal counters of `airbnb-intake` and `gites-de-france-intake` (`lastRunAt`, `unreadMails`, `unreadStreak`) are now `internal` settings: refused by `PUT /api/plugins/:id/settings` and hidden from `GET`. `airbnb-intake` 1.1.5 and `gites-de-france-intake` 1.4.7 ask for SDK 2.17.
- **Mail intakes:** a forged or exotic `Date` header that cannot be parsed no longer makes every pass throw (it blocked `gites-de-france-intake` and `airbnb-intake` for 30 days); the date is kept only if it parses and a mail that makes the intake step throw is stored as `ignored` with its reason. `website-booking` (`proofEnforcedSince`, `lastSeenPluginVersion`) and `google-calendar` (connection and sync stamps) declare their internal state as `internal` settings (SDK 2.17), and `booking-intake` refuses a settings PUT naming its webhook secret.
- **Online payment — an old full link paid the day the acompte is ticked, after the stay was re-priced, is booked the same whichever comes first** (spec `plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 45, audit R5 M1). The tick no longer counts as a second payment: no silent 100 € uncollected when the stay grew, no over-stated trop-perçu when it shrank, and the alert compares with the whole amount. The « autre montant » e-mail no longer ends with « une fois la différence réglée » when nothing is left to settle (rule 46). Plugin `online-payment` 1.4.6. +9 server tests.
- Plugins: an update or rollback of a plugin is refused when an active plugin that needs it declares a version range the new version does not satisfy (`REQUIRED_BY_RANGE`), on the card, the endpoints and « Tout mettre à jour » (which now judges cycles and ranges with every target applied). The unmet-range label no longer calls a version above the range « plus récente ».
- Plugins: the home's « plugin éteint » notice ends as soon as the plugin row is touched after the switch-off, so a core rollback followed by manual toggles no longer leaves a false alert.
- Plugins: activating a plugin whose requirement is installed outside its declared range is refused; « Tout mettre à jour » reports the real refusal instead of « Aucune mise à jour »; a refused rollback names the right remedy; a core update that strands a registry-installed dependant over a bundled requirement switches that dependant off at boot, with the home line.
- Plugins: the home line for a plugin switched off over a requirement installed at the wrong version says what is needed and asks to update it; an unrelated core sync no longer switches off a plugin over another requirement that was already off; the activation refusal for a requirement out of range is `REQUIREMENT_OUT_OF_RANGE`.
- **iCal sync no longer applies a stale feed** (spec `ical-sync-mapping-resilience.md` rule 8, audit R5-1, 2026-10-30). The 5-minute pass fetches every feed at once and applies them in turn; a feed fetched before an operator sync of the same source is now dropped, and one outage seen by both is counted once, so it no longer wipes a source's mappings nor raises a false « annulation à valider » for a stay just created.
- plugin:google-calendar The full reconcile pushes each stay in the per-reservation order and re-reads it first, so a stay cancelled or edited while it runs is not recreated or reverted in Google (spec `google-calendar-oauth-rework.md` rule 28, audit R5-2, 2026-10-30).
- The email send dialog no longer keeps the previous row's template: opened from the pending queue or from the history « Renvoyer », it starts clean for each row, so two quick sends can no longer leave the wrong email. A preview that answers late for a template just left is dropped, and « Envoyer » waits for the preview of the template picked.
- The hourly-resources booking dialog drops the late occupied-slots answer of the date it just left (hourly-resources 1.3.3).
- The home line for a plugin switched off because a requirement is out of range now reads « À mettre à jour puis réactiver depuis la page Plugins », since the plugin stays off after the update.
- Console: a fleet update no longer restarts a customer deprovisioned or erased after it started (the item is skipped), and the deprovision export and the erasure no longer overlap an update's snapshot or rollback.
- **Neat — bounded calls** (spec `neat-cancellation-insurance-subscription.md` rule 17, round-6 audit N6 and review D1). Each Neat client call has one 5 s budget shared by auth, request and 401 retry (headers and body read included), and the quote processor reuses one client and token per account. After an outage (timeout, network error, 5xx) guest pricing skips Neat for 60 s; a 4xx never opens that breaker. A hanging or degraded Neat no longer holds the public quote or the fiche preview beyond the WordPress plugin's 8 s: the fallback ladder (stale cache, then static tariff) runs inside it. Neat plugin 1.3.3.
- **A payment link paid after the stay was re-priced now alerts the admin** (« autre montant »). For an acompte link the alert says what was booked (« Acompte enregistré à X ; solde porté à Z ») instead of a false trop-perçu, and the confirmation is still sent; it says « le séjour » rather than « le devis » on a stay's own link.
- Console: an archived customer is never served again by mistake. « Réessayer » on a failed restart, process restart or rename is no longer offered (and is refused) once the customer is archived; a « Restaurer à une date » that reaches the customer after a deprovision closed it is refused instead of restarting the instance; a rename or a restore sent while a deprovision runs is refused (« Fermeture en cours. ») instead of moving the closed space to a new address or replacing the database just exported; a second deprovision sent while one runs no longer exports and mails a second time; closing a space while its address change is still waiting is refused (« Changement d’adresse à terminer d’abord. »), and erasing a customer removes the route of every old address it still holds, so an old address never serves the next customer's space (specs/hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md rule 38).
- **A silent push endpoint no longer stalls the payment poll** (spec `pwa-push-notifications.md` rule 14, audit R7 N7, 2026-10-30). Each web push send is bounded at 10 s (web-push socket timeout plus a hard deadline) and counts as failed past it, so the online-payment poll, the Qonto webhook, the Neat pass and the per-minute push runners can no longer stay blocked behind a push endpoint that accepts the connection and never answers.
- plugin:google-calendar The token revocation of « Déconnecter » carries the same 20 s bound as the other Google calls, so a silent Google endpoint no longer holds the disconnect request (spec `google-calendar-oauth-rework.md` rule 30, audit R7, 2026-10-30).
- **An acompte collected by a payment link can no longer be erased by « Sans acompte ».** A fiche still holding
  the switch from before the link was booked (saved with « Enregistrer quand même », by a script, or in the same
  second) zeroed the acompte, its payment and its journal entry again. The save and the live quote now keep an
  acompte a paid link collected; an acompte ticked by hand without a link can still be switched off. Review of
  PR #893 (`specs/disable-deposit-per-reservation.md` rule 13).
- **An acompte link paid after the solde was collected no longer lowers the paid solde.** The acompte is booked
  on top, the excess shows as a trop-perçu to refund, and the admin alert says « solde déjà perçu » and asks for
  the refund, with no confirmation sent to the guest. Review of PR #893 (`specs/plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md`
  rule 49).
- **« Sans acompte » is greyed out on an acompte a payment link collected, with the reason under it.** The switch
  used to look free, the summary previewed the acompte as gone, and the save silently put it back. A refused switch
  also keeps the acompte's recorded amount and deadline, so the history no longer reports a cleared acompte or a
  new deadline. Re-review of PR #893 (`specs/disable-deposit-per-reservation.md` rule 13).
- **An acompte link paid after the stay was switched to « Sans acompte » no longer loses the money.** When the
  Qonto deactivation failed or the guest paid in between, the payment was booked as a paid acompte the fiche hid,
  and the next save erased it from the stay and from the journal. The booking now re-enables the acompte
  (history « Acompte réactivé : payé par un lien retiré »), lowers the solde by as much, and the admin alert says
  so. Audit R7 M1 (`specs/disable-deposit-per-reservation.md` rule 12, `specs/plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md`
  rule 48).
- **Test suites no longer go red with the calendar** (audit R7 M-1). Ten server tests and nine client tests carried literal dates that the clock was about to pass (the first on 2026-11-07, the finance ones every New Year); their dates are now relative to today, or the clock is pinned where the test is about a calendar year.
- **The E2E servers reach no external service** (audit R7 L-1, spec `e2e-playwright-smoke-suite.md` rule 23). `GUESTFLOW_DISABLE_OUTBOUND=1`, set by every Playwright server and honoured only under `NODE_ENV=test` (a production instance carrying it by mistake keeps checking), skips the GitHub release check (`self-update-and-releases.md` rule 51) and the school-holidays sync (`school-holidays.md` rule 27, plugin 1.0.4), so a run no longer depends on GitHub or `data.education.gouv.fr`.
- **A hanging server test fails instead of stalling CI** (audit R7 L-2, `server-test-isolation.md` rule 5). The server and control-plane suites run with `--test-timeout` and `--test-force-exit`, so a test that never settles fails and is named — on Node 22 (per file) as on the CI's Node 24 (per test) — instead of the job being cancelled at its 15-minute limit.
- Console: « Réactiver » and « Annuler l’effacement » are refused (« Effacement en cours. ») while an erasure of that customer is queued or running, instead of being accepted and then lost when the erasure deleted everything; the erasure also re-reads the customer once it holds the lock and leaves one no longer archived (or whose scheduled erasure was cancelled or moved later) untouched, and a second « Effacer maintenant » queued behind the first answers « effacé » instead of blanking the page (specs/hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md rule 39).
- **Devis — dated and expired on the operator's day** (specs `devis-pdf-and-tourist-tax-fixes.md` rule 23,
  `devis-extras-parity-and-price-lock.md` rule 21, audit R8-1, 2026-10-30). Between 00:00 and 01:00 /
  02:00 in Paris the devis model still read the UTC date: a devis created then carried one day less than
  the configured validity and its PDF printed the previous « Date du devis », and a devis expired the day
  before kept its frozen prices and could not be extended. The model and the PDF now read the local day,
  like the rest of the server; `createdAt` stays a UTC timestamp. +10 server tests.
- **Payments — a request refused because the old link was just paid re-reads the fiche** (audit R8, `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 50): « Renvoyer la demande », the solde request, an installment's « Envoyer la demande », the devis payment request and a request e-mail sent from the fiche's dialog reload the money after 409 `LINK_ALREADY_PAID`, so the fiche shows the payment instead of offering the request again and the next save no longer meets « Fiche modifiée entre-temps ».
- **Payments — a request on the arrival day no longer reuses a link that lapses 15 minutes later** (rule 51): an open pre-arrival link minted before the arrival day lapses (`stay-started`) and a fresh link is minted; the e-mail preview shows the placeholder in that case.
- **Plugins — the online-payment plugin cannot be removed while a lapsed link's last payment check is pending** (rule 52): deactivate and uninstall answer 409 `LINKS_LAST_LOOK_PENDING`.
- **Payments — links minted by an older release during a rollback get their 36 h at the next boot** (rule 53): the validity backfill now repairs, at every boot, the open links with no known end.
- **Website booking — the pay endpoint no longer shows operator sentences to visitors** (rule 54): a link paid meanwhile answers « already confirmed » and an unreachable provider the provider-error wording, in the visitor's language.
- **Reservation — « Sans acompte » can be unticked when the acompte-held lock arrives on a ticked switch** (`disable-deposit-per-reservation.md` rule 14).
- **Reservation — a refused request that booked a payment no longer makes the next save stale** (`reservation-fiche-concurrency.md` rule 12): the client adopts the versions a refused `/api/payments` or `/api/emails` write moved.
- **Payment schedule — booked on the operator's day** (specs `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rule 54,
  `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 50, review of PR #899, 2026-10-30). The schedule read the booking
  day as the first ten characters of the UTC `createdAt`: a devis created between 00:00 and 01:00 / 02:00
  in Paris, 30 days before arrival, was sold as one single payment and flipped to a 30 % acompte with the
  solde due before it at its first unchanged save; a reservation's fiche showed that acompte too. The
  schedule, the pace's booking and cancellation days (iCal ledger included), the history window of a
  collected complement, the earliest arrival-payment date and the Clients « Créé le » now read the local
  day. +14 server tests.
- **Test suites no longer go red with the calendar, again** (audit R8 M-1 and its review, `server-test-isolation.md` rule 6). The « Sans acompte » suites added by #893, the converted-devis double-payment suite and the acompte due-date suite carried stays of 2027 (first red on 2027-05-09); the review found older fuses — the trop-perçu, session-overlap, stored-ledger and platform-solde suites and the whole reservation-save fixture family, first red on 2027-01-24, then 2027-02-12, 2027-05-25 and 2027-07-21; its second review found the next ones from 2027-03-11 — the client-duplicate, overpayment-by-poste, superseded-calendar, cot-line, nameless-iCal and Lodgify-decommission suites, the split-fallback suite of the accounting export, and the whole stored-ledger fixture family. Their dates are now built from today. The files named are green to 2030-01-02 under Paris and Los Angeles time (one mixed-clock test aside); the whole server suite, swept at 2027-03-11, 2027-05-25, 2027-07-21 and 2027-10-05, is red only on the seven tests a JS-only clock shift cannot fake (SQLite's own `now`).
- **A test line with a literal date fails the pull request** (`server-test-isolation.md` rule 7). `node scripts/check-test-dates.mjs --base <ref> --strict` names the dates from today on in the test lines a PR adds, and the `spec rules` job fails on them; `// date-literal-ok: <why>` marks a date nothing compares with today. A clock pinned or injected in one test (`vi.setSystemTime`, a stubbed `getTodayIsoDate`, a `NOW` constant, a `now:` handed to the code) exempts that test only, a pin in a helper the tests that call it, and a timer mock that leaves `Date` real exempts nothing. A line already on master or inte/plugins is not named again (`--known`, rule 9), so the integration PR and the syncs are not blocked by lines merged on the other branch.
- **The client unit suite runs in three shards, on PRs and in the release** (audit R8 M-2, `server-test-isolation.md` rule 8, `self-update-and-releases.md` rule 52). It had reached 711–798 s of its 15-minute cap, and the release ran server and client one after the other under 20 minutes; the release now runs the server suite and the three client shards side by side, and packages after all of them.
- **Console — « Réessayer » on a failed closing mail never sends a dead link** (audit R9 N1, specs `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 40 and `control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rule 45). The retry was offered on an archived customer and mailed the latest export link without looking at it: during an erasure it sent a link the erasure was about to kill, and after the 30 days it sent one already answering `410`, while the step read « Envoyé ». The retry is now refused with « Effacement en cours. » while an erasure is queued or running, runs inside the customer's lock, produces a fresh export when the link has expired or lost its archive, and fails with its reason when there is no database left. A retry queued behind the customer's lock does nothing when « Réactiver » or another tab's retry ran first, so an active customer never gets a fresh export nor a « a été fermé » mail. The mail writes the link's real last day (« jusqu’au JJ/MM/AAAA inclus ») instead of « pendant 30 jours ». +11 console tests.
- Payment links: a platform stay (Booking, Airbnb, Gîtes de France…) never gets a direct payment link any more. The e-mail dialog, the payment routes and the échéance are refused with « Réservation plateforme : aucun lien de paiement. » before any link is created; before, picking « Demande de solde » on a platform fiche mailed the guest a real link.
- Home « Échéances de paiement »: when « Relancer » finds that the payment has just been received, the card re-reads its rows and the row goes away. The refusal now keeps its code (409) instead of a generic 502.
- Payment links: clicking again within the hour after an expired link was deactivated reads that link first, so a payment that arrived late is recorded instead of a second link being sent.
- E-mail dialog: when a payment request cannot leave (platform stay, closed nights, payment to settle), the preview says why and « Envoyer » stays disabled.
- **Platform price check and two date labels — read on the operator's day** (specs
  `reservation-payment-ledger.md` rule 65, `direct-installments.md` rule 49, `guest-email-sequence.md`
  rule 36, `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 50, audit R9-1, 2026-10-30). The « prix plateforme hors
  grille » alert compared the tariff journal with the first ten characters of the UTC `createdAt`: a
  platform stay booked between 00:00 and 01:00 / 02:00 in Paris lost the alert whenever the tariff moved
  earlier that same day. « Demandée le … » on an installment and « Déjà envoyé le … » in the sequence
  simulation showed the day before for a request or a mail sent in that window. All three now read the
  local day. The journal side keeps a manual entry on the wall time typed in the form, so a platforms change
  declared late in the evening no longer silences the alert of a stay booked after it the same evening.
  +4 server tests.
- **A server suite can no longer report green with tests left unrun** (audit R9 M-1, `server-test-isolation.md` rules 5 and 10). Under load, `--test-force-exit` ended a test file's process before its last tests and the run stayed green — on GitHub CI 12 tests of `sas-commit.unit.test.js` went missing from a green job. The server and control-plane suites drop it: an exit watchdog now fails a file still alive 30 s after its last test (a hanging test still fails, named, on Node 22 and 24), and a completeness reporter fails the run, naming the tests, whenever a file's process ends before reporting every test it declared.
- **A planning test no longer fails on a loaded machine** (audit R9 L-1). `ResourcePlanningPage.stale-bookings` waits up to 5 s for the page's first paint instead of 1 s.
- **Email templates keep their « last modified » date across restarts**: the reservation-number recap migration no longer rewrites `updatedAt` of reminders it does not change.
- **Deleting a stay, devis, client or property is guarded everywhere** (spec `payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md` rules 49-52). `DELETE /api/reservations/:id` refuses cancelled stays, duplicates set aside and devis (409), and any stay carrying money — including money left on a devis converted into it (collected bucket, paid link, refund, penalty, compensation): « Un paiement est enregistré sur ce séjour : l'annuler plutôt que le supprimer. ». The forced client delete and the property delete refuse the same way; a devis carrying money is kept; an iCal cancellation approval cancels and keeps a stay carrying money instead of deleting it. Open payment links of a deleted row (and of its converted devis) are deactivated at the provider first; a link that cannot be deactivated refuses the delete. Open links pointing at a missing reservation are logged at boot.
- Direct stays read « Direct » in the reservation fiche's « Plateforme » menu and on the calendar week view, and the old « Tarifs » address no longer opens a tab of an absent plugin.
- **An instance without a plugin no longer takes, or speaks of, what that plugin brings** (specs/plugins-phase-0-foundation.md rules 29–30, found by the new bare-install E2E run, rule 28). The « Accueil » role can no longer be given without the « Arrivée et départ guidés » plugin, nor « Comptable » without « Export comptable » — the server refuses it, as the role picker already hid it; a role an account already holds is kept. An empty planning now reads « Rien de prévu sur les 14 prochains jours. » instead of naming resource slots an instance may not have.
- **Calendars — the logement calendar shows every stay « Tous » shows** (spec `calendar-property-parity.md`, 2026-10-28). Both read one `GET /api/calendar`; a platform « Désactivé » for a logement no longer hides its stays anywhere (the fiche's date-conflict hints included). The logement calendar scrolls like « Tous » (sticky month titles, each month loaded once, « Aujourd'hui » the only navigation; no more month arrows) and, on a phone, is a day agenda with coloured ribbons instead of a week pager. Guest names are readable again: one ribbon per week across the stay, never clipped.
- **Calendars — solid, distinct colours; closures on their logement's row.** One hue per platform with a text colour the server picks for contrast (past stays fade to a tint); a closure is a hatched bar in its logement's lane with a stay's half-day edges, on both calendars. Paramètres → Plateformes refuses a colour the calendar cannot print legibly and keeps the previous one.
- Caution: a single request marking the deposit both received (without a date) and returned on an earlier day is refused, as a return before its receipt always was, instead of recording a return dated before the receipt.
- Fleet console: a renewal invoice still in preparation is re-priced from the customer's current plan before Qonto is called (a plan change no longer blocks it with `AMOUNT_MISMATCH`), the shared login directory no longer lets a space list itself next to another customer's recorded contact address, and the plugin registry index can be signed with its own `CP_REGISTRY_PRIVATE_KEY` (the licence key is still the fallback).
- Fleet console: « Désinstaller » is offered only for a module that holds no data (a warning line with a count, such as paid hourly bookings, now counts as data), a module whose data the space could not count offers no action, the licence's removal orders carry `removal: true` so they never purge, and switching a module back on after the space already ran its erase order is journaled as « déjà effacées » instead of « annulé ».
- **Console (control plane), review fixes — billing:** « Prolonger » no longer leads to a second invoice: a customer has one unsettled invoice at a time, its reminders follow the customer's real end date, and its payment adds to the extended date. An invoice Qonto totals differently is held until « Réessayer la facture » instead of being numbered and cancelled every night; every Qonto creation call carries an idempotency key; an email leaves once even on two clicks; a payment recorded by hand counts once (`409 STALE` on a double click), also settles an invoice still in preparation, and gives way to a card payment that just landed.
- **Console, operator access:** a wrong password no longer locks the account (only wrong codes do), a TOTP code is accepted once, changing the second factor asks for the password, and backup codes are hashed like passwords.
- **Console, deprovisioning:** nothing is erased before « Processus et route arrêtés » is ticked, the export archive is deleted with its link (after 30 days, at reactivation, at erasure), and an unfinished rename blocks deprovisioning; until the directory is moved the login page sends users to the old address.
- **Console, catalogue:** prices and quotas are read by the server as typed (an emptied price or « 1,5 » units is refused) and the confirmation lists every price and quota the change touches.
- **First administrator script:** refuses a database at another version instead of migrating it.
- **Online payment — a converted devis's open links are no longer invisible to the fiche** (`plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rules 30-32, audit R3): the fiche lists, reuses and retires them, and a second payment of the same stay or acompte, whoever owns the link, is booked once with a refund row and an admin alert. An unknown link type such as `constructor` now gets the ordinary 400.
- **Dates can be typed on the keyboard again** (specs `datepicker-close-on-select.md` reading of 2026-11-02, `establishment-closures.md` rule 21; audit R19-L1). `DateField` blurred the native date input after every change, so in Chromium the first digit of the year dropped the focus (2028 → 0002) on every fiche date field; it now blurs only after a calendar pick, and holds its `min` / `max` while a date is being typed, until the blur or a click on the field (the fiche's bounds moved with the typed value and emptied the field). On the closures page, a start date typed in « Début de fermeture » no longer opens the « Réouverture » calendar mid-typing; a pick in the calendar still does.
- **Quote PDF: the « Surcoût voyageurs » row no longer prints the tier detail, and no character prints as garbage or as an empty box** (specs/tariff-events-and-extra-guest-tiers/spec.md §6, reading of 2026-11-05; specs/ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md rules 46–48): the row reads « Surcoût voyageurs (N pers. au-delà du tarif de base) » only, in French and English — the « 15,00 € la 1ʳᵉ nuit, puis 8,00 €/nuit » that followed it printed as « 1+1Ô’ àuit » on an L'Estiva quote, because the PDF standard face has no « ʳ » nor « ᵉ ». Direction marks, joiners, tabs, carriage returns and odd spaces pasted into a name, an address, a phone number or an option title no longer print as empty boxes; a fractional quantity prints « 0,33 » instead of « 0.3333333333333333 » running into the next row; a long bank account name or IBAN wraps inside its frame instead of under the BIC.
- **Mail intakes (Airbnb, Gîtes de France, Booking)** no longer let the text a guest types steer what is written. Airbnb: only the structured part of the mail after « Envoyer un message à » is read, the confirmation code must match the reservation link and the arrival the subject, and a repeated key header refuses the mail (airbnb-intake 1.1.1). Gîtes de France: every field is read on its own labelled line, dates must be real and the stay at most 120 nights (gites-de-france-intake 1.4.1). Booking: a cancelled fiche or another platform's fiche is never matched, several candidates are refused, and the e-mail must be a single address (booking-intake 1.3.6). The « Voir sur Airbnb » link accepts only exact Airbnb hosts. A cancelled fiche is no longer matched by the Gîtes de France intake either.
- **Typing an arrival year over a stored fiche no longer freezes the tab** (`reservations-backend-mvc.md` rule 14). Chromium hands the fiche each digit of a year typed over a complete date (0002, 0020, 0203…); the fiche's occupancy check walked the range night by night and wrote year 2 as « 2-10-31 », so it never ended. It now scans the occupied days and pads the year.
- A foreign write landing while a stay save or an installment change waits for the payment provider is no longer adopted by the open fiche: its next save is refused as modified in the meantime.
- **Final code review, low findings** (2026-10-18). The email draft preview picks its stays and dates its sample on the operator's calendar day, not the UTC one (one day behind between midnight and 02:00 in Paris); the email runners, push runners and scheduler share `utils/localDate` instead of private `isoToday` copies (specs `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 40). `/api/version` no longer tells which plugin loaded to a loopback caller on an instance without a proxy secret — behind a local reverse proxy that was every visitor (`hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 34). The console refuses a write whose `Origin`/`Referer` names another host, instances included, with the instance's own same-origin gate; Qonto's webhook is exempt, and the shared login page on `app.<domain>` sends `Referrer-Policy: same-origin` so a browser posts its forms with their real `Origin` instead of `null` (`control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rule 36). The website connection and gate connector secret routes are declared once, by their plugins (`hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 31). Duplicate `getToday` removed from the client API. +3 server tests, +6 console tests.
- **Start assistant: the recommended plugins activate on the first click** (specs/plugins-phase-p-productisation.md rule 37, final display sweep 2026-10-18 N-1). On a fresh install step « Plugins » always failed on the second preselected plugin with `UPDATE_IN_PROGRESS`: the first install answered before it released the plugin lock. The step now waits for each install to finish before the next, and a plugin left out is named with its reason. +5 server tests.
- **Start assistant wording** (rule 38): « Prix par nuit » is required and an empty price reads « Prix obligatoire »; step 4 is « Emails »; the last step reads « 2 plugins actifs », counted by the server.
- **Touch targets and sentence case** (rule 39): 44 px hit areas for « Détail », « + compl. », « Fiche » and client-name links on touch screens; toggle buttons in sentence case; « Plus tard » no longer breaks in the second-code banner.
- **A plugin's page while the plugin is off** (rule 40): « Plugin inactif » with a link to Plugins instead of « Page introuvable », for administrators (`GET /api/plugins/page`).
- **Impersonal, plain settings texts** (rule 41): no « vous / votre » left in settings, account and accounting texts; the SMTP labels become « Serveur d'envoi »…, the security mode is folded under « Avancé ».
- **Console on a phone** (specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md rules 37–38): the top menu keeps two entries and a « Plus » menu; the « Création » card names no host path and folds once done; card actions wrap under their title; « Paiements en ligne » keeps its title.
- plugin:online-payment « Paiements en ligne » keeps its title on a phone; the Qonto help names no header.
- plugin:weather-alerts The key field reads « Clé Météo-France (Vigilance) ».
- plugin:website-booking The WordPress connection text says the fields carry the same names.
- plugin:accounting-export Impersonal texts.
- plugin:linen Impersonal texts.
- plugin:google-calendar Impersonal texts.
- plugin:sas Impersonal texts.
- **« Plugin inactif » only while the plugin is off** (specs/plugins-phase-p-productisation.md rule 42): an unknown address under an active plugin's page reads « Page introuvable »; the page of an installed, disabled plugin — and Intégrations with none of its plugins active — shows « Plugin inactif » to an administrator instead of sending home; leaving the start assistant after a package install loads the app again so the new pages are there. +3 server tests, +2 client tests.
- A trop-perçu refunded after a paid stay is reduced is counted once: the paid solde keeps the amount received and the refund is the only reduction, so the accounting journal, finance « Encaissé » and the fiche's « Total perçu » all read received − refunded, and the tourist-tax account never goes negative (`reservation-refunds.md` rule 36, `accountant-accounting-export.md` rule 34, final review N1).
- « En retard » and the home dashboard count a late platform payout at what the platform owes (solde net of its commission), the solde part of « À encaisser » (`finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 41, N2).
- The « Taxe de séjour » line of a Qonto payment link is the stay's tax to the cent; the rounding cent rides on its own « Ajustement » line (`payment-links-vat.md` rule 9, N3).
- plugin:accounting-export the TTC-share split of 2026-10-18 (rule 32) applies from the first day of the month after accounting-export 1.0.5 is installed (stored once by its migration, fallback 1 November 2026): re-exporting any month production exported before gives the accountant the same cents (Kappa 624,54 / 62,46, Sigma 40,34 / 11,23, Lima 117,28 / 11,72) (`accountant-accounting-export.md` rules 33 and 35, N4).
- Guest emails greet an iCal placeholder client neutrally, « Bonjour, » / « Hello, », instead of « Bonjour Ical, » (`email-automation.md` rule 19, N5).
- Refund dialog: amounts accept « 12,5 », a typed amount never turns the stepper into a fractional quantity (« Montant libre »), the ceiling shows under each amount and the refusal reason sits right above the save button (`reservation-refunds.md` rule 37).
- A platform stay has no trop-perçu: a paid solde follows the platform amount the operator corrects, with no 409 and no trop-perçu on the fiche (`reservation-refunds.md` rule 38). Money kept on a direct stay is priced as a free line or the adjusted price, which closes the trop-perçu (rule 39).
- A late platform payout of 0 € (commission at or above the solde) no longer shows in « En retard » or on the home dashboard (`finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 42).
- **Guest app « Mon séjour », server review** (spec `guest-app.md` rules 4, 6, 11, 25, 27bis, 33-37quater, 41, 43, 45, 47, §4.3, §5). A card order awaiting its payment holds the free units and minutes it was quoted with (`priceOrder(…, { pending })`); its payment never bills the fiche beyond what was paid — a price raised meanwhile makes the order « Payée · à rembourser », a price lowered flags only the gap « à rembourser » (`basketJson.refundCents`, history and ledger row); a basket paid after its stay was cancelled still reaches its order; an abandoned order paid late is never committed. `POST /public/v1/guest/orders` honours an `Idempotency-Key` header; the order view carries `paymentUrl` while its link lives; the return page reads the provider at most every 10 s. The fiche's save refuses a slot a plugin holds for another stay. Per-person stock counts its people; the bath card of the home appears with its first free hour; service hours are unconstrained only while nothing at all is stored; the planning card lists staff-prepared lines only; « À préparer » ages out past stays; the guest cookie is `Secure` only with `HTTPS_ENABLED`; the studio re-reads its admin (`requireAuth.revalidate`). Online payment 1.7.1: the bank reconciliation expects a paid guest-order link.
- **Google Vacation Rentals catches up when it comes back.** Switched on again or reinstalled, the plugin re-sends every price and availability to Google at once instead of waiting for the nightly full re-send (spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md` rule 17).
- **Support access (H2 review):** a support session can no longer reach a forbidden route by changing the letter case or adding a trailing slash, nor decide or revoke its own access by any path; it writes no account and no credential (users, SMTP group, plugin secrets, Google and Qonto connections, Neat), reads no clear-text secret (gate connector) and cannot uninstall or turn off a plugin; its reads are logged once a minute and the customer reads the log in plain French (« Réservation 12 modifiée », « Refusé »); the support account is told by a flag, cannot be edited and is never restored by `reset-admin.js`; a managed instance accepts support only for its own slug, taken from the licence when `GUESTFLOW_SLUG` is not set (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rules 15, 18-22).
- **Forgotten password:** the answer leaves before the address is looked up, a caller over the IP limit no longer uses up an address's quota, and the counters stay bounded in memory.
- **Second step:** « Utiliser un code de secours » switches the code field to the letter keyboard; the backup codes dialog closes on « Terminé » only; the red support bar keeps clear of the phone's home indicator.
- Suivi financier: a month window naming no month (« 2026-13 », « 2027-00 ») is refused with « Ce mois n'appartient pas à l'exercice choisi. » instead of an internal error, on an exercise that does not close in December (specs/stay-profile.md rule 25).
- **A forged mail can no longer freeze the server.** The mailbox reader skips any part over 256 KB and turns HTML into text in linear time (the image pattern was quadratic: 160 KB took 4 s), the Gîtes de France supplements are read chunk by chunk instead of with a backtracking pattern, and the Airbnb parser caps line and message lengths. An unauthenticated mail that does not parse is now stored as `ignored` (out of every list) instead of being re-read and re-parsed at every pass for 30 days.
- **Mail intakes** read an HTML-only mail the way a browser shows it (whitespace collapsed, block tags start lines), log the mails whose text part is over 256 KB, and read a refused mail again after a plugin update. The client's `isValidEmail` now refuses lists and angle addresses like the server's.
- **iCal import hardened** (specs/ical-import-hardening.md, review 2026-10-18):
  - every nameless iCal stay gets a placeholder client of its own (« Ical Booking », « Ical Airbnb ») instead of one shared « Closed Not available » / « Reserved Ical » per feed;
  - « Synchroniser » on a stopped calendar (`isActive = 0`) is refused — it used to bring back the stays deleted after retiring Lodgify — and the platforms tab shows « Arrêté »;
  - a stay overlapping another reservation is flagged and notified on every feed, not only Booking's, including when a feed moves a stay onto taken nights;
  - feeds are fetched with a 20-second timeout and all at once: one silent platform no longer holds the sync for five minutes;
  - a truncated feed is refused instead of raising false cancellation alerts; two stays sharing one UID both exist; a UTC time is dated in Europe/Paris;
  - Gîtes de France « BOOKED » stays get a placeholder client each too (the shared « Booked Ical » is split at boot);
  - a UID that starts repeating keeps its existing stay on its own dates instead of moving it onto the other event's;
  - a stay moved off the taken nights loses its conflict badge;
  - the « Ical <platform> » placeholder left behind by a Booking or Gîtes de France intake is deleted at the next sync;
  - the sync line reads « Synchronisé à 02:05 · 1 nouvelle » with the full detail in its tooltip, and the stray « 0 » after the chips is gone.
- **Booking intake**: the iCal sources counted as Booking are derived from the calendars (Booking's feed, the Lodgify relay only while active); the setting is gone. **Gîtes de France intake**: a fiche whose rent is still to choose appears in the dashboard alert. Export card and reservation-number hints reworded.
- **iCal import** rejects impossible dates (`20270231`, `20271399`) and absurd ranges: an event starting more than 730 days ahead, ending more than 1 095 days ahead or lasting more than 366 nights is dropped, so `DTEND:99991231` can no longer create a placeholder blocking every later night.
- iCal: a sync pass already running no longer imports into a calendar stopped, deleted or re-pointed since the pass began; the pass skips it and writes no status.
- Finance and VAT regime: a date of the right shape that names no day (month 13, day 32…) in a custom finance window or a VAT regime change date is refused with its usual message instead of failing with a server error.
- **No more reload loop on a missing page file** (spec `inte-performance.md`, 2026-10-28). A page file answered by the SPA fallback reloaded the app in a loop because a successful prefetch of another page cleared the guard; the guard is now per route and cleared only when that route renders. Also: price calculations and previews no longer make the side menu re-read the properties, a read started after a write settles never joins one started before it committed, the client search of an existing stay loads its list when the client lookup fails, « Réessayer » in the email editor keeps what was typed, and the release archive no longer carries the Vite manifest.
- **Linen shortage alert**: a bath-mat shortage read « bathMat » instead of « Tapis de bain ».
- **Mail intakes (Airbnb, Gîtes de France)** no longer trust a forged sender: only mails whose header From address equals the configured sender are read, and a mail without `dkim=pass` for the sender's domain in the `Authentication-Results` written by the mailbox's own server (Gmail by default, otherwise the « Serveur de réception » setting; an RFC 8601 tokenizer reads it) is stored as a proposal flagged « Expéditeur non vérifié », listed on the dashboard, never auto-applied. An authenticated Gîtes de France mail never replaces the e-mail or phone already on the fiche. The mailbox password is only sent over TLS (STARTTLS required off port 993) and only to a host of the SMTP server's domain — pinned inside the SDK, so it holds for any plugin version; the IMAP host/port, sender and signature settings cannot be changed by a support session, which can no longer roll a plugin back or update it to a chosen version. SDK 2.12.0 (`mailbox.resolveImapHost`, `readMailsFrom` returns `authenticated`).
- **Split payments, round-34 fixes** (spec `mixed-method-payments.md` rules 42-49, 2026-11-09). A price cut after a split payment that left a reste or a geste no longer raises a false trop-perçu: the paid bucket keeps what it received, and the cut lowers the reste, then the geste. Lowering a split-paid complement on the fiche no longer asks to delete a refund that does not exist. A split payment matched at the bank can no longer be taken back, and a payment whose plain receipt is already matched can no longer be split, so no bank line is left orphaned. On a cancelled stay, the caisse-interne share of a split solde is refundable in the caisse interne. The « Résumé tarifaire » reads « Perçu en partie » while a reste is due; an empty last line at 0 no longer blocks « Encaisser »; a correction left open on an undone payment no longer hides the new one's buttons. Every boot re-checks the split payments (after a rollback then a roll-forward); the VAT regime switch lists the months of a split payment.
- plugin:sas Each opening of the check-in/check-out dialog starts with one method, so a check-out with nothing to collect after a split one completes; « Plusieurs moyens » is no longer offered to reception, and a crafted split is refused in French.
- **Split payments over several buckets, round-34 verification** (spec `mixed-method-payments.md` rules 50-53, 2026-11-09). The money received by one payment covering several buckets (the single arrival payment, the check-in and check-out SAS, the complement carrying the end of stay) is read as one sum: a later price cut lowers the reste or the geste first and no longer raises a false trop-perçu. Un-ticking only one bucket of such a payment, or switching one of its buckets to or from the caisse interne, is refused (« Paiement en plusieurs moyens : annulez le paiement unique en entier. » / « … passer par « Modifier ». ») instead of turning caisse-interne money into revenue or forgetting the reste. A geste on a split payment now comes off the accommodation in the refund register, as in the books, so a cancelled stay refunded in full no longer leaves tourist tax owed. A split end of stay no longer counts its departure lines (cleaning) as part of the price in the trop-perçu.
- Console « Modules »: the whole height of a module switch is clickable (a click on its top or bottom edge did nothing), and removing a module that holds no data reads « Désinstallation » in its line and in the journal instead of « Effacement des données ».
- **Activation hooks (SDK 2.11.0) fire when the licence turns a plugin live, and only after an install committed.** A plan upgrade that starts allowing an already-enabled plugin now runs its `ctx.onActivate` hook once; a requirement rolled back with its dependant no longer announces itself. An install that needs a restart still fires nothing at the next boot (known limit, spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md` rule 17).
- **Option and resource writes are validated on the server** (spec `options-resources-ux.md` rules 15, 16, 21). An option with an empty name, a negative or non-numeric per-property price (formerly skipped silently) or a percentage above 100 is refused with `422 VALIDATION_FAILED` and per-field details, as is a resource with an empty name, a fractional quantity, a negative price or a closing time before its opening time (formerly `400`). The overrides, defaults and bath-mat quantities of properties outside an option's scope are dropped on save by the server instead of the browser.
- Options & ressources: switching to the « Facturables au SAS » tab no longer makes the page title and the tabs disappear for a moment while the tab loads.
- **A payment on a link whose stay was deleted now alerts the admin** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 3-4, 2026-10-30). The poll, the webhook and the owed-deactivation retry used to keep such a payment silent (`no-reservation`, or the notifier skipping `not_found`). The money stays on the link as a record, nothing is booked and no confirmation is sent; the admin gets a push and an e-mail « Paiement reçu sur un lien sans séjour — {montant} » naming the link, its type, amount, creation and payment days, the provider's link and payment ids and the former stay, with no URL. +17 server tests.
- **A stay paid through an installment plan now reaches « Suivi taxe de séjour »** (specs/tourist-tax-declaration-month-stay-end.md rules 7-8, D14): the page waited for `balancePaid`, which paying an installment never sets, so such a stay was on no month and its tax was never offered for declaration. A stay with a plan is now listed at the month of its last night, for its whole tax, whatever is paid, so a tax not yet collected can be declared. Other stays keep their payment gate.
- Planning: a fast swipe or a smooth scroll upwards that crosses the top of the loaded days now loads the earlier days during the gesture, instead of stopping at the top. While earlier days load, a thin progress bar under the page bar replaces the skeleton, so nothing moves or is covered.
- **Planning scroll never stalls on an empty fortnight** (spec `dashboard-planning-scroll.md` rule 14). A window with no reservation used to stop the infinite scroll for good, so the stays after it (Christmas, the next season) could never be reached by scrolling.
- Operator-managed plugins: the module switch in the console no longer covers « Plus d’actions » (a tap switched the module on); a hidden lifecycle route is logged like an unknown one in the support log; a licence dated in the future can no longer shut out later ones (the console stamps them in order, the space ignores a stamp far ahead of its clock); a module an installed plugin needs says so on its line, and a module without data can be uninstalled from the console.
- **Operator-provisioned plugins: review fixes** (spec `plugin-operator-provisioning.md`, rules 29–36). A space on a release before the reconciler is no longer seeded (it would have lost its plugins at the upgrade); the hidden plugin lifecycle answers a support session with the same 404 as everyone; a refused erase order is retried hourly and shown on the console line instead of every minute and silently; plugins outside the plan no longer block a deactivation; an older licence never replaces a newer one; the Paramètres summary has no plugins line on a managed space; the menu follows the operator's choices without a reload. Console: two quick unticks in « Nouveau client » both hold, errors read red, the switch keeps its focus and a 44 px target, state lines follow the mockup.
- **A plugin that is gone no longer takes the server down** (spec `plugin-removal-resilience.md` rules 1, 2, 6). The plugin catalogue is built from the manifests that exist: a plugin whose folder is missing is skipped with one log line instead of crashing the boot, and an installed plugin whose code is gone is listed « Paquet introuvable : à réinstaller. » on the Plugins page, where it can be uninstalled. A plugin with no loaded module is never live, in development as in production (`/api/auth/me` no longer lists it). A stored open payment link is re-offered in the deposit reminder and in an installment request only while a payment provider is live.
- **Spec-rule gate counted every numbered line of a spec diff as a rule** (spec `spec-rule-coverage.md` rule 2). A study or a test plan with a « 1. » list blocked its PR with rules that do not exist. The gate now only counts entries of the « Functional rules » section. +2 server tests.
- **Gate access no longer runs while its plugin is off** (spec `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md` §1): the dashboard gate-keys endpoint, the gate fields of the SAS payload, the gate variables of the guest emails and the connector keys created at every boot all stopped depending on the client hiding them.
- **Google Agenda: the message after the Google consent screen shows again** — the callback lands on Intégrations instead of `/settings`, whose redirect dropped it (rule 19).
- **School holidays: the sync imports every period, on the right days** (rule 27). It kept the summer only (education.gouv marks the other periods `population = "-"`), started each period on the Friday (a UTC date instead of the Paris day), and duplicated hand-typed periods named differently. On Solio, the next sync moves the synced dots one day later and brings 2027-2028; a hand-typed « Été 2027 » may remain beside the imported summer, to delete from the Vacances tab.
- **The dashboard « Envoyer la demande / Relancer » no longer creates a Qonto link while online payment is off.**
- **A payment received online is now recorded exactly like the same payment ticked by hand**: the acompte or solde is split onto the stay's lines, as the accounting expects (it was only ticked).
- **A payment link GuestFlow abandons is now deactivated at Qonto** — when a stay is cancelled, or a link is replaced because the amount changed. If Qonto cannot be reached, GuestFlow retries at each check and says so; a payment that still lands on such a link is never booked on the stay, and the admin is told to refund it.
- A deactivated but configured Neat no longer prices the insurance nor calls Neat on every fiche preview and public quote.
- The website no longer announces « Tarif calculé pour vos dates » while Neat is off.
- The push preference « Souscriptions Neat » shows only while the Neat plugin is active.
- **Plugins, review fixes:** a plugin that failed to start is off everywhere — its menus, pages and calls are gone and its URLs answer `404 PLUGIN_INACTIVE`; the Plugins page says a refusal before the click and offers only « Désinstaller » on a failed plugin; a failed erasure leaves the plugin inactive; erasing the tariff recipes no longer changes the « à partir de » price; a setting a rollback to v3.5 rewrote is carried forward.
- **Licence:** an unreadable expiry date, a licence issued for another instance (`GUESTFLOW_SLUG`) and an unreadable licence file are refused; an expired licence still decides the plugins; resetting the password of a disabled account counts against the quota; opening a reservation in read-only no longer ends in the refused-write toast; the banner is worded by the server.
- **Online payment:** a stale link the guest already paid is never retired; a link already dead at Qonto is no longer retried forever; the plugin cannot be turned off while a cancelled link is still payable; a payment on a cancelled stay records nothing; online payments are dated by Qonto's payment time in Paris; a failed accounting split is written in the stay's history; the link list answers without a provider; the provider form no longer starts from domainesolio.com.
- **Neat:** a property with the insurance « par défaut » can create bookings again while Neat is off; « offert » and the price lock of a carried line are frozen; the partner is only asked for properties that sell the insurance; the property page no longer lists the insurance as « comprise » while Neat is off.
- **No trace of an inactive plugin:** the email editor's plugin variables come from the server and the templates only a plugin could send are hidden; laundry fields, site, Qonto and accounting wording leave the core screens when their plugin is off; « Vacances & fermetures » reads « Fermetures » without the school holidays; a reception account without the SAS sees why; SAS hints point to the real settings place; the « Réservation en ligne » card shows an error with « Réessayer » instead of vanishing.
- Every message touched is impersonal and short.
- specs/public-api.md rule 9 no longer claims a per-API-key limit on booking requests: the code never had one, and a limit shared by every visitor is refused.
- **Public booking form**: the phone is validated at the boundary (`phoneInvalid`, 8–15 digits, `+` and separators only) and the e-mail must be a single address, so a line break in a field can no longer forge a line of the owner's notification e-mail. Every value interpolated in the plain-text operator e-mails (site devis, digest, iCal reservation, conflict) has its control characters and line separators replaced by a space (website-booking 1.8.4).
- **Online payment — a hung Qonto request no longer blocks the poll for minutes** (`payment-polling-fair-use.md` rule 12): each request is aborted after 20 s (`QONTO_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`) and fails as a network failure.
- Email checks are linear and length-bounded everywhere (public booking request, clients, users, settings, contact paste), closing a request that could freeze the server for 35 s.
- The first-administrator script refuses a value that looks like a flag instead of reading it as the flag.
- Mail From display names are encoded by the mail library, never quoted by hand: a crafted name can no longer rewrite the sender or envelope; names are limited to 120 characters.
- Mail intakes: an Authentication-Results header with an unquoted echo is refused, the Gîtes de France opening sentence is anchored before the dossier number, iCal placeholder clients drop invisible Unicode characters, and the console passes dash-leading names to the first-admin script.
- Mail intakes: Airbnb subject and heading are read on bounded lines, DKIM counts only verdicts before the echoed envelope, the Gîtes de France mail must open with the centrale's sentence, and invisible Unicode format characters are stripped from guest names.
- Console login page: a malformed remembered-space cookie no longer causes a server error.
- Email templates: rendering is no longer quadratic on a long run of spaces, and a subject (300 characters) and a body (20 000) are capped in the editor, the texts screen and the CSV import; the 20 000 cap also applies to the stay texts, the mentions and the resource sentence.
- Self-update: the lockfile check now also requires a valid semantic version and a well-formed package name before building the expected tarball path.
- Start assistant: adopting the company email as login now reports the change even when ending the other sessions fails, and logs the failure.
- Plugin registry: two simultaneous uploads of one version can no longer leave a package file that does not match its signed hash, and an index date no longer stays frozen after a clock jump of more than a day (the instances' own tolerance); an unreadable file name on upload answers 400 instead of 500.
- Support access journal: a request path such as `/api/constructor` no longer prints a function's source in the customer's log.
