- **Bank reconciliation — review fixes (`specs/reconciliation-assisted-matching.md` §3.5).** A credit settled more than 3 days before the expected payout is no longer proposed (it let another stay's payout become a « sûr » match and be auto-confirmed); a grouped payout never beats an exact single stay and always waits for the operator; « Défaire » now takes an auto-confirmed installment back; a penalty is offered on a partial cover and completes it; the fiche's « Vous percevez » is net of penalties; a payout the fiche does not carry yet takes one credit at most; a platform receipt cannot be over-allocated through « Rattacher ». UI: the credit dialog no longer crashes on close, « Actualiser » keeps the filter, the desktop table shows auto badges, splits and groups, the home card only offers one click on « sûr » proposals, the penalty is declarable from the payout row of a locked fiche, and the statement editor no longer writes a stale commission back after a penalty.
- **Bank reconciliation — verification round.** The early side of a payout window is the stay (no credit before check-in; Lodgify and Stripe like a platform paying at booking time), « sûr » and automatic confirmation need a credit from checkout on and within 14 days after the due date, a group that explains the credit as well makes a single stay « à vérifier », card operations are never platform payouts, a confirmation never rewrites a recorded payout or its commission, a declaration completes a fiche only on an exact cover (and every read sweeps the ones the event missed), « Défaire » on an installment warns that its payment link stays closed, and the automatic-confirmation banner counts what « Voir » lists.
- Bank reconciliation follows the payments ledger again: every platform other than « direct » is settled by a platform payout (reverts the previous change).
- Installing a release (self-update and fleet console) now refuses a lockfile that resolves anything outside the npm registry or without an integrity hash, then runs `npm ci --ignore-scripts` with a minimal environment (proxy settings kept, persistent npm cache), so no install-time code of a release or dependency runs. The install still runs as the installer's own unix user (see self-update rule 40). Releases can additionally be required to carry an Ed25519 `SHA256SUMS.sig` once `GUESTFLOW_RELEASE_PUBLIC_KEY` is set (`scripts/sign-release.mjs`, step 8b of the release skill).
- Plugin removal matrix: every cell is green again. Core tests that need a shipped plugin now skip without it, the guard that catches them also sees fake packages carrying a shipped id, loops over ids and plugin paths built from a variable, and `plugin-removal.mjs cell` takes `--snapshot-port`. Test and CI tooling only; the production-build E2E job now uploads its server log.
- **Held dates: the first boot after the install, failures while withdrawing, and a mint racing a booking** (spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rules 11, 12, 32, 48, 61, review of #907). The boot steps that withdraw payment links (links already on taken dates, a duplicate set aside) no longer load any model while the database module is being built, which left `/api/reservations` answering 500 on the first boot after the install until a restart. A failure while withdrawing a link never loses an iCal booking nor fails a save that succeeded: it is logged and the 15-minute pass withdraws the link (a disk-full or I/O error still fails the import as a whole, never half of it). A link whose nights were taken or closed while the payment provider was creating it is withdrawn and deactivated at once, and the request refused. The « Dates prises » badge no longer shows on a devis that is no longer open or on a stay already over, and gets a 44 px touch target on phones; the refusal dialog of a devis is titled « Devis non enregistré ». +10 server tests, +1 client test.
- **Textes des emails**: the preview always shows the latest draft — a slower answer (or refusal) for an older draft no longer overwrites it.
- **Paramètres › Intégrations › Site**: a connection that cannot be read shows an error with « Réessayer » instead of an empty tab.
- **Paramètres › Intégrations**: a card of a plugin disabled during the session no longer keeps the page « modifié ».
- **Plugins**: « Tout mettre à jour » reloads the page or the list as the server asks; a restart whose former start time could not be read no longer reloads on the old process's answer; the « À configurer » chip is a 44 px touch target.
- **Aperçu d'email**: the template's own words are reachable from the keyboard like the other sentences.
- **Calendriers**: two plugins contributing day markers with the same key both show their dots.
- **Support access:** a support session can no longer download the Booking userscript (it carries the secret in clear), create or regenerate that secret, read the gate connector's keys, or read or rotate a property's iCal export token (the property's iCal card then shows the refusal); plugins declare such routes with `ctx.secretRoutes` (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rules 23–24).
- **Sign-in:** revoking a user's sessions (deactivation, password reset, role change) also ends a session waiting for that user's second step, and the second step refuses a user deactivated after the password (specs/security-auth-encryption.md rule 3).
- **Today:** the Dashboard and the Planning open on the server's date (Europe/Paris) instead of the browser's UTC date, which was the previous day between midnight and 2 a.m. and move to the new day when the page comes back to the foreground after midnight (specs/ds-sweep-planning.md rules 28–29).
- **Dashboard:** an accountant's dashboard no longer calls the admin-only email readiness and gets no 403.
- plugin:booking-intake The secret is created by « Créer le secret »; serving the script never creates one (specs/booking-extranet-intake.md rules 25–26).
- **Console — a plugin push never downgrades** (spec `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rules 30–31, second review of PR #701). « Versions › Plugins » offers only versions newer than the oldest one running, the push skips instances already at or past the version and refuses an older one; the instance's switch script refuses a downgrade too, unless the installed package cannot run on its release. A version that rolled back reads « — en échec » and is no longer preselected, and a plugin that failed to load is reported at once (« plugin linen 1.0.3 : chargement en échec »).
- **Hosted instances — « Réinitialiser l'accès » ends the account's live sessions** (rule 32), like an admin reset from the Users page.
- **Hosted instances — `/api/version` no longer tells a visitor which plugins are installed** (rule 33): `?plugin=<id>` is answered only to the console, which sends the instance's proxy secret.
- **Console — a lost invitation is no longer a dead end** (spec `hosting-h1-fleet-runtime.md` rule 24, review 2026-10-18). « Reprendre » on a failed invitation now issues a new provisional password and sends it again; the customer's page offers « Renvoyer l'invitation » and « Réinitialiser l'accès » (second step turned off), each journaled.
- **Console — new instances start with what the console knows** (rule 25). Their environment carries `GUESTFLOW_PUBLIC_URL`, `GUESTFLOW_REGISTRY_URL` and the registry's key, rewritten at a rename; the establishment (name, contact email, address, VAT number) is seeded at creation, so onboarding is pre-filled, « mot de passe oublié » works and the platform mails get their Reply-To. Réglages › Système shows the hosting's address as the public URL's default.
- **Hosting bootstrap** (rules 26–27): `bootstrap-host.sh` generates the licence key pair and takes the object storage keys once (`S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID` / `S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`), rewriting `/etc/default/litestream` from the console's env; rclone's remote is derived from `CP_BACKUP_URL` (no `rclone config`). The runbook says the repository is public and lists the licence key among what is kept offline.
- **Console — Versions** (rule 28): a release an update rolled back reads « En échec » and is never the default target; no scope is preselected.
- **Console — crashed instance** (rule 29): « Redémarrer » on the customer's page (systemd's failure reset first); host alerts link to the customer's page, in the console and in the email.
- **Console — smaller fixes**: a payment during the trial ends the trial (`control-plane-plans-and-access.md` rule 35); « Anciennes adresses » shows the redirect's end date and the reservation's; the support section reads « En attente » right after a request (`hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 26); the support journal folds one minute of reads into « Consultation : <pages> » (rule 25).
- **Console security hardening** (security review of `inte/plugins`, `specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md`
  rules 27, 31, 39; `specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 36):
  - a stolen operator cookie can no longer guess the password through « changer de méthode »: a wrong
    password there counts toward the five-failure lock, the lock ends the session, and `/mfa/start` /
    `/mfa/confirm` share the login's per-IP limit;
  - the TOTP code that confirmed the authenticator app is spent and cannot open a session; a typed
    password opens the second step for 10 minutes only; the login page's `/go` is rate-limited;
  - the console refuses to start outside `NODE_ENV=development`/`test` without `CP_SESSION_SECRET`
    and `CP_PROXY_SECRET` (it used to fall back to a public session secret and non-Secure cookies);
  - a managed instance refuses to start with the E2E suite's test-only public key;
  - `ip-address` 10.7.4 (GHSA-rxp7-6hxx-x8q5) in the console server, `vite` 8.3.5 in the console client.
- A deactivated or deleted account, or one still on its default password, can no longer read or change its second step, nor regenerate its backup codes, from a session left open.
- A support session can no longer change the sender name from the start assistant, nor a property's access code or Wi-Fi password (start assistant or property page).
- An instance upgraded from the release where an empty pool season meant 15 June → 31 August keeps that season on its properties, so the J-7 email still mentions the pool.
- **« Réservations à venir » in one call** (spec `upcoming-reservations-cards.md` rule 6, 2026-10-18). `GET /api/reservations/upcoming` returns the arrivals from today on, grouped by day, labelled and counted, each detailed: the page used to send one request per stay and could exhaust the API rate limit, locking every page behind 429s. +5 server tests, +3 client tests.
- **Pages open on the server's today** (spec `upcoming-reservations-cards.md` rule 7). `GET /api/today`; the dashboard and the planning no longer show yesterday between midnight and 2 a.m. in Paris, and the reservation list's payment status uses the server day. +3 server tests, +3 client tests.
- **Narrow action bars** (spec `ds-components.md` rules 19-20). `PageActionBar` keeps a readable title, lets its subtitle wrap, keeps its actions in a row and moves its centre node to a second row under 720 px; every bar button is 44 × 44.
- **Touch targets** (spec `ds-components.md` rule 21). On a touch screen every icon button, checkbox and radio is 44 × 44, every button and toggle button 44 px high; the « Voir les N options » toggle and the platform colour swatch too.
- **Mobile dialogs and texts** (spec `ds-components.md` rules 22, 25). The fiche's email preview opens full screen on a phone; « Nettoyer les clients », « Accueil » / « Départ », « Solde dû », « Lit bébé ×1 », « Indemnités », and the translations and self-update texts lose their « tu », « nous » and jargon.
- plugin:hourly-resources The booking dialog opens full screen on a phone; below 1200 px « Nouvelle réservation » is a « + » icon (spec `ds-components.md` rules 22, 24).
- plugin:accounting-export On a phone each journal line is a stacked row with its full amount, and the month totals wrap (spec `ds-components.md` rule 23).
- **« Prêt » ticks at once on « Réservations à venir »** (spec `upcoming-reservations-cards.md` rule 8). The box flips immediately and only its day is recounted (`GET /api/reservations/upcoming?day=`) instead of re-reading every stay of the season. +4 server tests, +3 client tests.
- **The dashboard shows the latest day it loaded** (spec `upcoming-reservations-cards.md` rule 9): a slower load for the browser's day no longer paints over the server's day. The refund date and the clients directory take the calendar day, not the UTC one, between midnight and 2 a.m.
- **Touch targets kept on « Suivi financier »** (spec `ds-components.md` rule 21, amended). The period and measure toggles, the CGV « Détails », the support bar « Quitter » and two other dense buttons are 44 px high on a touch screen; a bar whose centre is hidden or on its own row leaves its title the room (rule 19, amended).
- plugin:linen The extra laundry trip proposes today's date between midnight and 2 a.m. too, not yesterday's.
- **Guest emails — a broken variable or condition is refused** (spec `guest-email-texts-made-simple.md` rule 27). A `{{` without its `}}`, a stray `⟦`, a `{{#if}}` without « fin si » or a condition inside a condition is refused on « Textes des emails », in the template editor and in the imported file, instead of reaching the guest as raw text; one already stored is listed in « À compléter ». On the page a variable is one unit: Backspace or Delete removes it whole, a click selects it whole, and a refusal reads « ⟦…⟧ ».
- **Start assistant — nothing asserted that nobody said** (rules 28–32). A property created by the assistant has no deposit and leaves the deposit, the cleaning and the bath towels unsaid until answered (« Vos emails » or the property page), so the first emails no longer announce a 500 € deposit, « ménage non compris » or towels to bring. « Précédent », a reload or the assistant reopened edits its property instead of creating a second one; an instance that already has properties gets none from it. The article comes from the name (« au Fournil », « à la Bergerie », no « à l'Gite ») and is chosen by the owner otherwise. The preview's sample stay keeps the property's check-in time. The key-box code has its own field and « Accès » no longer leaves in the CSV.
- **First login** (spec `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rules 27–29): the bootstrap `admin@guestflow.local` becomes the company email typed at step 1, so « Mot de passe oublié » reaches a mailbox; the code that confirmed an authenticator app can no longer open a session in the same 30 s; the method picker stacks on a phone.
- **Dashboard — « Envoyer la demande » is back without online payment** (spec `plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 25). A row « à demander » opens the request email in the send dialog, to read before it leaves, instead of offering only « Reporter » (regression from v3.10.0). A reminder sent by hand counts as asked.
- **Emails** (spec `email-automation.md` rules 15–18): a failed send is told in French next to the buttons (no `EMAIL_SEND_FAILED`, no « Missing credentials for "PLAIN" »); « Quand » reads « À la réservation », « 15 nov. », « À la demande »… instead of « Jour J »; the full template editor shows variables by their French label like « Textes des emails »; a pending email has its own « Envoyer » button. The history shows Paris time (spec `email-history-rolling-window.md` rule 8).
- **Unsaved text is never lost** (rule 35): « Textes des emails » and the property page ask before leaving; the property's « Arrivée » block is saved by the page's Enregistrer; an empty property text reads « Texte commun utilisé » instead of « Vide »; « Options citées » reads « ⟦Prix⟧ ». Mobile: « Textes » and « Indemnités » titles, buttons on one row, send dialog full screen.
- **A fresh install lists no « GitesDeFrance » platform** (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rule 32). The CGV sentence is left out instead of ending on « ici : » while there is no link (rule 35). The neutral J-7 subject names the stay with its article (rule 32 of the texts spec).
- plugin:gate-access Intégrations labels in French, no server path shown.
- plugin:google-calendar One short line when the Google connection is not available on the installation, no variable name or server path.
- plugin:weather-alerts The address it uses is named after Paramètres › Établissement.
- **English emails write amounts the English way** (spec `email-language-fr-en.md` rule 8): « Stay amount: €963.39 », « deposit cheque of €400.00 », like the « €8 per person » of the stay texts, instead of « 963,39 € » beside them. French emails are unchanged.
- **First login and setup pages** (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rules 33–36): after the forced first password change, the login says « Mot de passe modifié » with the email filled in; « Fermetures » keeps a title and a whole « Aucune fermeture configurée. » on a phone; Plateformes and Utilisateurs switch to cards below 1200 px so no column hides in an inner scroll; the assistant's « Plugins » step folds the extra plugins under « Pour aller plus loin ».
- « Mon compte » no longer shows two « Mot de passe actuel » fields: the « Second code » card asks for « Mot de passe (confirmation) » (spec `hosting-h2-account-security.md` rule 30). Système › Traductions explains the file impersonally.
- **Property page — saving no longer resets the deposit or confirms the article** (spec `guest-email-texts-made-simple.md` rules 28, 30). An empty « Caution par défaut » keeps the stored amount instead of putting 500 € back (an emptied 400 € stayed 400 €); the reservation page no longer falls back to 500 €. An article nobody chose shows empty and stays listed in « À compléter » after a save of another field; the menu and its preview read « au Fournil » as the emails do.
- **Start assistant reopened — the seasons survive** (rule 29). « Suivant » at step 2 writes only the price of the rule the assistant created, and only when it changed; a « Haute saison » added since keeps its price.
- **Bed linen is not asserted on a fresh install** (rule 28): « Le linge de lit n'étant pas compris… » is left out until « Linge de lit » is answered on the Séjour tab or in « Vos emails », and « À compléter » lists it.
- **Names in « Y » or « H »** (rule 30): « Yourte », « Hutte », « Hameau » are no longer guessed « à l' » and are offered the five articles, « à la Yourte » included. A variable in single braces, « {clientFirstName} », is refused like a broken one (rule 27).
- plugin:accounting-export the CSV transliterates what Latin-1 cannot hold (« ’ », « Œ ») instead of corrupting it; a prorated bucket splits its TTC at its rate (no more one-cent gap); « Direct » in the Plan comptable (`accountant-accounting-export.md` rules 31-32, `accounting-platform-commission-and-no-deposit.md` rule 23, mega review #5 M3, m4, N5).
- plugin:linen a manual count typed with its sign keeps it, and bath mats get their own stepper (`laundry-manual-removals.md` rule 9, `laundry-bath-mat.md` rule 13, M4).
- plugin:neat `NEAT_API_BASE` points the client at a local mock (`plugins-phase-3b-neat.md` rule 24, N9).
- plugin:online-payment the Qonto connection copy is impersonal (N1).
- plugin:tariff-recipes the recipe browser's intro no longer explains server internals (N4).
- plugin:website-booking « today » of the public catalogue is the local day (rule 37).
- **Finance — « Marquer payé » on a late row ticks only what its chips list** (spec `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 38): a late platform payout marks the solde received and no longer marks the guest's on-site complement as paid.
- **Finance — « Sans montant » reads as such** (rule 39): a 0 € stay shows « Montant à saisir » instead of « Réglé · Acompte non payé · Solde non payé », and the filter's total counts the same nights as its insight.
- **Payment links — no refund request for the only payment** (`plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 24): a link retired because its bucket was ticked by hand, then found paid, becomes the payment itself; only a bucket settled on an earlier day raises « Paiement à vérifier sur un lien retiré ».
- **Local day in the plugins** (rule 37 note): the arrival scheduling's « not before » bound and the school-holiday sync window use the local day, no longer the UTC one between midnight and 2 am.
- **Finance — last year is no longer « 0 € » before GuestFlow existed** (spec `finance-dashboard-redesign.md` rule 33, mega review #5 B1). While the tracking start is less than a year old, a month already over compares on last year's final figure and a month not over shows no N-1, so the best-month insight no longer reads « contre 0 € l'an dernier ».
- **Finance — « En retard » counts every late payment** (rule 34, B2): own-channel stays through `isDirectChannel()` (a platform counted as direct included) and platform payouts past departure + their payout delay; the table lists each overdue element and the insight names the payouts.
- **Finance — a night is sold once** (rule 35, M1): nights sold, occupancy and pace nights are distinct per logement, so iCal duplicates no longer inflate them; revenue per night divides by the nights of priced stays; a « N séjours sans montant » insight opens the new « Sans montant » filter of « Réservations ».
- **Finance — wording and today** (rules 36-37): « Commissions évitées par le direct » in the success tone, « Canaux » without a minus sign, « Encaissable d'ici le » with the server's default date; every server read of « today » uses the local day (`utils/localDate.js`), no longer the UTC one between midnight and 2 am.
- **Payment links — a link settled another way is retired** (`plugins-phase-3a-online-payment.md` rule 23, M2): a bucket paid by transfer, cash or platform cancels its open link (at once on a manual tick, and on every poll), the poll checks it was not paid then deactivates it, and stops polling it.
- **Payment links — reservation links carry their VAT basket and their reference** (`payment-links-vat.md` rule 8, `qonto-payment-link-reference.md` rule 10, M6).
- **Tourist tax — the declared total keeps its cents** (`ds-sweep-finance.md` rule 23, M5).
- **Devis — a re-issued devis owes its payment on its new validity date; an expired devis says « (expiré) »; the HT column reads « Total HT »** (`deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 17, `devis-pdf-and-tourist-tax-fixes.md` rules 21-22).
- **Payment links paid at an outdated amount** (spec `reservation-payment-ledger.md` rules 31 and 47, review of 2026-10-23). A full-payment link paid after the stay grew an acompte now records the acompte and the solde together, so the acompte is no longer claimed a second time (a 250 € link on a 90 € + 217,20 € stay leaves 57,20 € owed, not 307,20 €). A retired complement link paid late is recorded the way « Marquer perçu » records it and traced in the history. An open link paid after a re-price outside the fiche's save (plugins, arrival SAS) is booked at the amount the guest actually paid instead of ticking the bucket at its new amount. +7 server tests.
- Neat: a failed subscription job whose stay stops qualifying (cancelled, insurance line removed, stay started, deposit no longer paid) is dropped instead of retried into a paid policy; one subscription pass at a time, even right after a data erasure.
- Public booking API: `/quote` and `/booking-requests` refuse a stay starting in the past, longer than 365 nights or ending more than 730 days ahead — an `endDate` of 9999-12-31 used to block the server.
- Gîtes de France intake: one intake that throws no longer stops every later pass; a started stay is refused before a client record is created; the mailbox is logged out even when the INBOX lock fails.
- Booking intake: the package asks for SDK `^1.6.0`, the first one sure to carry `platformWithholdsTouristTax`.
- Hourly resources: the erasure warning announces every resource setting the purge resets, heat-up and heat-retention times included.
- Gate access: the results Sowel posts back are type-checked (https link, string code) and filed in one transaction.
- Accounting export: a code comment no longer names a guest nor a reservation; its tests use a fictitious guest.
- **Website payment retries are no longer refused** (specs/public-online-payment.md rule 7, website-booking 1.5.1): `POST /public/v1/booking-requests/:id/pay` has its own `paymentStartLimiter` (default 20 / hour / visitor, `PAYMENT_START_RATELIMIT_MAX` / `_WINDOW_MS`) instead of sharing the 5 / hour booking-request budget, so a guest who sends a request then retries the payment a few times no longer gets a 429. SDK 1.7.0 exposes the new limiter.
- **WordPress plugin 1.16.1** (specs/wordpress-plugin-self-update.md rule 6, specs/wordpress-plugin.md rule 14): the self-update only accepts a `guestflow-booking-X.Y.Z.zip` asset of a GuestFlow release on github.com (repository and path checked, not just the host); a fully trusted `X-Forwarded-For` chain relays the connecting address instead of its client-written left-most hop; the payment redirect only follows an `https:` URL. `INSTALL.md` no longer suggests `curl -k`.
- Public documentation: private infrastructure details (LAN addresses, SSH login, container name, backup paths, a listing id, a personal mailbox) and real-looking guest emails in mock-ups are replaced by placeholders.
- **Accounting — a stay paid, then reduced, no longer books a negative tourist-tax credit** (spec `accounting-books-the-money-collected.md` rule 11, 2026-10-18). The contribs captured when the solde was paid (by hand or online) described the bigger stay; once the stay was saved with fewer guests or less breakfast they overflowed the entry and the export put the gap on the 46710000 line (`C-88,80`). Such an entry is now booked pro-rata of the current lines, as master did for online payments. Accounting-export plugin 1.0.1.
- **Reservations — a trop-perçu is reported and offered as a refund** (spec `reservation-refunds.md` rule 34). A save that brings a paid stay below what was collected is refused with 409 `OVERPAID` until confirmed; the fiche then opens the refund dialog on the amount, and the Remboursements block shows « Trop-perçu : X € » with « Rembourser », live while the fiche is edited — including the excess a paid acompte and solde keep once the stay is reduced. The trop-perçu is split by poste (rule 35): the refund dialog opens one « Trop-perçu » line per poste in excess, so a tourist tax paid too much is refunded on the tax instead of on Hébergement, and the alert says « Taxe de séjour : 21,60 € encaissés, 7,20 € dus ».
- **Hourly resources — no more double-booked sessions on the fiche** (spec `plugins-phase-3c-hourly-resources.md` rules 30-31). A save whose sessions use a resource more times at once than it exists is refused with 409 `SESSIONS_OVERLAP`; « Ajouter une séance » proposes the first free slot of the stay, from the check-in on arrival day, and greys out the slots outside the stay. Hourly-resources plugin 1.0.2.
- **Clients — no more duplicates, no client left by an abandoned fiche** (spec `clients.md` rules 12-13). A client whose email or phone is already known is refused with 409 `CLIENT_EXISTS` (the Clients page offers « Créer quand même »; the fiche offers « Utiliser ce client » and, rule 14, « Créer quand même » for a partner sharing the family phone). On a new reservation or devis, « Créer un nouveau client » writes nothing until the stay is saved: `POST /reservations` and `POST /devis` take `newClient` and write both in one transaction. New `GET /api/clients/duplicate`.
- **Clients — the city autocomplete works in production** (spec `security-hardening.md` rule 12). The production CSP allowed `connect-src 'self'` only, which blocked the commune lookup (`https://geo.api.gouv.fr`) of the fiche and the Clients page; that exact origin is now allowed, and nothing else.
- **Reservation screens — small fixes of the review** (spec `review-2026-10-18-reservations.md`; `reservation-price-arithmetic.md` rule 10). No « (NaN nuit) » on an empty fiche; amount fields show `7,20`, not `7.2`; the devis PDF strikes a price only when it is higher; the SAS reads « Étape N » without a total that grew with each upsell; « Petit déjeuner · 3 matins » instead of « ×3 »; impersonal texts on the client form, the meal covers and the Qonto error; the fiche is one column below 1200 px with the centred client name from 1200 px, and a long property name no longer widens the fiche or the calendar. SAS plugin 1.0.1, online-payment plugin 1.0.1.
- **Website booking engine (WordPress plugin 1.15.0):** every field has a label, the − / + buttons say what they count, calendar days are read in full with their availability, and the quote, hints and errors are announced to screen readers; retrying a failed online payment pays the same request instead of creating a new devis, and online payment is only offered while a payment provider is connected; with no CGV published while they are required the form says booking is closed before anything is typed; fields stay inside their box on any theme; a website booking switched off in GuestFlow reads « Réservation temporairement indisponible. » and « Module … inactif dans GuestFlow. » instead of a generic error; the availability calendar's weekdays follow the page's language; « 1 nuit » / « 3 nuits »; the e-mail keeps a row of its own on a phone; the CGV language switch reads « FR » / « EN »; the plugin declares `Update URI` and is tested up to WordPress 7.1.
- **Guest names:** a compound name keeps its capitals (« Jean-Pierre », « Le Goff », « Martin-Dupont », « McDonald ») instead of being lower-cased after the first letter.
- **Solio site:** the booking drawer no longer wraps the engine on pages where it does not load (a « Réserver » or « Payer » page was unusable); the insurance notice link is in English on the English pages.
- **Website key rotation survives a restart:** a key regenerated from Paramètres › Intégrations is no longer replaced by the previous one at the next restart (the self-update helper no longer hands `.env.local` values to pm2, and a rotatable key read from `.env.local` wins over a stale inherited copy).
- **Website payment return (WordPress plugin 1.15.0):** coming back from the payment page on a booking page shared by all lodgings shows the payment's status again instead of the lodging cards; a page whose booking block fixes a lodging is never picked as the automatic booking page; correcting the message or phone after a failed payment retries the same devis.
- Rolling back to v3.18.0 no longer lists the installment request as a pending email for every arriving stay: the template is seeded with an anchor that release ignores, and an existing row is moved by a one-shot migration (`specs/self-update-and-releases.md` rule 43).
- plugin:sas A plugin step that throws no longer stops the arrival and departure sas (1.7.3, needs SDK 2.14).
- **Arrival SAS: the sale only writes what the offer sells** (`sas-breakfast-and-catering-upsell.md` rule 15). A crafted call could bill any catalogue option at check-in, the cancellation insurance on a started stay included; everything outside the offer is now dropped, except a line this SAS already sold.
- **Arrival SAS: « Personnes servies » shows the line's amount** on a non-card per-person option, where it read « — » (`sas` 1.7.6, which now asks for SDK 2.21).
- **Arrival SAS: a per-person sale sent as units by a tab still on the previous bundle bills what was asked**, not the whole party; units that are not a positive number sell nothing (`non-card-option-served-persons.md` rule 28).
- **Locked fiche: the people or nights typed on an offered line sold at arrival can no longer be lowered** (`non-card-option-served-persons.md` rule 27).
- **Outgoing mail greets the SMTP server with the sender's domain** (specs/admin-account-management.md rule 21): nodemailer used to announce itself as `[127.0.0.1]`, which the receiving side records in the `Received` header and spam filters read as a sender that does not know its own name. Every transport now passes the domain of its From address (`domainesolio.com`, or the platform's noreply domain).
- **Accounting: money already collected never moves.** A tourist tax that rises after the solde was paid no longer rewrites the solde entry already exported; the arrival complement that collects the difference books it on 46710000 in its own month (it appeared in no journal before, so Finance « Encaissé » and the journal disagreed). A collected installment now books the split stored when it was paid, so a later save of the fiche no longer moves an installment entry of a past month (specs/accounting-books-the-money-collected.md rules 13-14, specs/direct-installments.md rule 47).
- **Finance « En retard » lists late installments** — tile, insight and detail — like the fiche and the payment deadlines; « Marquer payé » records each through its own endpoint (specs/direct-installments.md rule 48, specs/finance-dashboard-redesign.md rule 49).
- **Installment plan after a price drop:** the fiche's « Total perçu » counts the stay (or what was already collected, when more), the same figure live and stored, instead of the plan left above the stay (specs/reservation-payment-ledger.md rule 48).
- The stored ledger's acompte row names its ceiling (`amountEdit.max`, the accommodation) like the live one (rule 49).
- Cancelling a stay with nothing paid records `unpaid_deposit`, not `unpaid_balance` (specs/payment-schedule-and-cancellation.md rule 48).
- The cancellation insurance can no longer be deleted (`409`, it was archived), and a percentage with more than 2 decimals is refused (`422`) (specs/cancellation-insurance.md rule 28).
- A `PUT` on a received cancellation compensation answers `409 COMPENSATION_LOCKED` whatever its body (specs/cancellation-compensation.md rule 29).
- A payment link is never minted for an échéance already paid: `409 ALREADY_PAID` before any provider call (specs/online-payments-qonto.md rule 21).
- plugin:accounting-export 1.0.14 — tax rise booked with the complement; installment entries frozen at payment.
- Approving an iCal cancellation keeps the deleted stay's history, the « Suppression iCal approuvée » line included: `reservation_history` no longer cascades with the reservation.
- Reminding a platform reservation is refused as a platform reservation (400 `PLATFORM_RESERVATION`) even when no payment provider is connected, instead of 409 `NO_PAYMENT_PROVIDER`.
- The logement's « Plateformes » tab shows display names (« Gîtes de France », « Direct ») instead of the stored keys.
- Paramètres › Plateformes shows every column from 1280 px (compact table, the colour beside the name, one-line headers) and its cards below; « Sans corvées » is no longer hidden behind an inner scroll.
- Saving Paramètres › Plateformes judges « Compté comme vente directe » on the saved state: ticking it together with « Sans corvées » no longer stores a hidden chore-free flag.
- A platform first used by a reservation, an intake or an import appears on Paramètres › Plateformes at once, without a restart.
- `BookingCom`, « Booking.com » or `gites-de-france` sent for an iCal source (or typed in « Ajouter une plateforme ») land on the existing Booking / Gîtes de France platform and source instead of creating duplicates.
- The iCal export is RFC 5545 conformant: all-day `DTSTART;VALUE=DATE` / `DTEND;VALUE=DATE` and a UTC date-time `DTSTAMP`; the exported dates are unchanged.
- Plugin SDK 2.7.0: `ctx.core.reservations.update(id, patch, { client })` creates the guest's client in the same transaction as the update.
- plugin:airbnb-intake A fiche GuestFlow refuses no longer leaves an orphan guest client behind.
- plugin:gites-de-france-intake A fiche GuestFlow refuses no longer leaves an orphan guest client behind.
- plugin:booking-intake A fiche GuestFlow refuses no longer leaves an orphan guest client behind.
- **English e-mails list the booked options in English** (specs/email-client-language-and-fiche-polish.md rule 9): `{{reservedOptionsLabel}}` read the French title (« Planche du terroir ») for an English guest; it now takes the catalogue's English title, with the French fallback.
- **« La Bergerie » created from the property page reads « votre séjour à la Bergerie »** (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 47): only the start assistant derived the article from the name; the property page stored « au », unconfirmed, and offered « à l'La Bergerie ». A name holding its own article now sets it, confirmed, and the page offers that one only.
- **The e-mail history and the pending list use the e-mails' names** (« Merci et avis », not « Séquence — J+1 merci et avis »), and the « Modèle » filter and the send dialog list the templates in stay order (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 48).
- **The e-mail history is a list of cards on a phone or tablet** instead of a table cutting the address and hiding the status (specs/email-history-rolling-window.md rule 10).
- **A J+1 or a season e-mail stays in the history for its 3 days** (specs/email-history-rolling-window.md rule 9): with the arrival as the only reference, they were out of the window as soon as written and purged at the next run, out of reach of « Renvoyer ».
- **The operator's new-reservation e-mail and push name the platform « GreenGo », « Gîtes de France »** instead of the stored key « Greengo » / « GitesDeFrance » when the iCal source has no name (specs/site-booking-notifications.md rule 17).
- plugin:website-booking 1.8.2 — **Proof of work: a challenge fetched ahead no longer pays for later requests.** At verification a challenge must cover what its address owes now (base + 2 bits per request the same address sent in the hour, the flood surcharge with a one-step grace), else `403 CHALLENGE_INVALID` / `tooEasy`; the drawer's fresh challenge after every send and its single retry keep real guests through (specs/public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md rule 32). Opening the request store no longer creates the dropped `public_request_issued` table.
- **Plugins: a plugin activates only on top of active requirements** — `409 REQUIREMENT_INACTIVE` « Nécessite <nom> : à activer d’abord. », shown on the card (specs/plugins-phase-4-registry.md rule 37).
- **Payment-request e-mails write the requested amount like the rest of the mail**, in the guest's language (« €1,234.56 », « 1 240 € »), instead of the invoice format « 1234,56 € » (specs/email-language-fr-en.md rule 11).
- **English version missing on event paths:** a payment or installment request is refused `422` with what to do before any provider link is minted (it used to mint the link, then fail 502); a cancellation whose notice did not leave says so after the cancellation (`emailWarning`); the manual send's refusal tells the operator to write the English version or send in French (specs/email-language-fr-en.md rule 12).
- **Planning alerts:** a 1.5 h cleaning reads « 1h30 » (was « 1h5 »), and a stay without its own times takes its property's default check-in / check-out (specs/planning-arrival-alerts.md rule 11).
- **Hourly resources:** a fiche session is judged against the peak of simultaneous uses, not the count of uses touching the slot — with a resource ×2, bookings 17:00–18:30 and 19:30–21:00 no longer refuse 18:00–20:00 (specs/hourly-resource-quantity-and-sas-scheduling.md rule 35).
- **Emails › Modèles:** the installment request is named « Demande d’échéance », sits before the arrival and reads « À chaque échéance, à la demande » (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 49).
- **Planning cleaning alerts are computed by the server and land on the right card** (specs/planning-arrival-alerts.md rules 7–10): the server returns them keyed by card kind (with each day of the planning). A guest already in the house when the planning opens now raises the turnover alert on the next arrival; « Départs simultanés » is raised on departure rows from the real checkout times; the blue « Arrivée pendant nettoyage d'un autre logement » uses each logement's cleaning hours and real checkout instead of a fixed 2 h after 11:00; a stay's arrival card no longer shows its departure's alert.
- **Ticking a breakfast sold without chosen mornings works** (specs/breakfast-option-planning-card.md rule 8): the planning answered « OCCURRENCE_NOT_FOUND ». The toggles' failure toasts are French sentences, never a server code.
- **A fiche can no longer book an hourly resource over an external booking or another stay's session** (specs/hourly-resource-quantity-and-sas-scheduling.md rule 34): the save is refused 409 « Créneau déjà occupé le … : la ressource est réservée par ailleurs. ». A session already stored unchanged is not re-judged.
- `GET /api/reservations/upcoming?day=` refuses an impossible date (`2026-02-30`) with 400 « Date invalide. ».
- Calendar: on a phone the month list opens with the current month's first stay visible (it was under the sticky month label); a deep link to a logement's week view lands on the current week; the platform reads « Direct » everywhere; the month badge reads « Nov. 2026 »; a long client name no longer widens its day column.
- Planning on a phone: the house and person icons wrap with their names instead of sitting alone on a line.
- A reception or accountant user no longer triggers a refused `GET /api/settings` on every page (the favicon reads the settings for an admin only).
- **A guest added after the booking is billed the supplement** (specs/tariff-events-and-extra-guest-tiers rule 23): a stay sold for the included guests stored « no tiers », so a teen added later on the Lodge cost 0 € instead of 15 + 8 + 8 = 31 €. Reservations sold for ≤ 2 guests before this fix keep the old snapshot until it is rewritten.
- **The tourist-tax caption reads as the spec writes it** (tourist-tax-matches-the-office-calculation rule 18): « (272,55 € HT ÷ 3 nuits ÷ 2 occupants) × 5,00 % + 10,00 % dep = 2,50 €/adulte/nuit », French figures, no false « = X € HT/nuit » step — on the fiche and the public quote.
- **On a platform booking with its brut entered, « Base : X − Y de prestations comprises » names the paid base**, not the tariff (tourist-tax-included-services-deduction rule 17).
- **A platform commission is clamped to its échéance** (platform-per-echeance-commission rule 7): a commission typed on a stay with no acompte no longer gives a −100 € acompte net in finance, and the stored figure is the clamped one.
- **« 0 € » of tourist tax withheld by the platform is shown as 0**, not replaced by the engine's estimate (platform-tourist-tax-out-of-the-commission rule 26).
- **A served-persons count of 0 or less is refused** (400 `INVALID_CARD_PERSONS`) instead of being priced as the whole party (card-option-served-persons rule 19).
- **A reservation created with a baby cot and no ticked option keeps its cot supplement**: the line is now stored at creation, so the next save no longer drops the 5 € per cot (baby-bed-supplement rule 19).
- plugin:tariff-recipes Applying a recipe writes its extra-guest unit and included guests on the property (the Lodge recipe on a property left « par séjour » billed its tiers 0 €); the preview lists them (tariff-recipes rule 62).
- plugin:tariff-recipes A recipe naming a platform the installation does not record applies anyway: that platform's cleaning fee is skipped and named in the preview — the shipped recipes failed on a fresh install (tariff-recipe-platform-cleaning-fee rule 15).
- plugin:tariff-recipes A holiday block that adds no constraint no longer splits its season: the Gîte's 2026 Très haute is one range again (tariff-recipes rule 63).
- plugin:website-booking The Roostview read-only key works again: since the public API was mounted per prefix it was refused (403) on every route, including the catalogue, availability and quote reads it is meant for (specs/roostview-api-key.md rule 6).
- plugin:website-booking Anti-bot proof of work: only the requests an address actually sent raise its next challenge, no longer every challenge it fetched — five page views used to make a phone solve 22 bits (10–20 s). A challenge is judged at the difficulty signed into it, so one issued just before the hourly total crossed 20 is no longer refused « tooEasy » (specs/public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md rule 29).
- plugin:website-booking Past the 10-message cap on a quote the operator already handled, the answer says `messageAdded: false` instead of claiming the message was added (rule 30).
- plugin:website-booking The CGV refusals of a booking request (not configured, not accepted, outdated) answer in the visitor's language (specs/terms-acceptance-record.md rule 30).
- plugin:website-booking Public stays: an option or resource quantity is bounded by the stay (one per guest per hour of the stay; `1e9` was priced and printed), the baby-bed resource can no longer be ordered as a resource line (it bypassed `babyBeds ≤ babies`), and `POST /quote` refuses a party over the property's capacity with `409 OVER_CAPACITY` like the booking request (specs/public-api.md rules 17-19).
- Website request e-mails: the 15-minute summary lists one line per quote with how many requests it gathered (one stay sent 13 times read as 13 lines), and the immediate e-mail writes the stay dates as dd/mm/yyyy like the summary (specs/public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md rule 31).
- WordPress plugin 1.18.0 (unreleased): the booking block fetches its anti-bot challenge once the contact form is in view or touched instead of at every page load, and re-fetches it when seen after it expired; an answer with `messageAdded: false` reads « Demande reçue. » and never starts a payment.
- Reservation fiche: changing only the check-in or check-out time now re-runs the overlap check (fiche and server), so a late departure that blocks the next arrival is refused like at creation; a baby-bed increase re-runs the baby-bed check.
- A stay created with a line routed to the arrival complement now stores that complement; the SAS no longer reads « Rien à percevoir » for it.
- Saving a fiche no longer re-prices a tourist tax already collected (the save pins it like the live quote does).
- The tariff snapshot is refreshed on every save that rewrites the nights, so a notes-only save after an extension or an arrival move no longer moves the price.
- Saving a started stay no longer fails after the property's default caution changed, and the first save no longer writes a phantom « Caution 0 € → 500 € » history row.
- The acompte deadline is cleared when the acompte becomes 0 €, and is never due after the solde (stays booked 31-36 days ahead).
- Setting or clearing a hand-typed acompte now leaves an « Acompte saisi » history row.
- Money/percentage refusals of the reservation endpoints answer in French, with the machine code in `code`.
- A reservation created without times takes the property's default check-in/check-out times.
- The « no linen → no bed counts » invariant reads the lines the engine kept, not the payload.
- A reservation number held by any row (devis included) is refused with the French 400 instead of failing on the unique index.
- A malformed JSON request body answers 400 « Requête invalide : le corps JSON est mal formé. » instead of « Erreur interne du serveur ».
- A reservation posted without a client is refused with a French 400 `CLIENT_REQUIRED` instead of a server error (specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md rule 10).
- The welcome-pack free units a stay was granted now survive every save: removing the option, lowering its quantity or saving the stay on a platform for a while no longer bills it when it comes back (300 → 380 €). The tariff snapshot stores the grant (`grantedFreeUnitsByOption`) apart from what the lines consumed; an option new to the stay gets today's catalogue (tariff-recipes rule 66).
- A save stores the extra-guest tier table it replayed verbatim: a legacy `null` stays `null` (the marker of the Lodge stays sold at ≤ 2 guests before the fix) instead of turning into `[]` (tariff-recipes rule 67).
- A stay with a legacy 0 € acompte and a stored deadline saves again: a past locked fiche is no longer refused with `PAST_RESERVATION_LOCKED`, and an unlocked one writes no phantom « Date acompte … → — » history row (disable-deposit-per-reservation rule 11).
- A devis stores its arrival complement and its conversion to a reservation carries it, instead of opening a new « Rien à percevoir » hole (force-item-to-complement rule 19).
- `scripts/restore-to-pi.sh` no longer dies under macOS bash 3.2 in a UTF-8 locale right after stopping PM2 (`"$STAMP…"` read as a variable named `STAMP…`); a unit test now scans every `*.sh` for a non-ASCII character glued to an unbraced variable.
- `scripts/backup-from-pi.sh` records the release `current` points at instead of the retired `origin/release` branch (the manifest line was garbled); the backup/restore runbook no longer mentions a CI runner or pushing `release`.
- Reception planning cards show the billed quantity of an option or resource (« ×3 », was « ×1 »): the reception view keeps `billedUnits`.
- A reception (or any non-admin) account no longer logs a 403 on `GET /api/settings` on every page: the favicon hook only asks for the settings as an admin.
- « Voir l’option » / « Voir l’autre » instead of « Voir les 1 option » on the reservation page.
- The « Logement », « Heure d'arrivée » and « Heure de départ » selects of the fiche have an accessible name.
- No more React DOM-nesting warning on the Options page at phone width.
- plugin:sas The arrival recap says « Caution non perçue : reportée au départ. » after « Reporter au départ ».
- plugin:sas A non-integer linen quantity (1.5) is refused with 400 `INVALID_QUANTITY` at the preview and the commit, arrival and departure, instead of being rounded and billed.
- plugin:sas A linen element billed in several pieces keeps its count (« Taie d'oreiller ×2 » on the planning card, the SAS reopens on 2).
- Property page: with the acompte switched off, the « Annulation » helper no longer promises to keep an acompte; it says the cancellation follows the CGV (specs/property-deposit-switch.md rule 14).
- **An option moment or a bain-nordique session is dated on a day of the stay** (specs/reservations-backend-mvc.md rule 13, second reading; review of PR #938): the price preview, the reservation and the devis refused nothing but a malformed date, so a breakfast on 1900-01-01 or a session on 30 February was billed and stored. A date off the stay's days, arrival to departure morning, is now refused with « Date hors du séjour. »; a date the line already stores keeps saving.
- **The date window gives the right answer at its edges** (review of PR #938): a payment mark sent back with its stored date, or a platform penalty left on its stored solde date, is no longer refused as « Date trop ancienne »; a compensation received on a refused date shows the French message instead of a code; the mentions preview falls back to today on a date outside the window; the fiche, the closures dialog and the finance period judge the window from the server's day rather than the device's.
- **Console: a licence date lies within five years of today** (specs/control-plane-plans-and-access.md rule 47): the start and end of a new subscription, « Prolonger » and « Forcer actif » refuse a date such as 9999-12-31.
- Recording the solde of an older stay (its acompte recorded before line contributions existed) no longer makes that acompte disappear from the accounting export of its month: the stored-amount split is only written on a stay that already has a contribution; the others keep their usual export.
- Planning: scrolling up right after the page opens could snap the view back under today's header, and the loading skeleton above the timeline made the view jump. The operator's own scroll is now kept when content is added above, and the skeleton no longer moves the cards.
- **« Configurer » lands on its plugin** (specs/plugins-phase-0-foundation.md rule 26): the Intégrations section stays in view below the bars while the later sections load, on a phone and on a computer.
- **Saving a reservation opened from a link stays in the app** (specs/ui-review-2026-10-19-operations.md rule 22): with nothing of the app behind it, « Retour » and the save go to the planning instead of leaving for a blank tab.
- **Keyboard and screen readers**: the quote number of each row of the quotes list is a link Entrée opens; the mini planning's arrows read « Jour précédent » / « Jour suivant »; each settings group of the sidebar is a link with its own « Déplier / Replier » chevron.
- **Settings search by email name** (specs/settings-rationalization.md rule 29): « accès », « préparation », « merci », « rappel de solde » find « Textes des emails » (« J-2 » and « J-7 » are gone since the emails got their names).
- **« + Variable » on a field never clicked** adds the variable at the end, after a space, instead of before the first word (specs/guest-email-texts-made-simple.md rule 46).
- **Phone layout**: the onboarding assistant puts « Suivant » at the bottom, under the thumb, as the SAS does (shared rule, specs/design-system-reference.md §9); a page bar holding a single button gives it to the top bar instead of drawing an empty band (Suivi financier, Calendrier, Devis); Enregistrer / Annuler stay level with the back button; a long logement name wraps in the calendar filter; the settings search placeholder is no longer cut.
- **Finance colours from the theme**: the year-over-year badge, the pace notice and the insight icons use the soft tokens and their text shade (≥ 4.5 : 1).
- Spec citations of three test files point at the right spec (no orphan citation left for the coverage tool).
- Home: the « Aujourd'hui » button of the date navigation brings the date back to today again (it threw an error).

## Removed
- **`GET /api/planning/alerts` and `GET /api/planning/option-cards` removed** (spec `dashboard-planning-scroll.md` rule 10): `GET /api/planning/days` returns the same data per day, in one call.
- **Domaine Solio's site material leaves the product repository** (`specs/plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rule 30). `integrations/wordpress/solio-site/` (the site's mu-plugins), `scripts/configure-aventura-lodge-2026.mjs` and the Solio section of `integrations/wordpress/INSTALL.md` now live, with their history, in the deployment's private repository; the server tests that read only those mu-plugins moved with them, and the ones that also read the public `guestflow-booking` plugin keep their plugin assertions here (the calendar locale check is now `wordpress-plugin-calendar-locale`). A new guard fails if the folder or the script comes back.

## Migration
- **Validation en deux étapes (rules 43–44):** additive columns on `user_two_factor` (`pendingStartedAt`, `pendingSends`, `lastCodeSentAt`, `pendingTokenHash`, `actionCodeHash`, `actionCodeExpiresAt`, `mailsSent`, `mailsSince`, `loginMailsSent`, `loginMailsSince`), added idempotently at boot, no row rewritten. An activation begun before the update has no start and reads as expired: one click on « Recommencer ». Accounts already protected keep their method, secret, backup codes and trusted devices.
- `platforms.relaysGuestEmail`, `reviewWhereFr`, `reviewWhereEn` added; one-shot `platform_guest_contact_v1` ticks the relay switch on existing Airbnb / Booking rows and writes the former review wordings (Gîtes de France, Airbnb, Booking), so existing e-mails are unchanged.
- `user_push_channel_prefs` added; one-shot `push_channel_prefs_v1` carries the « Souscriptions Neat » choices over from `user_push_prefs.neat`.
- **Migration** — `reservations.complementCollectedTax` (nullable) and three triggers record the tourist tax as stored the moment the arrival complement is collected, so the trop-perçu of a tax that falls afterwards is refunded on the tax poste. Added idempotently at boot; rows collected before this version stay NULL and read the history as before. No existing data is rewritten.
- **Payment links and property documents: additive columns, one move at boot.** `payment_links` keeps `qontoPaymentLinkId` / `qontoPaymentId` next to `providerLinkId` / `providerPaymentId` (added and copied from the old names on upgrade, kept equal by triggers); nothing is lost. Property documents already uploaded are moved from `uploads/` to `private-documents/` (beside it) and their `documents.filePath` rewritten to `private/<name>`; a missing file is logged and its row left alone. This move is not undone by a rollback (self-update rule 39).
- `dates_unavailable_notice_anchor_v1` moves the `dates_unavailable_notice` email template from the `start` anchor to `datesTaken` (one-shot, writes only when the value differs; copy, offset and `enabled` untouched).
- **`reservation_refunds.afterCancellation`** (INTEGER, nullable) — added by an idempotent `ALTER TABLE` at boot. A new
  refund records 1 when its stay was already cancelled, 0 otherwise; existing rows stay NULL and keep reading their
  `createdAt` against the stay's `cancelledAt`. No backfill, no existing row rewritten.
- `reservations.complementPaidSplit` (TEXT NULL), `reservations.removedLineContribs` (TEXT NULL) and the trigger `reservations_clear_complement_paid_split`: additive, no backfill, NULL reads as before. A complement collected earlier reads live until it is re-collected. A rollback ignores both columns and reads entries live again (`specs/self-update-and-releases.md` rule 39, reading of 2026-11-05). `accounting-export` 1.0.25 keeps its floors and reads them behind a column guard.
- **Three nullable `reservations` columns and one trigger, additive** (spec `platform-added-guests-tourist-tax.md` §5). `platformPaidOccupants` is backfilled once: current occupants on every non-direct stay with a brut, except a stay that already moved under v3.21.0, which keeps `platformPaidGuests`. `touristTaxAddedGuestsAdults` is backfilled where an added tax is stored. `touristTaxDeclaredWithAddedAmount` stays NULL on existing rows, which keep the date test. Trigger `reservations_clear_added_guests_declared` clears the added row's tick when the complement is unticked. No stored amount moves; a rollback to the floor (v3.18.0) runs unchanged.
- **Open devis re-stored as one payment** (specs `deposit-blocks-the-dates.md` rule 20): at every boot, and after every save of a logement for its devis, an open devis whose solde falls on or before its validity date but whose stored row still holds an acompte is re-written as the single payment (acompte 0, the pre-arrival total due on the validity date, one history entry), and its open payment links billing the old amounts are withdrawn (`amount-changed`). Converted devis are never touched. Idempotent; on the production copy of 2026-11-09 no row qualifies.
- plugin:hourly-resources `resource_bookings_v3` adds `paidVatRate` (the VAT rate frozen at payment, NULL = not subject) and `invoiceMarkPending`; bookings already paid get the rate in force at install, invoiced ones their invoice's rate. Nothing else moves (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rule 35).
- plugin:hourly-resources Migration `resource_bookings_v4`: `resource_bookings.qontoClientIdentity TEXT` (nullable). Rows invoiced before keep their Qonto client id but carry no identity, so their Qonto client is not reused: the next invoice of that guest creates one with what his record holds (spec `resource-booking-pricing-and-invoicing.md` rule 50). No data rewritten.
- **`cancellation_notice_kept_sum_v1`** (one-shot): in the stored `cancellation_notice` template, the exact seeded sentence « l'acompte de {{retainedDepositAmount}} déjà versé reste acquis à titre d'indemnité » (and its English twin) becomes « la somme de {{retainedDepositAmount}} déjà versée reste acquise à titre d'indemnité »; a body the operator reworded is left as it is. No schema change.
- **`cancellation_notice_neutral_opening_v1`** (one-shot, rule 63): rewrites only the exact seeded opening sentence of the stored `cancellation_notice` body (French and English) to « Nous avons le regret de vous informer que votre séjour … est annulé. » / « We are sorry to inform you that your stay at … has been cancelled. ». A body the operator reworded, the send mode and the enabled flag are left as they are. Same tokens, so an older release renders it unchanged.
- `plugins.off_at` (nullable, idempotent `ADD COLUMN`) stamps a system switch-off; no backfill.
- Payment e-mails: the sentence « Ce lien est valable jusqu'au … » of the default request templates and the acompte reminder moves into a block of its own (`{{#if hasPaymentLinkValidUntil}}`). After a rollback, the earlier release drops the line instead of writing « valable jusqu'au . ». Migration `payment_link_valid_until_sentence_v2` changes only the templates nobody edited, recognised by SHA-256. An edited template keeps its text and is named in the boot log.
- **`calendar_parity_superseded_v1`** — three nullable columns on `reservations` (`supersededAt`, `supersededByReservationId`, `supersededReason`) and a one-shot, idempotent data migration run at boot before any read: only the true duplicates the removed display filter hid are set aside (1 stay in production, `0 €`, no payment), each with a `reservation_history` entry; the other hidden stays simply become visible in their logement calendar. The boot log lists the ids. Nothing is deleted.
- **`platforms.forbidsGuestChores`** (spec `airbnb-no-checkout-chores.md` rules 8–9): new column, default
  0. The one-shot `chore_free_channels_v1` ticks the existing platforms whose name starts with « Airbnb »
  (`AirbnbTente` included), once; a new database ticks none. No data changed otherwise.
- A database trigger `devis_number_retire_on_delete` records the number of every deleted devis in `devis_number_retired` (specs/public-request-abuse-and-grouping.md rule 35). At the first boot, open devis whose stored schedule differs from their read are re-written to it, and open devis with no validity date are stamped with the date they read (specs/deposit-blocks-the-dates.md rule 25; one history entry per devis, logged as `devis re-stored to their read`).
- **Installment plans — schema** (spec `direct-installments.md` §5). New table `reservation_installments`; three additive aggregates on `reservations` (`installmentsPaidAmount`, `installmentsUnpaidAmount`, `installmentsTarget`, 0 for every existing row); `email_log.occurrenceKey` (`''` for every existing row). Two tables are rebuilt on boot, inside one transaction, rows copied one-to-one and ids kept: `payment_links` (its `type` CHECK accepts `installment`, new nullable `installmentId`) and `email_manual_queue` (primary key `(templateId, reservationId, occurrenceKey)`). Idempotent; no existing reservation has a plan, so no existing amount or date changes. The seed adds the manual template `installment_request`.
- `property_arrival_facts` and `stay_text_flags` tables, and `properties.nameArticleConfirmed` (existing properties marked confirmed). No backfill; Solio's emails render unchanged.
- **`guest-app` tables** (spec `guest-app.md` §5). Installing the plugin creates its own tables (`guest_app_*` — the stock counts `guest_app_stock_counts` among them —, `guest_sessions`, `guest_stay_links`, `guest_booklet_*`, `guest_media`, `guest_orders`). Additive: no core table changes (the one core column, `payment_links.basketJson`, has its own note), and nothing is created while the plugin is not installed.
- `users.isSupportAccount` (default 0) marks the « Support GuestFlow » account, set on the existing row by its address `support@guestflow.invalid`. Additive; no other row changes (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md rule 21).
- Control-plane database: new tables `instances`, `fleet_updates`, `fleet_update_items`, `backup_checks`, `mail_counters`, `host_redirects`, `host_alerts`; `provisioning_steps` rebuilt once to accept the `running` status (rows kept). The C-phase manual step rows (`instance`) are no longer shown. The instance database is unchanged.
- Account security tables (`password_reset_tokens`, `user_two_factor`, `user_backup_codes`, `user_security_events`, `support_access`, `support_access_links`, `support_access_log`), `users.twoFactorSnoozedUntil`, and the inactive « Support GuestFlow » account (`support@guestflow.invalid`, never listed, never counted). Additive; no existing row changes (specs/hosting-h2-account-security.md §5).
- **Shared iCal placeholder clients split at boot** (specs/ical-import-hardening.md rule 1): a client that only nameless iCal stays use, with no e-mail, phone, address or note, is kept by its first stay; every other stay gets its own copy, logged in its history (« Client »). Placeholders are renamed « Ical <platform> » (« Booked Ical », the Gîtes de France one, included). Clients carrying a guest's coordinates are never touched — `node server/scripts/diagnose-shared-clients.js <guestflow.db>` lists them for a manual repair. Idempotent, nothing deleted.
- `email_templates.anchor` of the `installment_request` row goes from `start` to `installmentDueDate` (migration `installment_template_anchor_v1`, writes only when the value differs). Nothing else on the row changes.
- **Laundry schedule (`linen` 1.2.0)**: new plugin table `laundry_schedule_versions`, seeded once from the former `laundryWeekday` as `{ [weekday], 1 day }` effective from the beginning of time — every regular trip, pick-up and card of an existing install is unchanged, and so is the stock projection except for one correction: a past manual line (extra linen or linen washed at home) now stays counted for the week after it instead of dropping out of the initial state. `laundryWeekday` stays stored (frozen) for a rollback to 1.1.x; the generic settings write now refuses it.
- Intake plugins `airbnb_intakes` and `gdf_intakes` gain `senderVerified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` (idempotent): rows already applied are set to `1`, rows still open stay `0` and are proposed with the warning at the next pass.
- **A database upgraded from v3.10.0 or later** keeps the state of its intake plugins (`gites-de-france-intake`, `booking-intake`, and `airbnb-intake` from v3.16.0); the phase 0 seed (`plugins_builtin_seed_v1`) then installs the other plugins active, as on any existing database, and every row becomes `bundled`. The ledger entries `plugins_backport_seed_v1`/`_v2`/`_v3` stay in `migrations`, unread.
- **The phase 0 seed installs the twelve former core features only** (spec `plugins-phase-0-foundation.md` rule 10, 2026-10-18): on an existing database that first meets the plugin engine, « Saisie Gîtes de France » and « Saisie Booking » are no longer installed active — they wait, not installed, on the Plugins page.
- **Split payments** (spec `mixed-method-payments.md` §5): new tables `payment_settlements` and `payment_tenders`, and four `reservations` columns (`splitOffBooksAmount`, `splitWaivedAmount`, `splitWaivedCashAmount`, `splitShortfallDue`, default 0). Additive only: existing payments are unchanged.
- **The default payment e-mails gain their validity sentence, on untouched templates only** (`payment_link_valid_until_sentence_v1`, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 36). At the first boot, the French body and the English body of `deposit_request`, `balance_request`, `full_request`, `installment_request` and `deposit_reminder` each get the sentence « Ce lien est valable jusqu'au {{paymentLinkValidUntil}}. » / « This link is valid until {{paymentLinkValidUntil}}. » after the link only when they still hold, to the character, one of the default texts GuestFlow shipped for that template (any version, so an install seeded with an older default is recognised too); nothing else in the text changes. A side the operator edited keeps its text exactly and is named in the boot line `[migration:link-valid-until] …; edited, left as they are: …`; the variable is available to add by hand. No sending mode changes (every template stays Manual); nothing is deleted.
- **Links already on taken dates are withdrawn once, without telling the guest** (`dates_taken_links_withdrawn_v1`, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 61). At the first boot, every open payment link of an unpaid devis or reservation whose nights a stay holding its dates already covers is withdrawn (`cancelReason = 'dates-taken'`, the deactivation marker when it has a provider id), one `[migration:dates-taken-links]` line per link and a total; no network call at boot, the first poll pass reads each payment then deactivates the link (booked in date conflict if it was paid meanwhile). New table `dates_taken_notices` (one row per stay); each stay concerned gets a row `not-sent` / `upgrade`, which the coming « Dates prises » dashboard card shows as « client non prévenu » and which never triggers a guest e-mail. Nothing is deleted; paid rows are untouched.
- **No guest notice for a withdrawal older than this version** (`dates_taken_notice_backlog_v1`, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 64). At the first boot, every stay already holding a link withdrawn for taken dates without a `dates_taken_notices` row gets one, `not-sent` / `upgrade`: it shows on the « Dates prises » card as « client non prévenu » and never triggers a guest e-mail. The default template `dates_unavailable_notice` is seeded (stored Manual). Nothing is deleted. `payment_links` gains `takenByReservationId`, `unpaidReadAt`, `noticeJudgedAt`, `withdrawnAt` and `noticeClosedReason` (nullable; links withdrawn before keep them empty and are never mailed), and `dates_taken_notices` is rebuilt once to allow the `sending` status, rows and ids kept.
- **Open orphan payment links withdrawn once** (`orphan_payment_links_withdrawn_v1`, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 5). At the first boot, every `open` payment link whose stay row is missing becomes `cancelled` with `cancelReason = 'orphan'` and, when it has a provider id, the deactivation marker; one `[migration:orphan-links]` line is logged per link. No network call at boot: the first poll pass reads each link's payment, then deactivates it (`[payments] orphan link #… deactivated at …`) or sends the orphan alert if it was paid meanwhile. Paid, cancelled and expired rows are untouched; nothing is deleted.
- **Links already open get 36 hours from the install** (`payment_link_validity_backfill_v1`, spec `payment-link-lifecycle.md` rule 34). At the first boot, every `open` payment link — Qonto's `0001-01-01…` sentinel and complement links included — gets `expiresAt = install + 36 h`, one `[migration:link-validity]` line per link; they then lapse like any link, so operators re-send the requests still wanted. Two nullable columns are added to `payment_links`: `lastLookAt` and `deactivationAlertedAt` (additive: the previous release ignores them). Nothing is deleted; paid, cancelled and expired rows are untouched.
- **`payment_links.basketJson`** (spec `guest-app.md` rule 35, `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md` rule 47). A nullable column naming the basket a `complement` link bills (`{ source, ref }`, a guest order). Additive: every existing link keeps NULL and behaves as before.
- **New `plugins` table** (spec `plugins-phase-0-foundation.md` §5). Migration `plugins_builtin_seed_v1` installs the twelve built-in plugins **active** on every existing database (one with a property or a reservation): nothing changes on screen after the update. A brand-new database starts with none. No existing row is touched.
- **Plugin settings table** (spec `plugins-phase-1-sdk.md` §5): new table `plugin_settings (plugin_id, key, value, updated_at)`. The one-shot migration `plugin_settings_from_app_settings_v1` copies the Météo-France key and the eight Google Agenda fields there, byte for byte (secrets stay encrypted with the same key). The old `app_settings` columns stay in place, unread, so a rollback to v3.5 finds its data; they are dropped in a later release. Google stays connected and the weather key stays set.
- **Plugin tables created on install**: the tables of the five moved plugins (`weather_vigilance_cache`, `school_holidays`, `school_holidays_sync_state`, `tariff_recipe_runs`, `gate_key_results`, `gate_connector_state`) leave the core baseline. An existing database keeps them untouched (the plugins' migrations find them and only record themselves as `plugin:<id>:<name>` in the `migrations` ledger); a new database gets them when the plugin is installed. The 15 hard-coded school-holiday periods are no longer seeded: installing the plugin syncs from education.gouv. The Sowel connector keys are only created once `gate-access` is installed.
- Linge (8), Export comptable (4) and Site WordPress (2) copy their settings from `app_settings` into `plugin_settings` once, through a migration of their own. The old columns stay in place, unread, for one release. The three laundry tables leave the baseline of a new database and appear when Linge is installed.
- `payment_links`: `qontoPaymentLinkId` → `providerLinkId`, `qontoPaymentId` → `providerPaymentId`, new `provider` (`'qonto'` for every existing row) and `remoteCancelPendingAt`. Existing open links stay findable by the poll and the webhook.
- The 19 `qonto*` columns of `app_settings` are copied once into `plugin_settings` (`online-payment`), secrets as their encrypted values; the old columns stay, unread, and are emptied by « Effacer aussi ses données ». `publicSiteOrigin` stays in `app_settings`.
- The 13 `neat*` columns of `app_settings` are copied once into `plugin_settings` (`neat`), the client secret as its encrypted value; the old columns stay, unread, and are emptied by « Effacer aussi ses données ».
- `neat_subscriptions` and `neat_price_cache` are now created by the plugin (`tables_v1`); an existing database keeps every row. A database where Neat was never installed has neither table.
- The fiche payload's `neat` block moves to `pluginBlocks.neat`.
- **`reservation_custom_options.sasLineKey`** (TEXT, NULL) added by the core. The `hourly-resources` plugin's first boot runs `tables_v1` (`resource_bookings`, `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`: every row kept; the core no longer creates the table) and `tag_evening_supplements_v1`, which tags each evening supplement the arrival SAS wrote before (a SAS custom line named « <resource> — supplément soirée » on a stay carrying that `per_hour` resource) with `hourly-resources:evening:<resourceId>`. Nothing visible changes for Solio.
- **`plugins` table** (spec `plugins-phase-4-registry.md` §5): `+ manifest`, `+ pinned_sha256`,
  `+ previous_version`, `+ previous_source`, `+ previous_sha256`, `+ rollback_until`; the existing
  `builtin` rows become `bundled` and take the version of the copy the release ships. Nothing else
  changes: every plugin stays installed and active as it was, with its data.
- **`productisation_v1`** (spec `plugins-phase-p-productisation.md` rules 24–25), once, on a database that already holds properties or reservations: today's wording of the guest emails becomes data (stay texts, option phrases, the confirmation order, the nordic-bath sentence), Lodgify is marked as a direct sale, the push subject and the SAS extinguisher check are kept. Additive only; Solio's emails render byte for byte as before (golden test, and 0 difference over 672 renderings of a production copy). New columns: `platforms.countsAsDirect`, `resources.emailBookedText(En)`, `app_settings.onboardingCompletedAt`, `vapidSubject`, `bookedConfirmationOrder`; new tables `stay_texts`, `email_mentions`, `email_mention_options`.
- `reservations` gains `publicUpdatedAfterSendAt` and `operatorSeenAt` (NULL on existing rows); new core tables `devis_number_retired`, `notification_digest_windows`, `notification_digest_items`; the website-booking plugin adds `public_request_challenges_used`, `public_request_log`, `public_request_stays`. No data is rewritten.
- **Migration.** Plugin migration `plugin:online-payment:reconciliation_v2` adds `bank_reconciliation_lines.auto` (default 0) and `bank_reconciliation_lines.groupId` (NULL); idempotent, existing lines are unchanged and count as confirmed by the operator.
- **Migration.** Core: table `reservation_platform_penalties` and column `reservations.platformPenaltyAmount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` (idempotent, in `database.js`); every existing reservation reads 0, so no figure changes until a penalty is declared. Past penalties already hidden in a commission are not detected.
- `resource_bookings` gains offered time, payment lines and date, arrival, billing address and Qonto invoice columns (plugin migration `resource_bookings_v2`). A booking already ticked « Payé » keeps it, dated from its last change, with no method, and stays out of the accounting export. The Qonto connection must be reconnected once (Réglages › Intégrations) to grant the invoicing scopes.
- `property_resource_prices.price` accepts NULL (`resource_property_price_nullable_v1`, `specs/options-resources-ux.md` rule 28): a resource's property line with only a free first hour now inherits the base price instead of storing a copy of it. The table is rebuilt at boot with every row and value kept as stored; existing rows are not reinterpreted (a stored `0 € + 1 h offerte` line stays « free there »).
- The existing bath lines (`30 €` with a free first hour) stay frozen copies of the base price they were saved with until the operator blanks their price field; only then do they follow the base price.
- New columns on `properties`: `pendingFacts` (empty on every existing property, so no email changes), `cleaningIncluded`, `towelsProvided` (NULL: the options decide, as before), `poolSeasonStart` / `poolSeasonEnd` (copied once from `app_settings`, whose columns stay, unread); `app_settings.onboardingPropertyId`. One-shot `cgv_link_condition_v1`: a stored template printing `{{cgvUrl}}` outside `{{#if hasCgvUrl}}` gets that paragraph wrapped in the condition (identical while the link exists). No data is lost. `properties.bedLinenProvided` (NULL on every existing property: the options decide, as before); `pendingFacts` may also hold `linen` on a property the start assistant creates.
- plugin:linen `laundry_trip_manual_additions.bathMats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` (plugin migration `manual_additions_bath_mats_v1`); existing manual lines read 0 bath mats. No data rewritten.
- `payment_links.cancelReason TEXT` (idempotent `ALTER TABLE` in `utils/paymentLinksProviderMigration.js`): `'settled'` on the links retired because their bucket was paid another way; existing rows read NULL. No data rewritten.
- **Plugin SDK 2.0.0** (specs/sas-arrival-total-server-side.md rules 13-14bis): the client step contract `sas.arrival.steps[].recapLines` is removed — a step bills through its server `ctx.sasCommit().complementItems`, which the arrival preview now calls on every gesture and which must therefore be pure. Every plugin of this repository moves to `sdk: "^2.0.0"` in this release and installs with it. **A plugin installed from outside this repository and built against SDK 1 is refused by this version until its author republishes it against `^2.0.0`**; none is known today.
- **Social links:** the Instagram address is copied once into the new list (`app_settings.socialLinks`, migration `social_links_from_instagram_v1`); `instagramUrl` is kept in sync so installing the previous release renders the same e-mail. No payment delay and no pool season is rewritten.
- `reservation_installments.paidSplit` (TEXT, nullable) is added on boot (idempotent `ALTER TABLE` in `utils/installmentsMigration.js`): the accounting split of an installment, stored when it is collected. No backfill: installments collected before the column keep the live split.
- `reservation_history` is rebuilt once without its foreign key on `reservations` (`reservation_history_outlives_reservation_v1`): every row, id and the id counter are kept; deleting a stay now leaves its history in place.
- One-shot `property_own_article_v1`: every property whose name starts with its own article (« La … », « Le … », « Les … », « L'… ») and whose stored article differs or was never confirmed takes that article, confirmed. Nothing else is touched.
- New table `breakfast_fallback_done (reservationId, date)` — the « préparé » flag of a breakfast card that has no chosen mornings. Created empty and idempotently at boot; no existing row is touched.
- plugin:website-booking Migration `public_request_tables_v3` drops the `public_request_issued` table (issued anti-bot challenges, kept 24 h): challenges handed out no longer count towards the difficulty. Nothing else is touched.
- One-shot boot repair `created_complement_amount_repaired_v1`: restores the arrival complement of reservations created with a line routed to it and never saved since (unpaid, not adjusted, SAS not done, and only when `finalPrice − acompte − solde` equals the routed lines). Each repaired stay is listed in the boot log and gets a history entry.
- The complement repair `created_complement_amount_repaired_v1` now proves the gap as `finalPrice + tourist tax − acompte − solde` (it matched no stay with a tourist tax) and only touches stays not yet ended (force-item-to-complement rule 19).
- One-shot boot cleanup `zero_deposit_deadline_cleared_v1`: clears `depositDueDate` on reservations whose acompte is 0 € and on which nothing was ever paid; the count is logged (disable-deposit-per-reservation rule 11).
- `reservation_custom_options.quantity` (INTEGER, default 1) is added at boot. One-shot `sas_custom_line_quantity_v1`: an existing arrival-SAS custom line whose label is a priced linen item and whose amount is an exact multiple (> 1) of that price gets that count; every other row keeps 1. No amount changes.
- Two columns added to `app_settings` (`vatFranchiseThreshold`, `vatFranchiseThresholdSetAt`), empty for every existing installation: no data is modified.
- New `vat_periods` table (history of the VAT regime) and `reservations.vatPeriodId` column, created at startup: period 1 takes over the current VAT rate (`app_settings.vatRate`, kept as a mirror), every existing reservation is attached to it, and a trigger attaches each new reservation. Nothing priced or collected is modified; the operation is idempotent.
- **Vigilance Météo-France now requires Arrivée et départ guidés** (`weather-alerts` 1.3.0, spec `plugin-dependencies.md` rule 20). A space that runs the weather plugin without the SAS plugin sees it « Nécessite Arrivée et départ guidés » (installed, not loaded) after the update; its settings and data are kept. Installing the SAS plugin from the Plugins page registers the weather plugin again at once, without a restart. No schema change.
